tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.



yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷi nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmi kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunili niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷi ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkum tolliyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēviyumilli. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉi aṭiyalām.
- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nāki - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulami mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam uṭiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟinta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭimiyē eṉṉi ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toki curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷi varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.
vaṇṇārappēṭṭi	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷi
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟimaliya māṇpu
nuḻivuri
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
munti aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum takavārnta perumi peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yurikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟi moḻikaḷukkum tanti moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻpparappāḷarāyp potunilik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum perumi peṟṟa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭi maliyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpati nili nāṭṭiya niṟimali, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuttāl orupāṭi eṉṟu aṟiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalimali pēḻiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟimi pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpati,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunili tirital ceyyuṭ killi 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇi ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumiyum kuṟumiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷi ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟimali aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takiniṉṟa taṇṭamiḻi - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟimali yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivi
 pintamiḻ kākkum paṭi
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭiyum kalit tiṟap paṭippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟi āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟi āṟṟali 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti
paṉimali mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amintavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimali - imayam; nīla āṟu - nil; amitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷi, nūlaka iyakkat tanti araṅka nātarum, innāḷ civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakimippaṭutti vittami perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avi,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭuri 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nāki aṭikaḷi - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammiyār! ivviṇiyar koṭiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉi ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷatti vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvari payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum niliyil, arumit tantiyār iyaṟki eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyit toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmi tamiḻukku illi. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppilli eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟiyiyum, nākiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟiyiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟiyāl vāṭum aṉṉikkuc cumiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉi māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpatiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyi niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, toki nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi, iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvi mukappil, ematu patiṉintām āṇṭil muṟippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyatiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uriyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nāki intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc civa cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉi maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉipeyaril maṟuppuri eḻutiṉār. ataṉikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉi virivilē ceṉṉikku varuvippōm nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉi vāḻvu amiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉi māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumi kaṇṭu, pērāciriyarik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uriyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattiyum mūtaṟiviyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanili ovvāmiyāl nākikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉi: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaric ceṉṉikku aḻittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpiki eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉi kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra ak kallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟit talivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kaliñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkali māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappāli (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci uri eḻutiṉār.
avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟi uri, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭamoḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭatti viruppappāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikki kuṟiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyi muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. viyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉi kacaṭukaḷi nīkkiyatu? viyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟic ceyta arumi eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kāriki āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārit taḻuvutal uṇmi tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapuratti vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟut tiya avviraṇṭu koḷkikaḷiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷi oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. inniliyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkikaḷiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉiyār ciṉṉammiyār, tuṇiviyār cavuntiram ammi yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvarik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpiki, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. inniliyilēyē 27.08.1911il civat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭi (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷip paṭi. muṉṉēyē avari aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvi
nām mīḷpārvi oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akaviyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvuri vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉi; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉi; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉi - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpiki yār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭami eṉpatu, akattē amintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷi viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa niliyām. av veḷippaṭṭa niliyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravilli! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka niliyam, potunilik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amippu uruvākum niliyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avi iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa niliyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭit tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭit tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭit tūṇṭal valimi mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyi veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takimi ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉi uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmi,
tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟi yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantip pēṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟoki, arumaṇik kuviyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟ koṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumit tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 vari mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavi. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaviyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉitti yum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vitta kuṟuvaṭṭu kaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉami, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷi mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annili yil,
ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar uliviṉṟit
tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyi vāymi moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amintu tāṅkal, iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟi muṉvittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappuk kām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icik kalikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākaratti veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉi vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉip pakutikaḷiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, urivēntar auvi cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷiyum muḻumi muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - toki - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari veḷivanta- tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumi iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇiyiyum makkaḷiyum uṟaviyum aṉpiyum naṇpiyum oruṅkē iṇittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇippiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟimaliya māṇpu
maṟimaliyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumi! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭit tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭi! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakimi! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymi viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar niliyam,						iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							         irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturi - 6
625006.

malikka vikkum maṟimaliyam!
patippuri
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉi vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmi uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇari aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattit tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭi eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭi niṉivu kūrum vakiyil, taṉit tamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum `maṟimaliyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyi eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭippukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimi yūṭṭiṉar.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivuk kaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻi 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavi, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavi, peyar terintum pārvikkuk kiṭṭātavi - eṉap palavakiyāyk kiṭittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭippukaḷ! avaṟṟi `maṟimaliyam eṉum talippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavaliyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt tici nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputiyal palar kikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟimali yaṭikaḷārit tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyatti etirppatilum camaṟkirutatti vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēm pāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyit tamiḻar neñcaṅ kaḷil arumpa vittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavittatu taṉit tamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukiyiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kaliñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvi avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta viruti civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malimaliyaṭikaḷ).
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyarik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟimiku āṅkilam maṟivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulami mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
 maṟimali yaṭikaḷ maṟiyāt tiruppeyar
 vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉi maṟimali yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamilli.
ceṉṉiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talimuṟikkum varuṅkālat talimuṟikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉi (cātaṉi)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avi kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpati aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalatti otukki vittu, tamiḻ nalattik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalip piṉṉiṇippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻikkum uṇarvi iḷamiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmi eḻavittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avari eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavi! avaṟṟil orupakutiyi yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vi. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvatti mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉivar kikaḷiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumi - civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟi aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟi aṉivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭikkum paṇiyic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumiyik karutātu uḻikkum maṉavuratti muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷi iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!

`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amitta ittaku amippukaḷukku mūla viciyākat tikaḻum perumiyip peṟuvōr maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavi. avi toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉittup paṭippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavariciyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟimaliyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.

accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamippu:  
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā						


nūluri
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya puṉikati nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu innūl. aṭikaḷār iyaṟkit tōyvum, uvamic ceṟivum katit tiruppaṅkaḷum oṉṟi oṉṟu veṉṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ēṟattāḻa 300 pakkaṅkaḷiyuṭiya innūl 1911 il kaḻaka veḷiyīṭāka vantuḷatu.
āṅkilak kati muṟi vaḻi nūlip paṭittiruntālum nūliṉ amippu muḻuvatum taṉit tamiḻnāṭṭu amippēyām eṉṟu patippuri pakarkiṟatu. itaṉik kaṟpār taṉittamiḻ māṇpum pintamiḻp pulamiyum, paṇṭiya vaḻakkum, aṟivum, āṟṟalum, naṉmi vēṭṭalum, piṟarkkutavalum, āṭcit tiṟaṉum, cūḻcci vaṉmiyum, putiya katiyamikkum poṟpum peṟuvār eṉpatum patippuri.
vaḻi naṭantu pōkum iḷiñaṉ eṉat toṭaṅki (mutal atikāram) kumuta valliyum nīlalōcaṉaṉum eṉa niṟivu ceykiṟār (patiṉāṟām atikāram).
ovvōr iṭattu vaṇṇaṉiyum, ovvōr āḷvaṇṇaṉiyum pakkam pakkamāka nīḷkiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. 625 ām āṇṭu iḷavēṉil kālattil eṉa varalāṟṟupputiṉap pōkkil mēl malit toṭar vaḻiyil nīlamalic celavāy nērkiṉṟatu. cellum iḷiñaṉ cantiraṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ neṭuntolivil iruntu vantu ōr iṭukku vaḻiyil ceṉṟu kaḷvar pācaṟi oṉṟi aṭikiṟāṉ. kaḷvar talivaṉiyum kāṇkiṟāṉ. 
ellām cevviyāka nikaḻntaṉa eṉa iḷiñaṉ, kaḷvar talivaṉiṭam kūṟa avaṉ, itaṉāl nākanāṭṭu araciyiṭattum nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉṉum putta iḷiñaṉiṭattum karutiya vaṇṇam muṭittu vantāy eṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
mēṟkari eṉṉum taṉ nakaril iruntu payaṇam pōvataṟku mikavum icinta vaḻiyiṉi nīlalōcaṉaṉukkuk kāṭṭiyatu pōlavē, nākanāṭṭil iruntu varum aḻakiya kumutavallikkum kāṭṭiyirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟu av viḷiñaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
kaḷvar talivaṉ peyar nallāṉ; vanta iḷiñaṉ nīlakiriyāṉ. kaḷvar talivaṉ āṟu kaḷvarkaḷi aḻittu nīlalōcaṉaṉ varum vaḻiyi nōkki yirukka viṭuttāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉōṭu tuṇivar iruvar varuvatum colli viṭuttiruntāṉ. avarkaḷic ciṟiyākap piṭittu vara vēṇṭum eṉpatu avaṉ āṇi.
kutiriyil muṉṉē vantavaṉ nīla lōcaṉaṉ; kuṭaku nāṭṭāṉ; maṟṟi iruvarum viyākkira vīraṉ, kēcari vīraṉ eṉpār.
ciṟi piṭikkum muyaṟci tōṟṟuppōy mūvar piṇamāka, mūvar tappi ōṭiṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutal mūvarum vaḻiyit toṭarntaṉar. ōriṭattil vaḻi mūṉṟākap pirintatu. aṭuttirunta kuṭiciyil kēṭka avaṉ nīlakiri cellum vaḻiyik kāṭṭiṉāṉ. appaṭik kāṭṭiyavaṉ kaḷvaṉ iruḷaṉē, akkuṭici vāṇaṉ uṭiyi vāṅki uṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉaṉām.
avvaḻi kuṟuki neṭitu ceṉṟu oru paranta veḷiyi aṭintu niṉṟuviṭṭatu. oru kuṭici iruntatu. paḷiṅkupōl nīrōṭi iruntatu. ataṉpāl paḷiṅkaṉṉa naṅki orutti niṉṟāḷ. nīla lōcaṉaṉ avaḷ aḻakil mayaṅkiṉāṉ. nīlakiri cellum vaḻi taṉakkut teriyum eṉṟum tāṉum aṅkē cella iruppatākavum kūṟiṉāḷ. avaḷ tōḻiyar iruvarum uṭaṉiruntaṉar. avaḷ peyar mīṉāmpāḷ eṉṟāḷ. avaḷ taṅkum iṭam teḷinīr vēli eṉṟāḷ. nīlalōcaṉaṉum mīṉāmpāḷum muṉṉē cella maṟṟi iruvar, iruvarum piṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. muṉṉē oru kōpuram vantatu. aṅkē taṅkiṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mīṉāmpāḷuṭaṉ taṉiyaṟiyil taṅkiṉāṉ. maṟṟiyōr nālvarum oruṅkē ōraṟiyil taṅkiṉar. mīṉāmpāḷ mayakka valiyil nīlalōcaṉaṉ vīḻntu neṭunēram uṟaṅkik kēcari vīraṉāl, eḻuppappaṭṭu mayakku nīṅkiṉāṉ. nallāṉ cūḻcciyāl ciṟi vikkappaṭa irunta nīlalōcaṉaṉ kēcari vīraṉ tiṟattāl viḻippuṟṟu nallāṉ paṭi varumuṉ tappi veḷiyēṟiṉar. avvāṟē nākanāṭṭaraci kumuta valliyum varak kūṟiya vaḻiyi māṟṟik koṇṭatāl ciṟippaṭutta muṭiyavilli. kaḷvar talivaṉ nallāṉ ellām tōlvi eṉa varunti vēṟu cūḻcciyil iṟaṅkiṉāṉ.
nallāṉ mātavaṉ eṉṉum ēvalaṉ uṭiyi māṟṟik koṇṭu kumutavalli ceṉṟa vaḻi nōkki virintu ceṉṟāṉ. oru taṅkal viṭutiyil niṉṟa kutirikaḷik kaṇṭu mātavaṉ kūṟiya aṭiyāḷam teḷintu, nallāṉum ōr aṟiyil taṅki, kumutavalli taṅkiyirukkum aṟiyik kaṇṭu koṇṭu ayarnta pōtil avvaṟikkuc ceṉṟāṉ. ataṉik kumutavalli aṟintum ayarntāḷ pōlak kāṭṭiyirukka avaḷ viralil irunta mōtirattik kaḻaṟṟi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉāṉ. vēṟu aṇimaṇikaḷi eṭuttilaṉ. viḻittuk kūṟiṉāl, avaṉ katti patam pārkkum eṉpatāl kumutavalli amintiruntāḷām. tōḻiyariṭam mōtiram iḻantālum nām nīlamali ceṉṟu evariṭam ataṉic cērkka vēṇṭi vantōmō, avarik kaṇṭu uṇmiyic colvōm eṉa mēlum pātukāppuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭaṉar.
taṅkal viṭutiyār takka vīrarkaḷōṭu pōkac colli āḷkaḷuṭaṉ viṭuttār. avarkaḷ cellum vaḻiyaḷavum  ceṉṟu mīḷa mūvar maṭṭum toṭarntaṉar. kāṭṭuk kuṟuvaḻiyūṭē puliyoṉṟu vantu iraṇṭu vīrarkaḷi vīḻttiyatu. ammūvarum nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutalāya mūvar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ kumuta valliyiṉ parivip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu avarkaḷukku utavuvatākak kūṟic colli uṭaṉ ceṉṟāṉ. cellum vaḻiyil mutiyar iruvar taḷarntu kiyēnta avarkaḷukkuk kācu tantāḷ kumutavalli. uṭaṉvanta nīlalōcaṉaṉum taṉ kācu tara muyala, piyil iruntu kācuṭaṉ kumutavalli mōtiramum vīḻakkaṇṭāḷ. ataṉi veḷikkāṭṭātavaḷāyt taṅkal viṭuti oṉṟi aṭintāḷ. aṅkē kumutavalliyum tōḻiyarum taṅkuvataṟku ōriṭam tīrmāṉamāyiṟṟu. nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutalōr vēṟiṭattut taṅkik kāliyil puṟappaṭat tīrmāṉittaṉar.
kumutavalli taṅkum aṟikkup pōkum pōtē tōḻiyari virintaḻittuk kutiri mēl ēṟac colli virivuṟṟāḷ. virintu pōṉa pōkkil ceṉṟu, taṭṭuppaṭṭa kuṭiciyil taṅkiṉar. aṉṟiravu aṅkē taṅki mīḷavum puṟappaṭṭaṉar. nallāṉ mīṉāmpāḷik kaṇṭu tāṉ kumutavalliyiṭam mōtiram paṟṟiya veṟṟiyi urittu, nīlalōcaṉic ciṟippaṭuttat tavaṟiyati aṟintu varunti uriyāṭi mīḷ muyaṟci mēṟ koṇṭāṉ. mīṉāmpāḷi nīlakirikkuc cantiraṉiṭam cella ēvi, tāṉ nīlalōcaṉaṉip paṟṟap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. tappic ceṉṟa kumutavalli cellum vaḻiyil mīṉām pāḷik kaṇṭāḷ. iruvarum iṉitu uriyāṭiṉar. mīṉāmpāḷ nīlakirikkut tuṇivaruvatu pōl kāṭṭit takka iṭattuc ciṟivikkak karavāka eṇṇiṉāḷ.
avaḷ taṅki nekiḻvāka uriyāṭik koṇṭirukkum pōtu karumpāmpoṉṟu maṟintuvantu mīṉāmpāḷ kālic cuṟṟit tīṇṭiyatu. āṅkup putitu vanta oruttiyum uṭaṉvanta tōḻi oruttiyum naccunīkkum maruttuvam pārttum nacciṉ vēli kuṟiyavilli, ēṟiyatu, mīṉāmpāḷ piḻiyōm eṉṟa niliyil taṉ vayam irunta mōtirattit tantu nallāṉ maṉivi tāṉeṉṟum, nallāṉukku ekkēṭum ceyyak kūṭātu eṉṟu vēṇṭiyum, āyiṉum viḻippāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyum uyir nīttāḷ.
kumutavalliyum tōḻiyarum nīlakiri cērntu, tāṅkaḷ taṅkutaṟkut tiṭṭappaṭuttiya maṉōkarar vaḷamaṉi eytiṉar. avar veḷiyūr ceṉṟiruntār eṉiṉum vēṇṭiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ aṉittum ceytiruntār eṉpatu kēṭṭu aṅkē taṅkiṉar. maṉōkarar mīṇṭapōtu kumutavalli, avar illilē iruntu nīlalōcaṉaṉ varakkaṇṭāḷ. maṉōkarar nākanāṭṭaraci yiṭattum, nīlalōcaṉaṉiṭattum ovvoru mōtiram vaḻaṅki yiruntatiyum avaṟṟip peṟṟu avarkaḷ kumutavalliyum nīlalōcaṉaṉumē eṉa uṟutippaṭuttik koṇṭār.
eṉ iḷiya nēcarkaḷē nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum nākanāṭṭi orukāl aracāṇṭa kōc ceṅkaṇṇaṉ pērap piḷḷikaḷ āvīrkaḷ eṉpatu uṇmiyēyām eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. iruvar kikaḷiyum iṇittu maṇavaṭiyāḷam purintār. paḻiya varalāṟṟik kūṟi niṟivu ceykiṟār.
- irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


kumutavalli nākanāṭṭaraci

1966il civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam patippitta nūliṉi 
mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlip paṟṟiya kuṟippuri...
putumip pulavar putiṉap pulavarāṉār. `pūttut tōṉṟum putiṉak kalikaḷip pulavarkaḷ kūrntu kaṇṭu nūlkaḷip paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttum aṭikaḷār tāmē muṉṉōṭiyāki ipputiṉak kaliyil pukuntār.
tamiḻmaṇṇil kati vaṭippukaḷ iruntālum iḵtoru taṉikkaliyākap piṟappeṭuttatu mēli nāṭṭiltāṉ. `nāval eṉṉum ittāliyac colliṟkup `putumi eṉṟu poruḷ. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ittāli nāṭṭu aṟiñar tāṉiyēl tīpō eṉpār ipputumiyi mutalil paṭittār. ivari ikkaliyiṉ tanti eṉpar. ikkaliyiyē nām putiṉam eṉṟu tamiḻil ippōtu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
aṭikaḷār ippaṭippi vaṭikkavēṇṭumeṉṟu muṉintu iraṇṭu nūlkaḷip paṭittuḷḷār. oṉṟu `kumutavalli allatu nākanāṭṭaraci eṉṟa āṅkilap putiṉattiṉ taḻuval. ireyiṉālṭueḻutiya āṅkila nāval itu. itu tam kōṭpāṭu koḷkikaḷik kāṭṭi eḻuta vāyppāka iruntati uṇarntu vaṭittār. tamiḻarmatam nīṇṭakālat tuyil nīkka ikkaliyiṉ eḻutuvatākak kuṟittuḷḷār.
nallōr tuṉpuṟṟu iṉpuṟuvar; tuṉpam iḻip pōr tuṉpuṟuvar; uyirppali atilum māntappali kūṭātu; kātalar kūṭuvar; kātal pūṇṭōr kaṭavuḷ niliyiyum peṟuvar; nallāṭci kaṇṭōr vēṟṟuk kaṭciyi virumpār eṉṉum karuttukaḷiṉ potivu ipputiṉam. iṭiyiṭiyē tam kōṭpāṭukaḷākiya civam, cittāntam, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, taṉittamiḻ vaḷarttal mutaliyavaṟṟiyum vivaramāka vittuḷḷār. itaṉit tamiḻ marapil koṇṭucelvati nūliṉ peyarē aṟivikkiṟatu. itaṉi ōr `aṟaneṟip putiṉam eṉalām.
- kōvi iḷañcēraṉār
tamiḻ māmali
PREFACE

The art of fiction has, in modern times. become a potent means in the hands of educated men to awaken the finer instincts of man, so that this awakening of one’s own better part might itself serve to correct his mistakes and render him a useful and helping member of society. Within his mind exist in latency capabilities of various kinds and degrees of which one class is evoked by one particular set of outward facts and another by another set of facts. With such peculiar  occurances of outer world corresponds the manifestation of evil or good character of man. Generally speaking, the eveil part of human mind finds itself readily and easily called forth into activity by most of the surrounding objects while its better part, for want of fitting means, rarely gins access into the world outside. Now the most desirable function of a learned man should, as far as his innate abilities permit, have been to touch upon the inmost spring of man’s delicate nature and open the doorway to the region of love and virtue. But to go deep down the softest and most fertile part of human nature is not within the power of those unwary scholars who adopt improper means without discrimination. It is only those who are cutious and shrewd, those who spend apparently a greater part of their time in the selection of efficient means, in the making of it more attractive than it usually seems to be, that accomplish this very significant and well-meaning task. Take any branch of knowledge: philosophy or science, religion or literature, in their hands it becomes a very effective weapon.
Now, of all these instruments of knowledge, the fiction as an interesting branch of art appeals to all classes of people without efort and aggressiveness, and hence its place is dily becoming more and more prominent amidst them all. In almost all civilised countries but of India, especially southern India, the art of fiction has made a rapid and nearly a perfect development. But here it is still in its infancy. The host of novel writers are primarily led by mercinary and other motives. No genius has as yet arisen and no novel breathes the atmosphere of true art. In most of the Tamil novels that I have come across. I find neither plot nor character delineation, neither a true representation of life nor a chaste and felicitious diction of language. The story chiefly turns only upon love passion--the love in its most degraded type and gross earthly form. Although the Tamil language is pliant and rich in vocabulary capable of conveying the finest shades of meanings, yet in all the Tamil Novels published in a decade or two the diction is rendered very unwholesome by the introduction of unassimilated foreign words from Sanskrit and other languages and by the unhappy combination of words and phrases. In the matter of happy diction and choicest expression of thought a real and fastidious scholarship is the only necesary requirement; but of this not a single Tamil novel of recent times bears a notable mark. Even the Tamil renderings of some English Novels have been done by inelegant hands in so tasteless a manner that the beauty of the original appears in the new drees transformed into ugliness. Except a few who look upon themselves as civilised and up-to date, the min portion of the Tamils brought up in a simple and pure life do not like these novels at all. They like to read novels written in a pure and simple. Tamil style depicting the nature and human life as found in their experience but agreeably ennobled by the moral eelevation of the novelist’s ideal thought. Having keenly felt their want I ventured to produce this adapted Tamil translation of an eminent English novel in the hope that the pure and simple Tamil into which the ideas of the original are cast would please the Tamils very much leading them further and further into an appreciation of the art and study of fiction coupled with their own progress of life.
To the Tamilians of yore and their descendants the art of fiction was not an unknown thing before the introduction of modern novel. How carefully this art was studied by them would become manifest from a glance at the critical commentary of Nakkirar on Iriyanar Agapporul written about the first century of the Christian era. And another poetic work of considerable literary merit like that of Chucer, I mean Udayanankadi, was already in existence some six centuries before Christ, in which many a tale of Tamil antiquity appears in a collected form each of which, of course, incorporated into the min story with so profound a sense of unity that every one of them looks like to many inseparable limbs in a whole body. Besides this interweaving of one story into the other, the entire poem drawing faithful portrits from real life and tracing the distinctive features of individual character, combine them all to one complex whole so as to impress the mind of the reader with a sense of the strong unifying power of the poet. But it was a poetic age. Even story-telling went on in verse. From the domin of poetry fiction was slowly emerging to breathe the freer atmosphere of prose, when the pernicious influence of Sanscrit broke in upon the mind of the Tamil scholars and vitiated their taste for simplicity of life. A whole series of Puranic legends and obscene myths found their way into Tamil. And the Tamil people of mediaeval period having come under the influence of priests began to pay unlimited respect to mythical accounts of Gods and lost all their study of real life and with it the art of fiction also. But fortunately for us the study of English has once more opened our eyes to the real grandeur pertining to the art of fiction; and once agin has the unwholesome contact of Sanscrit begun stealthily to sap up the vitality of the Tamil language.I need, therefore, hardly say that it is obligatory on every son of the Tamil country to wake up from his long indifference and do his utmost to render his services in the cuse of his own mother tongue Tamil which is, in fact, one of the few highly cultivated ancient languages of the world. I have great pleasure in saying that the following Tamil adaptation of a famous English novel was done by me to my greatest satisfaction and it is for the readers to judge how for I have succeeded in preserving the profound artistic skill of the English author in chaste, easy and elegant diction of Tamil.

THE SACRED ORDER OF LOVE, 
PALLAVARAM,	VEDACHALAM
1st of May 1911

atikāram - 1
vaḻi naṭantu pōkum iḷiñaṉ

ulakattil mikacciṟanta mali nāṭṭip paṟṟi varuṇittuc collap pōkiṉṟōm. teṟkēyuḷḷa kumarimuṉiyiliruntu mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkarip pakkamāy vaṭakku nōkkic cellukiṉṟa mēṟkaṇavāy malit toṭar eṉpatu oṉṟuṇṭu. immalit toṭaril nīlakiri malikku arukāmiyil uḷḷa malināṭu iyaṟkiyamippiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḷaṅkaḷ nirampi mikap polintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaḷintu vaḷintu ōṭuṅ kāṉ yāṟukaḷālum teḷinta aruviyōṭṭaṅkaḷālum nīr ūṭṭappaṭum pūkkaḷ niṟinta veḷinilaṅkaḷum, iṉippāṉa paḻaṅkaḷ niṟinta tōppukaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭa mēṭṭu nilaṅkaḷum, kāṭcikku iṉiya pacumpul ceḻippāy vaḷarn tirukkum paḷḷattākkāṉa nilaṅkaḷum. vaḻinaṭippayaṇam pōkiṉṟavar kaḷukkup peru makiḻcci tarukiṉṟa malikaḷiṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa  vaḻikaḷum vāṉil mīṉkaḷ viḷaṅkutal pōlap pallāyiram pūkkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirukkum meṉpul vaḷarntu uyarnta poṟṟikaḷum. pañcu, caṇal, nārppaṭṭu mutaliyaṉa maṇṭi vaḷarntu kāṟṟāl aliyuṅ kollikaḷum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum aṅkuḷḷa tōppukaḷiṉ uḷḷē nuḻintu kāṇpēmāyiṉ mātuḷampaḻam, palāppaḻam, māmpaḻam, nārattampaḻam mutaliyavaṟṟic cumicumiyāyt tāṅki niṟkiṉṟa maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu iruppatu kāṇalām. aṅkuḷḷa malikaḷiṉiṭiyiṟ cantu veḷikaḷilē uḷḷuruvip pōyk kāṇpēmāyiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷiluṅ kuli kuliyāyp paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkukiṉṟa tīviya tirāṭcik koṭikaḷ piṉṉal piṉṉalāyp piṇintiruttal kāṇalām. akaṉṟa ilikaḷi vilakkik koṇṭu puṟantōṉṟik kiṭakkuṅ kommaṭṭip paḻaṅkaḷ paḻutta koṭikaḷ tāmē eppakkat tiṉum peruviḷiccalāyiruntalāl avviṭaṅkaḷil irukkum ēḻik kuṭittaṉakārarum appaḻaṅkaḷi oru poruṭ paṭuttuvatilli. .iviyēyaṉṟip paṟaṅkippaḻam, pūcaṉip paḻaṅ kumpu kumpāyp paḻuttut toṅkum kollikaḷik kāṇpavar ivi iyaṟkiyilēyē ivvāṟu koḻumiyāy viḷin tirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyāmal kuṭikaḷāl naṉṟākat tiruttip payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟē niṉippar. ikkolli kaḷic cūḻntu vēli pōlirukkum putarkaḷiṉ mēṟ civakkap paḻutta koḻuṅkaṉikaḷuṭiya kovvik koṭikaḷum, kuṉṟimaṇik koṭikaḷum paṭarntirukkiṉṟaṉa. marap pontukaḷ tōṟum maliveṭippukaḷ tōṟun tēṉīkkaḷ palamalar kaḷiṉiṉṟun tiraṭṭit tokutta tittippāṉa tēṉ aṭikaḷ nirampi yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi iṅkēyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ pakkattil kāṭṭut tārākkaḷ taliyi acittu acittuc celvatu kāṇalām; aravam aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa avviṭattil paṟavit tokutikaḷ paṟakkum pōtu uṇṭākum iṟakkiyiṉ ōcitāṉ ovvorucamayaṅ kēṭkappaṭum. nīr ōṭikaḷil kariya aṉṉappaṟavikaḷum veḷiya aṉṉappaṟavikaḷum moḻu moḻuveṉṟu ōṭum nīril caṟuvalāy mitantu celkiṉṟaṉa; varakaṅ kollikaḷil maṭappamāṉa puḷḷi māṉkaḷum kalimāṉkaḷum mikavum virivāyt tuḷḷit tuḷḷik kaḷippāy ōṭukiṉṟaṉa; yārum varuttuvār iṉmiyāl muyalkaḷ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp pulnilaṅkaḷil accamiṉṟi mēyntu tirikiṉṟaṉa; cilavēḷikaḷil iru maruṅkum paḻutta maraṅkaḷ variciyāy aminta kāṭṭu neṟiyi metuvāyk kaṭantu celkiṉṟaṉa. cilavēḷikaḷil mikavum parumaṉāṉa kaḻuku iṅkumaṅkum paṟantu pōvatu kāṇalām; āyiṉum avi arukāmiyil uḷḷa mali uccikaḷil tām kūṭukaṭṭi vacikkiṉṟaṉa. ammalikaḷiṉ kariya muḻiñcukaḷiliruntu pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa aruvikaḷ uṇṭākik kīḻ aṭivārat tiluḷḷa nilattiṟ pāyntu ataṉi vaḷampaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam aṭivārattiluḷḷa vaḷappamāṉa nilamum, ataṉi aṭuttu uyarnta malittoṭarum tammil iruvēṟu vakippaṭṭa iyaṟkiyuṭiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkutali vēṟeṅ kuṅ kāṇpatu aritu. attaṉitta malit toṭar pākaṅkaḷil malikaḷ oṉṟaṉ mēloṉṟāy uyarntum, kuṉṟukaḷ mēṟ kuṉṟukaḷ ceṟintum, uyarattiṉ mēl uyaramāyk kaṟpāṟi kaḷum, accam vāynta ceṅkuttāṉa koṭikaḷum iruṇṭa vaḻi kaḷum, aruvi viḻuntu kuṭinta perum paḷḷaṅkaḷum, paṉip pāṟikaḷum niṟintum oruṅkupaṭṭut tōṉṟum mika uṉṉatat tōṟṟamāṉatu mika uyarcciyuṭiyatāyum, kaṇṭārkku uḷḷa mēmpāṭṭi viḷivikkum peruntakimi yuṭiyatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ā! iṅṅaṉam nām varuṇittuk kūṟiya aṭivārat tiṉiyum viyakkaṟpālatām avvuṉṉata malit toṭari ṉiyum mēṟkuvintuḷḷa vāṉam mēkamiṉṟi nīlaniṟañ ciṟantu teḷivuṭiyatāy virintu evvaḷavu paricuttamāy viḷaṅku kiṉṟatu!
koccimutalāṉa teṉṉāṭukaḷiliruntu mēṟpakkamāy vaṭatēcañ celluvōr tam iṭakkippuṟattil virintu kiṭakkum im maliyanāṭu iṅṅaṉam pēraḻakuṭiyatāyc ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭu koḷka. nīlakiri maliccāraliluḷḷa immalināṭu cēra vamicattārkku urimiyuṭiyatāyiṉum akkālattil aracāṇṭa naraciṅkavaruma cōḻaṉ eṉṉum vēntarvēntaṉ cāḷukkiya nāṭṭu aracar mutalāyiṉārip puṟaṅkaṇṭu pērāṟṟaluṭiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiṉamiyāl, maṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭu vēntarāṉa cēra pāṇṭiyar ivaṉōṭu etirkka añci amintiruppa malināṭṭuk kōṭiyiluḷḷa nīlakiri maliccāral ataṉi yaṭuttuḷḷa cōḻa nāṭṭōṭu oruṅku cērntu cōḻa vamicattārkku uriyatu pōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. naraciṅkavaruma cōḻaṉum annāṭu taṉakku urimi yuṭiya taṉṟātal naṉkuuṇarntu ataṉi muṟṟun taṉvayappaṭutta niṉiyāmal taṉpaṭiyil oru pākatti aṅku nilippittu aṅku naṭattappaṭum vāṇikam cevviyāka naṭipeṟum poruṭṭu ataṉik kāppatu pōl avinayittuvantāṉ. annīlakiri nāṭṭil uḷḷōrum vēṟṟaracaṉ oruvaṉukku nām aṭaṅki oḻukak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷōm eṉpati niṉi yāmal, tām virumpiyavāṟu tammuṭiya ciṟṟaracaṉ kīḻ iṉitu kālaṅkaḻittu vantārkaḷ. im maliya nāṭṭiluḷḷa makkaḷ aḻakāy iruppar, āṇmakkaḷ ellārum nīṇṭuyarntu oṭintu viḻuvatu pōṉṟa aḻakiya uṭampum pārvikku nalla tōṟṟamum uṭiyarāy iruppar; peṇmakkaḷ ellārum tam uṭampiṉ teḷivāṉa cāyalāṉum, uṟuppukkaḷiṉ mikat tiruntiya amippāṉum, avvuṟuppukkaḷellām orē oḻuṅkāyk kaṉinta aḻakuṭimiyāṉum, ulakameṅkum piracitti peṟṟuc ciṟakkiṉṟārkaḷ.
ki.pi, 625-vatu varuṭam iḷavēṉiṟ kālattiṭiyil orunāḷ or iḷiñaṉ mikavum aḻakāṉa oru kutiri mēl ēṟik koṇṭu nām muṉṉē varuṇittuc colliya maliya nāṭṭil vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉukkup patiṉeṭṭu vayatu irukkalām. ivaṉ pārvikku nalla tōṟṟam uṭiyaṉāyum iruntāṉ ivaṉ viḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟa oruvakiyāṉa cuṟucuṟuppu, ivaṉ uruvameṅkum kaḷḷat taṉmiyuṭiyatōr tōṟṟatti viḷi vittut tiṭukkiṭac ceytalāl ivaṉi aḻakuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu collakkūṭa villi. maṟṟu, ivaṉ uṟuppukaḷ mikac cevviyāṉa amippuṭiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa. cilavēḷikaḷil ivaṉ kaṇṇiṉ tōṟṟatti nōkkukiṉṟavarkku ivaṉ viram, vañcaṅ, koṭumi mutalāṉa tīya kuṇaṅkaḷu ṭiyavaṉ pōl kaṭumiyāyt tōṉṟum. āyiṉum, mali nāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟa varkku ittakiya pārvi potuvāka iruttalāl, itu koṇṭē avar kuṇaṅkaḷi aḷantaṟital oru ciṟitum poruntātu. iṅṅaṉam nām varuṇikkiṉṟa ivviḷiñaṉuṭiya uṭampiṉ cāyal peṇkaḷukku iruppatu pōl teḷivāyt tikaḻntatu; āyiṉum makaḷirkku uḷḷa meṉmiyilli; avaṉ kaṉṉaṅkaḷil kaṭaṟcippiyiṟpōṟ civanta vaṇṇat tōyccal illiyāyiṉum iḷam paruvattiṟku uriya civanta niṟañ ceḻumiyāy iruntatu, mōvāyil mayir illi, mīcimayir talimayiri viṭa mikak kaṟukaṟuttu arumpiyiruntatu, tali mayir paḻuppu niṟamāyp paṭṭup pōl vaḻuvaḻuppāy iruntatu; immayirip piṭarivariyil naṟukkit toṅka viṭṭut taliyiṟ poṟcariki viḷimpukōtta ciṟupāki cūṭiyirun tāṉ, uṭampiṉōṭu oṭṭi iṟukap piṭittirukkum kāṟ caṭṭi, kiccaṭṭi yiṭṭiruppataṉāl pūṅkompu pōl oṭintuviḻum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya avaṉ uṭampiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷ tiruttamāṉa aḻakuṭaṉ poruttamuṟṟiruttal naṉku tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. avaṉ iṭuppil iṟukap piṇittirukkum arikkaccil ilēcāṉa koṭuvāḷ oṉṟu toṅkiṉatu, melliya tōliṉāl titta aṭiccaṭṭi muḻaṅkāl aḷavum māṭṭi yiruntāṉ. avaṉ ēṟic celluṅ kutiriyiṉ cēṇaṅ kaliṉam mutaliyaṉa ellām naṭuttaramāṉa ciṟappuṭiyaṉa; curuṅkac collu miṭattu, ivviḷiñaṉ periya pirapu vīṭṭiluḷḷa ēvalāḷi kaḷil uyarnta niliyil uḷḷavaṉākat tōṉ ṟiṉāṉ.
iṉi oruvakiyāṉa kuṟippuḷḷa ivaṉ kaṇkaḷ peruttu nīla niṟamuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟippi nōkkāmal avi teḷivāka iruttali māttiram pārppavarkku avi mikavum aḻakākat tōṉṟum, nalla  kuṇaṅkaḷukku ōr uṟi viṭam pōlat tōṉṟi akaṉṟu uyarnta neṟṟi poṉṉiṟamāy maḻamaḻaveṉṟu viḷaṅkutalāl avaṉ kaṇkaḷil uḷḷa koṭuṅkuṟippu avvaḷavākat terita lilli. avaṉ mukattiṉ kīḻppākaṅkaḷellām avaṉ nallaṉ eṉṟaṟiyumpaṭiyāka amintiruntaṉa. mikac ceḻumi yāka avaṉ itaḻkaḷ avaṉiṭattuk kaḷḷaṅkavaṭu illāmi kāṭṭuvaṉa pōl iruntaṉa. avvitaḻkaḷ viriyuntōṟum iṭiyē mayakkun taṉmiyuṭiya nakiyoḷi tōṉṟutal pōla maṟṟi āṇ makkaḷiṭattil pārttalaritu. aṅṅaṉam nakitōṉṟum pōtellām, neyppāṉa pavaḷat tuṇṭiṉmēl ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir virital pōlavum, iṅkulikac ceppiṉ vaṭimpil viḻumiya muttukkaḷ patittatupōlavum iraṇṭu variciyākap paṟkaḷ tōṉṟum. 
iṉi ivvāṟu varuṇittuc collappaṭṭa iḷiñaṉ kuṭakilirun tāyiṉum, iṉṉum ataṟkun toliviluḷḷa nākanāṭṭiliruntāyiṉum vantavaṉpōlmēl kaṭaṟkariyic cārnta mēṟkaṇavāy maliyanāṭṭil vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉ nīlakiri nakarattiṟku nērē pōkum vaḻiyiṟ cellāmal iṭiyē mēṟkaṇvāy maliccāraliṟ koṇṭupōyviṭuṅ kuṟukalāṉa oru cantu vaḻiyil ippōtu tirumpic ceṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ cila nāṭkaḷāka vaḻinaṭantu vantirukkavēṇṭum; ēṉeṉil, ciṟitu tūramāttiram payaṇam paṇṇiyiruntāl taṉkutiriyip paṟṟi ippōtu kavali koṇṭu pārppatu pōl pārkkavēṇṭuva tilliyām; ippōtu ataṉic ciṟitu nērattiṟku oru taram niṟuttik kīḻ iṟaṅki nilattiṟ koḻumiyāy vaḷarntirukkum iṉiyapul mēyaviṭṭut tāṉ tīvitirāṭcak koṭi paṭarnta maraniḻalil iruntu cāyntu koṇṭu talikkumēlē kuṭumpukuṭumpāyt toṅkumpaḻak kuliyil oṉṟupaṟittut tiṉṉuvāṉ; tirumpavuñ ciṟitu nēraṅkaḻittup payaṇam paṇṇuvāṉ; ivvāṟu cila nāḻikiyāka avaṉ vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ.
iṉi ivaṉpōkuñ cantuvaḻi mika nīṇṭatā yiruntālum metteṉṟa puṟkaṟṟi neṭuka viruttalāṟ kutirikku vaḻiyiṟ ciṟitum varuntamilli. atuvēyumiṉṟi avvaḻiyi liṭi yiṭiyē pala ciṟṟaruvikaḷ ōṭivaruvatāl kutiri taṉ kālkaḷik kuḷirac ceytukoṇṭu taṇṇīr aruntit tākattiyun taṇittuk koḷvatāyiṟṟu. iṉi ñāyiṟu maṟikiṉṟa camayattil ivviḷiñaṉ accantuvaḻiyiṉ muṭiviṟpōyc cērntāṉ; naṭuvil iṭiveḷi yāyuḷḷa mēṭṭuppāṅkāṉa nilattiṟku irupakkattum ceṅkuttāy āḻntukiṭakkum paḷḷattākkuḷḷa maliccāral toṭar puṟṟu irukkiṉṟatu. im mēṭṭuppāṅkāṉa malit toṭarpi ṉuḷ avaṉ nuḻintu pōvāṉāyiṉāṉ; iṅṅaṉam pōkap pōka iruḷōveṉṟu karukittōṉṟum malit tōṟṟam avaṉ cilanāḻiki muṉṉē vaḻinaṭantuvanta vaḷaviya nilattiṉ ṟaṉmiyōṭu evvaḷavu māṟupaṭṭuk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu! mālippoḻutu muṟṟum vantu iruṇṭavuṭaṉē, karaṭu muruṭāṉa immalippāṅkil virintu kiṭakkum oru kāṭṭukku arukēvantu cērntāṉ. avaṉ aṅkuḷḷa vaḻittuṟika ḷellām muṟṟun terintavaṉ pōlavum, aṅku etaṟkum añcātavaṉ pōlavuṅ kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. maraṅkaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu piṇintu vaḻituṟiyiṉṟi ellām kuḻampalāyk kiṭakkum avviruṇṭakāṭṭiṉ iṭiyē putitāka vaḻiccelvōr yārkkum aṟiyakkūṭāta vaḻiyi ivaṉ kūrmiyāṉa kaṇkaḷ terinteṭuttaṉa; piṉṉum ōr ari nāḻikiyil taḻik kumpiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē veḷiccan tōṉṟutaluṅ kaṇṭukoṇṭāṉ; maṟṟuñ cila nimiṣaṅ kaḷuḷ, maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil kuṟukku uttiraṅ kaṭṭi ataṉmēl veḷḷāṭṭut tōlāṉum māṉṟōlāṉun titta pōrvi yiṭṭuc camitta āṟu kūṭāraṅkaḷuḷḷa oru pācaṟiyiṉi aṭintaṉaṉ.
iṉi ippācaṟi vīṭṭu muṟṟattiṉ iraṇṭiṭattil neruppuk koḻuntuviṭṭu erintatu; in neruppaṇṭiyil cilar mālik kālattiṟku vēṇṭum uṇavu camittukkoṇ ṭiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellārum nīṇṭuyarntu valiyarāyum aḻakāyum iruntaṉar. taliyil civappukkuñcan toṅkum āṭṭuttōṟkullā aṇintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṭiyi-liṭṭirukkum tōṟcaṭṭi kālukkup pātukāvalā yiruppa taṉṟi naṭivirivukkut taṭi ceyvatilli. atu vallāmal, kittup pākkiyum ciṟṟuṭivāḷuñ cerukiya arip paṭṭi kiyil kūriyakattiyum toṅkaviṭṭiruntārkaḷ. kūṭāraṅ kaḷuḷ eṭṭippārttāl evvaḷavu āṭkaḷ iruntārkaḷō avvaḷavu tuppākkikaḷum iruntaṉa. curuṅkac colluṅkāl, immaliyanāṭṭil neṭuṅkālam vaḻippaṟi koḷḷi ceytukoṇṭu varupavarāyum, innāṭṭiṟ pukunta naraciṅka varuma cōḻaṉpaṭikkuk koṭumpakivarāyumuḷḷa kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṉaruḷ oru pakutiyiṉarāka iṅkuḷḷa ic ciṟukūṭṭat tār kāṇappaṭṭaṉar.
iṉi ippācaṟi vīṭṭaṇṭi vantukoṇṭirukkum iḷiñaṉ taṉkutirik kuḷampaṭiyiṉ ōci acciṟu kūṭṭattār kātiṟ paṭumeṉpatu terintavuṭaṉē avaṉ taṉ itaḻkaḷik kuvittup paḷīreṉṟu oru cīḻki yaṭittāṉ. accīḻkki ōci akkāṭeṅkum uruvic ceṉṟatu. akkaḷvar kūṭṭattār taṅkaḷ naṇpaṉ varavi aṟivataṟku iccīḻkki yōci ōr aṭiyāḷam pōlum! mikavum tiṇintu piṉṉikkiṭattalpaṟṟi annāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē naṭattikkoṇṭu vanta taṉ kutiriyōṭu avviḷiñaṉ avar etiriṟ pukumpoḻutu neruppiṉ veḷiccam avaṉ mukattiṟpaṭavē avarellārum uṭaṉē avaṉit terintukoṇṭaṉar. avaṉō avarkaḷiyum avviṭattiyum putitukaṇṭavaṉāy illi; avarkaḷum avvāṟē avaṉōṭu naṉku paḻakiṉavar kaḷippōl iruntaṉar. naṇparkkuriya mukamaṉurikaḷ oruvarkkoruvar vaḻaṅkiṉar; avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ av viḷiñaṉuṭaṉ vanta kutiriyik koṇṭupōyt tīṉi mutaliyaṉa aḷittāṉ.
uṇmiyākavē itu nērttiyāṉa kutiri eṉṟu oruvaṉ ataṉ kaḻuttiṟ ṟaṭṭikkoṭuttu uṉakku itu nalla utavi ceytatu eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ.
ām eṉṟu avviḷiñaṉ maṟumoḻi tantu eṉakku iṉṉum neṭunēram iruntatu paṟṟi innāḷ muḻumiyum mikametuvāy ataṉi naṭattikkoṇṭuvantēṉ. uṅkaḷ talivaṉit tavaṟāmaṟkāṇuñcamayam naṉku aṟivēṉātalāl irāppoḻutiṟkumuṉ iṅkuvara muyaṉṟilēṉ eṉac coṉṉāṉ.
vēliyiruntāl oḻiya eṉṟu akkaḷvaṉ aruki luḷḷa tī veḷiccattil oru kuṟipput tōṉṟanakittu eṅkaḷ talivaṉukku ellānēramum oṉṟutāṉ. atu niṟka, uṉ kutiriyippaṟṟip pēcumiṭattu eṉṟu toṭarntu kutiri māmicattiṟ paḻakkappaṭṭavarpōl ataṉ uṭampiṉ oḻuṅki yum ovvōr uṟuppiṉ amiviyum metuvāka aḷantu nōkki ā itu mika uyarnta pirāṇi eṉa moḻintāṉ.
intac cātikku uriya aṭiyāḷam iṅkē iruk kiṉṟatu. eṉṟu avviḷiñaṉ ataṉ piṉ ṟoṭiyil tīykkappaṭṭa oru ciṟiya taḻumpik kuṟittuk kāṭṭi avaṉōṭu ottup pēciṉāṉ; ēṉeṉil, ammalināṭṭiluḷḷa kutirikaḷiṉ cātip pakuppiṉi vaḻuvātu neṟippaṭap pōṟṟi vippataṟku avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉam aṭiyāḷam iṭutal vaḻakkam.
uṇmi! intac cāti nallatu tāṉ eṉṟu ataṉip pārttuk koṇṭirunta maṟṟoruvaṉ viyantāṉ. āyiṉum, eṅkaḷ talivaṉ ēṟic celuttukiṉṟa kutiriyiṉum itu tāḻntatutāṉ. ēṉeṉṟāl, ataṉpiṉ toṭiyil ilāṭak kuṟi yiruppataṉi nāṉ collumpōtu iḷiyōy, nīyē aṟivāy eṉṟāṉ.
naṇpaṉē, niṉ talivaṉ kutiriyiṉi nāṉ aṟiyēṉ pōlavum, kutiriccātikaḷuḷ atumika arumi yāṉa teṉpatik kuṟikkum ilāṭa aṭiyāḷatti nāṉ kāṇēṉ pōlavum nī pēcukiṉṟāy. atu niṟkaṭṭum, eṉakkāka eṉ kutiriyiṉip pārttukkoḷ; uṅkaḷ talivaṉōṭu nāṉ uṭaṉē pēcalvēṇṭum eṉṟu avviḷiñaṉ moḻintāṉ.
iraṇṭāvatu pēciṉa kaḷvaṉ appaṭiyē avaṉit taṉpiṉṉē varumpaṭi aḻittuk koṇṭu pōvāṉā yiṉāṉ. vaḻituṟiyillāta akkāṭṭiṉ iṭiyilē iruvarum pukuntu kūṭāra muṟṟattil erintu koṇṭirunta neruppu veḷiccat tiṟku appuṟam pōy viṭṭārkaḷ; eṅkum orē iruḷāy iruntatu. āṉāṟ kaḷvaṉō tāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṉi mikavum naṉṟāy aṟintavaṉpōl virivāka nērē ceṉṟāṉ. iḷiñaṉō avaṉpiṉṉē oṭṭik koṇṭu pōyiṉāṉ. ōciyiṉṟi eṅkum iruṇṭukiṭakkum avviṭattil caṭutiyilē yārō kāvalāḷaṉ aṟikūvum ōr oli kēṭṭatu. uṭaṉē ataṟkuk, kūṭappōkuṅ kaḷvaṉ etō viṭi coṉṉāṉ, ciṟitu tūrattil maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil maṟṟoru veḷiccan tōṉṟac cila nimiṣaṅkaḷil oru kūṭārattiṟku muṉṉē tī eriyum muṟṟattil iruvarum pōyc cērntārkaḷ.
muṉṉēkaṇṭa  pācaṟi vīṭṭik kāṭṭiṉum ik kūṭāram periyatāyum uyarnta taṉmiyuṭiyatāyum iruntatu; itaṉpaṭāttiluḷḷa tōliṉ ōraṅkaḷil nīlappaṭṭut tikkappaṭṭiruntatu; itaṉ nuḻivāyilum, nīlappaṭṭiṉāṟ ceḻumiyāṉa pūttoḻil ceyyappaṭṭa tiri iṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tīyil maṇaṅkamaḻa uṇavu camikkum maṭpāṇṭat tip pārttuk koṇṭē ōr ēvaṟkāraṉ uṭkārntiruntāṉ. iḷiñaṉi aḻittuk koṇṭu vanta kaḷvaṉ kūṭārat tuḷḷē avaṉi nuḻintu pōkumpaṭi colli viṭṭut tāṉ veḷiyē niṉṟuviṭṭāṉ.
akkūṭārattiṉuḷḷē nilattil virikkappaṭṭa pāyiṉ mēl irupattumūṉṟu allatu irupattunāṉku vayatiṟku mēṟpaṭāta oruvaṉ cāyntu koṇṭiruntāṉ; avaṉ mukattil piṟari aṭakkiyāḷum poruṭṭu iyaṟkiyilēyē amintuḷḷa orukaḷi tōṉṟiṟṟu. avaṉ uyaramākavum mellitākavum iruntāṉāyiṉum. ottuc caminta avaṉ uṭampum, cevviyākap poruttappaṭṭa uṟuppukaḷum avaṉ mey valiyiṉ mikutiyiṉi iṉitu viḷakkiṉa. avaṉ talimayir kariyatāy mika aṭarntiruntatu. avvāṟē mikavum kariyavāṉa avaṉ kaṇkaḷ perum pirakācamuṭiyaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avaṉ mukattiluḷḷa maṟṟi uṟuppuk kaḷellām kaṭitteṭuttaṉa pōl iṉitamin tiruppavāyiṉa; avaṉ mukatti oru pakkac cāyvāyp pārttāl māttiram mūkku kaḻukupōṟ ciṟitu vaḷivāyiruttal tōṉṟum. kaṉṉa mīciyum mīciyuṅ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mōvāy mayiriṉṟi naṉṟāyc cirikkap paṭṭiruntatu. kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟku uriya vaṇṇam avaṉ aṇintirunta āṭi taṉ āṭkaḷ aṇintiruntavaṟṟik kāṭṭilum uyarntatāyum. arik kaccup pūttoḻil ceyyappaṭṭatāyum kittulakkum, curikiyum veḷḷippiṭi yuṭiyaṉavāyum, kuṟṟuṭivāḷum avvāṟē maṇikaḷ aḻuttiya piṭiyuḷḷatāyum. pāyiṉmēl avaṉ arukiṟ kiṭanta cuḻal tuppākki tiṟamiyāṉa toḻiṟpāṭu uṭiyatāyum viḷaṅkiṉa. kūṭārattiṟ ṟoṅkaviṭṭirunta veḷḷi viḷakku ivaṉ mukattil oḷiparappiṉamiyāl ivaṉ mukakkuṟippi naṉku aḷantu pārttu ivaṉ māṭṭu yārkkum añcāta oru vaṉkaṇmiyum, maṉa ūkkamum, evaṟṟiyum poruḷ ceyyātu muṭikkum pērāṟṟalum peruntiṟamiyum amintu kiṭattali eḷitilē aṟintukoḷḷalām. avaṉ maṟṟiyōrik kāṭṭilun taṉakkuḷḷa uyarcciyiṉiyum naṉṟāy aṟintirun tāṉ. iṅṅaṉam avaṉ ellāvakiyāṉun taṉ kīḻ uḷḷār. taṉcoṟpaṭi aṭaṅki naṭantu taṉ iṭattu aṉpuṭiyavarāy naṭantu koḷḷavum, tāṉvēṇṭum poḻutu avarivalintu oṉṟu ceyyumpaṭi vaṟpuṟutti muṭikkakkūṭiya āṟṟalum uyarcciyum uḷḷavaṉāyiruntaṉaṉ.
avviḷiñaṉukku ikkaḷvar talivaṉ putiyaṉ allaṉ. kūṭārattiṉuḷ nuḻintavuṭaṉē avviḷiñaṉ ivaṉi mikavum mariyātiyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅka attalivaṉuñ cilacamayaṅkaḷil uyarntōr tammiṉum tāḻntōri naṇpuṭaṉ aṉpāy naṭattu muṟippaṭi nēyarkkuriya iṉiya moḻikaḷ colli aḻittāṉ.
cantirā, niṉvaruki naṉṟākuka. eṉṉaceyti koṇṭu vantāy? eṉṟu akkaḷvar talivaṉ kēṭpāṉā yiṉaṉ.
ataṟku ellāñ cevviyāka nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avviḷiñaṉ maṟumoḻi tantiṭṭāṉ.
.itaṉāl, nākanāṭṭaraci yiṭattum nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉṉum putta iḷiñaṉiṭattuṅ karutiya vaṇṇam kāriyatti muṭittu vantāyeṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ attalivaṉ.
mēṟkari eṉṉun taṉ nakarattiliruntu payaṇam pōvataṟku mikavum icintavaḻiyiṉi nīlalōcaṉaṉukkuk kāṭṭiyatu pōlavē, nākanāṭṭiliruntu varum kumuta vallikkuṅ kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu av viḷiñaṉ ottukkūṟiṉāṉ.
cantirā, naṉku ceytāy eṉṟu taṉmukattil makiḻcci tōṉṟac coṉṉa attalivaṉ mīṇṭum muṉṉorumuṟi nī eṉakku mikavum nuṭpamāy eṭuttuccoṉṉa varalāṟṟil uṉakku nampikki uṇṭō eṉpataṉit tirumpavum aṟiya virumpukiṉṟēṉ. ēṉeṉil peṟappaṭukiṉṟa apporuḷiṉ uyarcciyi nōkkiṉallatu, avvaḷavu periyatōr apāyakaramāṉa kāriyattiṟpukuvataṟku nāṉ niṉikka māṭṭēṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ.
nāṉ nuṇukkamāy virittuccolliya ovvoṉṟum uṇmiyāmeṉṟē nampukiṉṟēṉ. tāmarivēli eṉṟu collutaṟkup peritum vāyppuṭiya maliyavēliyiṉkaṇ apperum poruḷ putiyalāy irukkiṉṟatu eṉṟāṉ cantiraṉ.
putumi! putumi! immēṟkaṇavāy malittoṭar muḻuvatum eṉakkut teriyāta cantu, pontu, mūli,muṭukku oṉṟum illiyeṉṟu eṉṉip peritāka eṇṇiyirunta eṉakkuntaṭṭuppaṭāmal tāmarivēli eṉpatoṉṟu iruttal peritum viyappāyirukkiṉṟatu! aṅṅaṉam oṉṟirukkumeṉa ituvariyil nāṉ aiyappaṭṭatu milliyē! āyiṉum, appaṭiyoṉṟu irukkalām:- appaṭiyoṉṟu irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum eṉṟu kātiṟkēṭkum paṭiyākavē attalivaṉ muṉakiṉāṉ.
aḵtappaṭittāṉ! uyarntōy. mali nāṭarkkuḷ vaḻaṅkum ic ceytiyāvatu umakku eṭṭiyirukkavēṇṭumē eṉṟu cantiraṉ aḻuttip pēciṉāṉ.
ām! ataṉālētāṉ nī mutaṉmutal akkatiyiṉi eṉakkuc coṉṉapōtu nāṉ avvaḷavu kavaṉamāyk kēṭṭēṉ. nāṉ kuḻantiyāyiruntapōtu eṉ peṟṟōr - eṉṟu collum pōtē attalivaṉ mukamvēṟupaṭap perumūccuviṭṭu ām nāṉ kuḻantiyāyiruntapōtu iṟantupōṉa eṉpeṟṟōr, tamiḻarkku mutaṟṟantiyuntāyum iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa kālattil immalittoṭar iṭiyil uḷḷa tāmarivēliyil avviṟivaṉ kaṭṭaḷi yāl taṅkiyiruntaṉareṉavum, avarukkuppiṉ makkaḷ yārum ataṟkuṭ pukuta iṭam peṟuvatilli eṉavum, āyiṉum orō vorukāl neṭuṅkālam iṭiyiṭṭut tiruvaruḷāṇiyāṟ celuttappaṭukiṉṟa paricuttaṉāṉatuṟavi paricuttamāṉa av viṭattik kuṟuki āṇṭuc cuṟṟilum uyarntu viḷaṅkum malikaḷiṉ naṭuviṟ kīḻē polintutōṉṟum iṉpamāṉa iḷaṅkāviṉi kāṇpaṉ eṉavum colvatu vaḻakkam eṉṟāṉ.
eṉṉuṭiya katiyāṉum nīṅkaḷ colliyavaṟṟāṉuṅ kuṟippikkappaṭṭa avviṭantāṉ nantamiḻarkku mutaṟṟantiyun tāyum uṟinta iṉpanilameṉṟu nāṉ collavilli. eṉ aṟivukkup pulappaṭṭapaṭi kēṭṭāl, aḵtu avviṭam aṉṟeṉpatē eṉ karuttu. nammutaṟṟanti tāyārkkuppiṉ avviṭam aḻintu pōyiṟṟeṉal tāṉ uṇmi; āyiṉum immēṟkaṇavāy malittoṭaril eṅkō ōriṭattil avar irunta ammaliyavēlip peyarkoṇṭa ōriṉpa iḷaṅkā viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭumeṉpatu māttiram nāṉ uṟutiyākac colvēṉ. iṉi nāṉ coṉṉa maṟṟi varalāṟukaḷik koṇṭu, aṅkup poṟkuviyal uṇṭō illiyō eṉpataṉi nīṅkaḷē aṟintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, nāṉ coṉṉatu maliyanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum varalāṟṟuṭaṉ mikavum poruntiyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu cantiraṉ coṉṉāṉ.
avvaralāṟṟiṉāl, atu pōṉṟa iṉpamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ immalittoṭariṟ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avviṭaṅ kaḷil mikac ciṟanta malarkaḷ māttiram pūkkiṉṟaṉa, arumiyāṉa iḷamaraṅkaḷ māttiraṅkoḻukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, pāmpu mutalāṉa ūrvaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇavākuntaḻikaḷ aṅkilli eṉṟum, amiḻtamayamāyuḷḷa at taṉitta iṭaṅkaḷil, pulika ḷēṉum narikaḷēṉum nuḻiya māṭṭā eṉṟum, avviṭattic cuṟṟi arippaṭṭikipōl uḷḷa ceṅkuttāṉa malikaḷ puṟattēyiruntu pārppavarkkut tiri pōl avar pārviyiṉit taṭiceytalē yaṉṟi veḷiyili runtu pōm makkaḷ aṭiccuvaṭu tōyāvaṇṇam eḻuppiya varampupōlavum kuḷirkālattuk koṭuṅkuḷir kāṟṟum vēṉiṟkālattu veṅkatirveppamum nuḻiyāvāṟu kaṭṭiya araṇpōlavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭiciyākap poṟkuviyalum veḷḷikkuviyalum putiyalāyuḷḷa kaṉikaḷum uyarnta muḻumaṇikaḷ kuvinta muḻiñcukaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum nām viḷaṅka aṟikiṉṟōm. eṉṟu perukiyeḻunta makiḻcci taṇintāṟpōlat tāḻnta kuraliṟ pēciṉāṉ attalivaṉ.
parampari parampariyākac collappaṭṭu immali nāṭarkkuḷ vaḻaṅkivarum avvaralāṟṟiṉi nīr num uḷḷattiṟpotintu vit tiruntum, nāṉcoṉṉa akkati yiṉi nīr oru kaṇamāvatu aiyamuṟattalippaṭṭatu eṉakku mikavum viyappāyirukkiṉṟatu eṉac cantiraṉ coṉṉāṉ.
nāṉ ataṉi aiyappaṭavilli; mutaliliruntē nāṉ atip paṟṟi aiyappaṭāviṭṭālum, nī coṉṉavaṟṟi ellām uṇmiyākavē nampic coṉṉāyō eṉṟaṟi yattāṉ palakēḷvikaḷ kēṭṭēṉ eṉṟu etaṟkum añcāta taṉ iyalpukku iciyip poṟumiyāṉa kuralōṭu attalivaṉ pēciṉāṉ.
nāṉ coṉṉavaṟṟil nīr ēṉtāṉ aiyappaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cantiraṉ kēṭṭāṉ.
avaṉ eṉ nilimiyil uḷḷa oruvaṉ taṉ āḷ colvati aiyappaṭāviṭṭālum oru tarattiṟku iraṇṭu taram ōr uṇmiyi āyntaṟiyavēṇṭāmā? eṉṟu virintu kēṭṭuc cantirā! nāṉ colvatikkēḷ. uṉṉik kaṭiciyāka nāṉ kaṇṭatumutal nāṉ niṉittatu itu! nī coṉṉa kalavaramāṉa upāyattil talikīḻāy viḻuntu varuntu talikāṭṭiṉum, avaṉ mēṟpāyntu viḻuntu avvirakaciyam terinta avaṉikkoṇṭē ataṉitterintu koḷḷal nallataṉṟō! eṉṉi nampu; uṉ talivaṉi orumuṟi iṅkupiṭittukkoṇṭu vantu vittukkoḷvēṉāyiṉ - eṉpataṟkuḷ;
avaṉ cettāluñ cāvāṉ oḻiya ivvirakaciyatti māttirañ collāṉ eṉṟu cantiraṉ iṭiyiṟkūṟi ik kāriyaṅkaḷellām eṉakkutterintapiṉ, avaṉ kaṭiciyākap payaṇappaṭṭup pōṉapōtu avaṉ kūṭavē pōvataṟku nāṉ camayam pārttuk koṇṭirukkavilliyā- eṉṟu col vataṟkuḷ;
nallatu nallatu eṉṟu attalivaṉ avacaramāy iṭimaṭakkic cantirā, nī coṉṉavāṟē ākaṭṭum! atik kuṟittu nāṉ ārāyumpōtu, nī ceyta upāyappaṭi ceytaliviṭa vēṟuvaḻiyilli, avvupāyam ciṟantateṉṟu urippataṟkum nāṉ piṉvāṅkēṉ. periyanaṉmi kiṭippa tāyiṉ, atu mikavuṅkalavaramāṉa upāyameṉṟu coṉṉa     
taṉāl uṇṭākum iṭiñcalkaḷi niṉintu piṉ vāṅkuvēṉ eṉṟu niṉiyātē, naṇpā cantiraṉē, ceytu muṭikkakkūṭiya kāriyattiṟku eḷitāṉa vaḻiyetuveṉṟu kāṇpatē eṉakku vaḻakkam. ilēcilē ceytu muṭikkakkūṭāta kāriyamāyiruntāl māttiram ciṟitu cuṟṟāṉa vaḻiyiṟ cella muyalvēṉ. iṉivirittuc collavēṇṭuvatilli; pōtum! ippōtu mālikku uṇavu cittamāyirukkum nām ataṉi uṇpēm. uṇṭapiṉ nī eṉpakkattiṟ paṭuttu ayarvu tīrttukkoṇṭu viṭiyaṟkāliyil nīlakiri nakarattil uṉ talivaṉiṭam pōyccēr eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.

atikāram - 2
vaḻi maṟittal

ippōtu collappaṭum viṣayaṅkaḷ muṉṉatikārattiṟ kūṟiya karumaṅkaḷ naṭanta mūṉṟunāṉku nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ nērntaṉavākum.
ñāyiṟu kīḻtticiyiṟ ṟōṉṟukiṉṟa camayattil āṟupēr kuṭakiluruntu nīlakiri nakarattiṟkuc cellum pātiyi aṭuttu aṭarntirukkuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉ nīḻalil taṅki yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ ēṟi vanta kutirikaḷiṉ nilimiyip pārkkum pōtu avarkaḷ iraviṟ cila nāḻikikaḷāka vaḻi naṭantu vantirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. ivarkaḷ taṅkiya iṭam nīlakiri nakarattiṟku nāṟpatumil tūrattiṟku ippāl, mēṟkaṇavāy malittoṭarukku arukāmiyil uḷḷatu. oruyāṇṭil mukkāṟ paṅkuparuvam mika iṉiyatāy viḷaṅku vatāṉa ammalināṭṭil, ivviṭiyaṟ kālamāṉatu mikavum aḻakitākap polivuṟṟatu. paṟavikkuḻāṅkaḷ maraṅkaḷiṉmēṟ pāṭik koṇṭiruntaṉa; aṉṉappuṭkaḷ kālvāykaḷiṟ cerukkuṭaṉē mitantu koṇṭiruntaṉa; koḻuviya malarkaḷ itaḻvirintu kāṟṟu vāṭṭattil naṟu maṇam parappiṉa; aḷaviṟantaṉavāy muḻumuḻup parumaṉuṭiya vāṉa navamaṇi kaḷuṅ kulikuliyāyt taḻikaḷiṉ iṭiyē toṅkik koṇ ṭiruppatupōlat tōṉṟum. mātuḷam paḻam, elumiccampaḻam, koṭimuntirippaḻam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṟattiṇintu viḷaṅkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēl ñāyiṟṟiṉ iḷaṅkatirkaḷ tōyntu miṉumiṉuveṉṟu miḷirntaṉa; vāṉameṉṉum nīlavitāṉattiṉ mēl mēkakkaṟi oruciṟitum illāmiyāl atuteḷintu kāṇappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. māṟṟuyarnta poṟ kōḷampōṟ pirakācikkum iḷañāyiṟu taṉ katirkaḷic cūḻavirittu nīlaniṟattōṭu viracum iṭantavira, avvāṉiṉ teḷinīlavaṇṇam iṭi yaṟuntu pōkāmal orēoḻuṅkāyt toṭarpuṟṟu vaḷivāyt tikaḻntatu.
ippōtu ikkuṟuṅ kāṭṭilirunta āṭkaḷ aṟuvarum, muṉṉatikārattil nāṅkāṭṭiya kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟ cērnta varkaḷ eṉṟaṟiya vēṇṭum. nām muṉcolliyavāṟē uṭi uṭuttup paṭikkalaṅkaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar. āyiṉum, ippōtu cuḻal tuppākkikaḷit tām vēṇṭiyapōtu kiyāḷat takkavakiyāy mutukiṉ mēṟṟoṅka viṭṭiruppatu oṉṟu tāṉ vēṟupāṭākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷukkut talivaṉpōṟ kāṇappaṭṭa oruvaṉ ivviṭantāṉ. nām terintu koṇṭapaṭi nām virumpiya kāriyam ippōtu ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkum nāḻikikkuḷ muṭintuviṭum. vāruṅkaḷ. nammuṭiya kutirikaḷi ikkāṭṭuk kuḷḷē koṇṭupōy viṭṭu mikunta nēramuṅ koḻumpul mēyac ceyvippōm. eṉṟu aḻittāṉ.
iruḷā, ippōtu nī colliya kaṭṭaḷikaḷ? eṉṟu maṟṟoruvaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
avi curukkamāyiruntālum, nammuṭiya talivaṉ eppōtum namakku iṭuṅkaṭṭaḷikaḷip pōlavē kaṭumiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu iruḷaṉ viṭi pakarntaṉaṉ.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, avi mēyappaṭuvataṟkum iṭañ ceykiṉṟaṉa. nammuṭiya talivaṉākiya apperiya payaṅkara māṉa nallāṉ taṉ karuttiyum tāṉ muṭikkuṅ kāriyaṅ kaḷiyum eṉakkuṅkūṭac colluvatilli. iṅkē nammi yaṉuppi viṭṭatupōlavē tāṉum maṟṟōr iṭattiṟkup pōyiruppati ūkikkumpōtu, cantiraṉāṟ collappaṭṭa oru ceyti allatu ōr upāyattiṟkum itaṟkum etō campantam iruppatākat terikiṉṟatu eṉṟu iruḷaṉ coṉṉāṉ.
cilakālamāy mūṉṟu nāṉku taram nammuṭiya talivaṉiṭam vantu koṇṭirukkum cantiraṉ eṉkiṉṟa avviḷiñaṉ yār eṉṟu avarkaḷil maṟṟoruvaṉ 
viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku iruḷaṉ nīlakirip paṭṭiṉattilirukkum paṇak kāraṉ oruvaṉ vīṭṭiṟku avaṉ uriyavaṉ eṉpataṟkumēl avaṉippaṟṟi eṉakku oṉṟumē teriyātu. itu niṟka. nām iṅṅaṉam vīṇpēccup pēcikkoṇṭu kālattik kaḻippati viṭa, nallāṉ eṉṉiṭattuc colliya ēṟpāṭukaḷi uṅkaḷukkuc collukiṉṟēṉ kēḷuṅkaḷ! nām āṟupēr irukkiṉṟōm; nām etirkkavēṇṭiyatu mūṉṟupērittāṉ eṉpatu teriyum. inta mūvaril oruvaṉ atikārattāṉum poruḷāṉum uyarnta putta vālipaṉ eṉṟum, maṟṟi iruvarum avaṉukkuc cēvakar eṉṟum aṟivīrkaḷāka. avarkaḷ cevviyāṉa kutirikaḷiṉmēl āyutaṅkaḷit tarittu varuvārkaḷ eṉpatil cantēkam illi; ataṉmēlum avarkaḷuṭiya maṉō tiriyam vēṟēyāka irukkiṉṟatu. kūṭumāyiṉ avarkaḷmēl irattañ coṭṭāmal mūvariyuñ ciṟiyākap piṭikkavēṇṭuvatu nammuṭiya kaṭami eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
ā! nām tuppākkiḷi upayōkikka nērumāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam irattañ cintāmaṟ piṭippatu mikavum varutta māyirukkumē eṉṟu avarkaḷil maṟṟoruvaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku iruḷaṉ itaṟku māṟākattāṉ uṅkaḷukku itu collap pōkiṉṟēṉ. ammūvariyum nammuṭiya malik kōṭṭikku uyiruṭaṉ koṇṭu pōyviṭṭāl, nām eṭutta kāriyamum nan talivaṉ iṭṭa kaṭṭaḷiyum mikavuñ cemmiyāka muṭintaṉavākum. ātalāl, uṅkaḷāl āṉa maṭṭum pārttuk kūṭāta viṭattu māttiram uṅkaḷik kāttukkoḷḷum poruṭṭut tuppākkikaḷi upayōkap paṭuttuṅkaḷ. itutāṉ nallāṉ iṭṭa kaṭṭaḷiyum eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
mikavum valimiyuṭiya malināṭarkaḷāṉa nām aṟuvarum ōr imippoḻutil antap puttarkaḷ mūvariyum piṭiyāviṭṭāl, appuṟam eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu maṟṟoruvaṉ coṉṉāṉ.
ataṉmēl vaḻimaṟikkappaṭum pirayāṇikaḷ varukiṉṟa pātiyi iruḷaṉ paricōtittukkoṇṭu celvāṉā 
yiṉāṉ; aṅṅaṉam pōkiyil, pātiyiṉ .irupakkaṅ kaḷiṉumuḷḷa kuṟuṅkāṭu oṉṟu cērntu mēlē kavintirukkum ōr iṭattit terintukoṇṭu aṅkiruntu nammuṭiya kāriyatti niṟivēṟṟa virumpi, ataṟku arukāmiyil uḷḷa ōriṭattiṟ kutirikaḷi naṭattikkoṇṭupōyp pulmēya viṭuttu, ovvoruvarum oru noṭiyil ēṟi etirppataṟku vēṇṭum oḻuṅkukaḷellām ceytu vittut tāṉum cittamāy patuṅkiyiruntāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam orumaṇinēraṅ kaḻintatu; kaḻintavuṭaṉ neṭun tūrattil mūṉṟu pirayāṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tuvark kāviyūṭṭiya taliccīrā aṇin tiruntami yāṉum, kuṟippiṭṭa kālattil mūṉṟu pērāy nīlakirip paṭṭiṉattiṟkuc cellumpātiyil vantu koṇṭiruntamiyāṉum, tām etirpārtta pirayāṇikaḷ ivarkaḷ tāmeṉṟu akkaḷvar uṟuticeytaṉar. kutirimēṟ cavāriceytuvarum mūvaril oruvaṉ maṟṟiruvarkkuc ciṟitu muṉṉēṟi vantaṉaṉ; ivaṉ taṉ mārpil iṟuka muṭintu aṇintirukkuṅ kuppāyattiṉ poṟcarikimēlum, poṉ miṉukkuc ceyta vāḷuṟi mēlum, kutiriyiṉ uyarnta cēṇaṅkaliṉam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mēlum ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ tōyntu oḷirntaṉa. ākiyāṟ kuṭakiliruntu nīlakiri nakarukkup pōkum celvattāṉum, atikārattāṉum uyarnta putta iḷiñaṉ ivaṉtāṉ eṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamuṟa iṭamilli. iṅṅaṉam ētum camucayamiṉṟi muṉṉēvantu koṇṭirukku miḷiñaṉ kaṭṭiḷa miyōṉāy mikavum aḻakuṭiyaṉāy iruppatum, avaṉ piṉṉē varum maṉitar iruvarum iḷamikaḻintu tiṇṇiya uṭampuṭiyō rāyiruttalum aṅkup patuṅkiyirunta kaḷvar kaṇṭukoṇṭaṉar.
uṭaṉē iruḷaṉ oru kuṟi ceytaluṅ kaḷvarellārum maraṅkaḷiṉ naṭuviṟ kutirimēl ēṟikkoṇṭu, muntirik koṭikaḷ piṉṉal piṉṉalāy neruṅkit tiri maṟippup pōṟ paṭarntirukkuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭṭōrattil, pirayāṇikaḷ kaṇkaḷukkut teṉpaṭāmal etirppataṟkuc cittamāy vantu niṉṟaṉar. vantuniṟṟalum, iraṇṭām taramum orukuṟi ceyyap paṭṭatu. uṭaṉē māṉ mantiyāyiṉuṅ kāṭṭu vilaṅkiṉaṅ kaḷāyiṉuñ caṭutiyil taḻikaḷāṉum paḻaṅkaḷāṉum maṟikkap paṭṭa tiriyik kīṟik kiḻittup pukuntatupōla mēl vantu viḻuntu, iruvar nīlalōcaṉaṉiyum maṟṟi nālvar avaṉpiṉ vanta maṟṟiruvariyum vaḷittuk koṇṭaṉar.
ivvāṟu akkaḷvar evvaḷavu curukkeṉap pukunta ṉarō avvaḷavu virivil apputtar mūvar valatuki yiṉum vāḷ uṟi kaḻikkappaṭṭu miṉṉiṉa; atē camayattiṟ cēṇattiṟ cerukiyirunta kit tuppākkiyiṉi iṭatu kiyāl ovvoru varum uruvi yiḻuttaṉar. ākavē akkaḷvar etirpārtta vaṇṇam avarkaḷ kāriyam niṟivēṟuvatu aritāykkāṇap paṭṭatu; iruḷaṉum maṟṟoru kaḷvaṉum nīlalōcaṉaṉ mēṟ kutittu viḻavē, avaṉ orukiyāṟ cuḻaṟṟi vīciya vāḷ oruvaṉ mēṟ paṭumpoḻutu, avaṉ maṟṟik kiyiṟ piṭitta tuppākki maṟṟoruvaṉic cuṭṭu kīḻē vīḻttiyatu. iruḷaṉō avvāḷvīccukkut tappikkoṇṭu oru kiyil vāḷēnti maṟṟoru kiyāl avaṉip piṭikka muyaṉṟāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉ pakivaṉippōlavē taṉ kutiriyit tiṟampaṭa naṭattuñ cāturiyam naṉku vāyntava ṉātalāl kutiriyiṉ kaṭivāḷattip piṉṉukku piṭittu iḻuttut taṉ pakivaṉ taṉṉik kāttukkoḷḷutaṟku iṭañceytāṉ. ivvāṟu cilanēram iruvarkkum pōr mūṇṭu naṭakkiyil, tiṭīreṉṟu iruḷaṉ taṉ kutiri mēṉiṉṟum vaḻuvi maṟintu pōyiṉāṉ; appaṭi maṟikiyil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tāṉ avaṉmēl eṟinta paṭivīccu avaṉi kollamāṭṭāmiyāl avaṉ maṟintu pōṉatu ētō ōr upāyamāka irukkiṉṟateṉa uṭaṉē terintu koṇṭāṉ. teriyavē avaṉ taṉ kutiriyiṉiṉṟum pāyntu pakivaṉip piṭittuk koḷḷa muyalāmal iccaṇṭiyiṉ muṭipi yaṟiyumporuṭṭu oru kaṇanēram vāḷāviruntaṉaṉ.
uṇmiyākavē iruḷaṉ avvāṟu maṟintupōṉatu oru tantiram. malināṭṭukkaḷvar pōrmukattil ittakiya tantiraṅkaḷ ceytu veṟṟiyaṭivatil mikavun tiṟamiyu ṭiyavarkaḷāy iruntaṉar; pōr naṭantukoṇṭirukkiyil caṭutiyilē kutiri mutukiṉ mēliruntu vaḻuvi ataṉ vayiṟṟiṉ kīḻppukuntu kāl utiyum vaḷiyattiṟ kālviral māṭṭi iraṇṭu kikaḷāṉum piṭariyip piṭitta vaṇṇamāy toṟṟik koṇṭiruppar. ïªj¢ Nœ¢á Æida¿ahj vâÇ j‹ FâiuÆÅ‹W« ghŒªJ ï¡Fâiu nkš VWth dhÆD«., aṉṟi etiriyōṭip pōyiṉāṉ eṉṟu niṉintu taṉ kutiriyi viṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkuvāṉā yiṉum uṭaṉē avaṉ mēṟ pulipōṟ pāyntu paṟṟik koḷḷuvar. itē vakiyāy nīlalōcaṉaṉit taṉ kutiriyiṉiṉṟum iṟakkum poruṭṭu iruḷaṉ ivvāṟu ōr upāyañ ceytāṉ.
ōr imikoṭṭil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tāṉ aiyappaṭṭatu uṇmiyeṉṟu kaṇṭu kutiri cēṇattiniṉṟum uruviṉa taṉkit tuppākkiyi iruḷaṉ maṇṭikku nērāyk kuṟikāṭṭit taṉakkuk kīḻppaṭiyumpaṭi kēṭṭaṉaṉ. itaṟ kiṭiyiṟ pōrpurintu koṇṭiruppōrkkup piṉṉēyiruntu kuṇṭukaḷ vantu viḻuntaṉa; iccamayattiṟ kaḷvar iruvar muṉṉē kāṟṟiṉuṅ kaṭuki vantu iruḷaṉi nōkki uṉṉik kāppāṟṟik koḷ eṉṟaṉar.
miṉṉal tōṉṟuvatupōla iruḷaṉ kutiri appuṟan tuḷḷippāyntu pōyiṟṟu; atē camayattil nīlalōcaṉaṉ tuppākkiyiṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa kuṇṭukaḷ muṟipaṭṭup paṟantōṭuṅkaḷvaṉ kātōramāyk kiṟukiṟu veṉṟu cuḻaṉṟu pōyiṉa. paṟantōṭiṉa iruḷaṉ nūṟṟimpatu muḻantūram pōṉavuṭaṉē taṉ kutiriyi niṟutti ippuṟan tiruppi mutukiṟ ṟoṅkaviṭṭirunta kittuppāk kiyiṉi aviḻkkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. nīlalōcaṉaṉ kūriya kaṇkaḷukku itu terintavuṭaṉē, malināṭṭukkaḷvar kuṟi piḻiyāmaṟ cuṭuvatil vallavarkaḷeṉpataṉi uṇarntu, tāṉ ataṟku ilakkāy akappaṭāmal, taṉ kutiriyi muṭukkik kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉuḷḷē pukuntuppōyt tappiṉāṉ. ivaṉ itaṟkuḷ nuḻinta piṟkaṇattē tuppākkik kuṇṭukaḷ kiṟukiṟuveṉṟu cuḻaṉṟu ceṉṟaṉa. iruḷaṉ taṉ etiri tappippōyviṭṭati aṟintu kutiriyit tiruppikkoṇṭu virintu pōyiṉāṉ.
itaṟku muṉṉamē nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉpiṉvanta kāvalāḷar eṅṅaṉamāyiṉār eṉpataṉi oru noṭip poḻutilē aṟintukoṇṭaṉaṉ; ippōtu ataṉic cevviyāka aḷantaṟivataṟkuc camayam vāyttatu. kaḷvar aṟuvaril iruvar apputta iḷiñaṉi maṟittaṉar. ivviruvaril oruvaṉ cuṭappaṭṭu viḻuntiṟantāṉ; maṟṟi yōṉ piḻittōṭip pōyiṉāṉ. kāvalāḷar iruvariyum vaḷittukkoṇṭa kaḷvar nālvaril, iruvar taliyiṟ cuṭappaṭṭu uyir oḻintu nilattiṟ kiṭantaṉar. maṟṟiruvaril, oruvaṉ kattiveṭṭiṉāl vāḷēntiyatōḷ aṟupaṭṭum, maṟṟoruvaṉ kaḻuttu muḷḷelumpu oṭintum etirniṟka lāṟṟātu muṉṉē coṉṉavāṟāy ōṭippōyiṉar. ākiyāl nīlalōcaṉaṉ kāvalāḷarum tan talivaṉip pōlavē veṟṟimaṟañ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum ivar kaḷil oruvaṉukku māttiram iṭatu tōṭpuṟattuc catiyil vāḷ aḻuntu veṭṭukkāyamiruntatu; itupaṟṟi avar ciṟitum maṉantaḷartaliṉṟi tammuṭiya talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻntaṉar.
kollappaṭṭa kaḷvar mūvaruṭiya kutirikaḷum ivarkaḷ kaṭ pārvikku akappaṭāmal avviṭatti akaṉṟu ōṭippōy viṭṭaṉa. nīlalōcaṉaṉum avaṉ kāvalāḷarum vaḻi naṭuviṟ kiṭanta appiṇaṅkaḷi akaṟṟik koṭimuntirip pantar niḻaṟkīḻ kiṭatti viṭṭut tām pōm vaḻiyiṟ celva rāyiṉar. cellum pōtu iṅṅaṉam vantu vaḻimaṟittavarkaḷ vēṟār nōkkamumiṉṟik koḷḷiyiṭutalē kuṟippāka vuṭiya āṟalik kaḷvar kūṭṭattiṟ cērntavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇiṉar.
appōtu nīlalōcaṉaṉ avarippārttu akkoṭiya varkaḷ kuṟuṅkāṭṭil maṟintiruntapaṭiyē nammiccuṭāmal etirēṟivantu pōriṭṭatu eṉakku mikavum putumiyāy irukkiṉṟatu. appaṭi avarkaḷ cuṭṭiruntāl nām ellām uyiriḻantiruppōm. malināṭṭuk kaḷvar kuṟitavaṟāmaṟ cuṭuvatil mikavum vallavarāyiṟṟē eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
atikkēṭṭatum, aṭarntu paḻuttuppōṉa tāṭiyum, maṉavuṟuti, ūkkam, poṟi mutaliya uyarkuṇaṅkaḷ naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa mukamum, taṭittut tiṇinta uṭampum uṭiya vaṉāṉa mūttakāvalāḷaṉ eṅkaḷ araciṉ celvamē, nammiṭattuk koḷḷi koḷvatuṭaṉ nammic ciṟiyākap piṭittuk koṇṭupōyt tam mali araṇil vittiruntu viṭutali peṟumporuṭṭup peruntokiyāṉa poruḷum aṭiyalām eṉkiṉṟa nōkkattōṭu avarkaḷ nammi etirttavarkaḷāka eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟaṉaṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ eṉ nampakamuḷḷa kēcarivīra nī ūkittuc colliyatu eṉakkup poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. am maṟavarkaruttu atuvāyiṉ. avar tōlvi yaṭintatum nām veṟṟiyaṭintatum avarkaḷuṭiya nalla ēṟpāṭṭiṉāl allavā? avarkaḷ mutaṟkaṇattilēyē nammic cuṭṭuk kollāmaṟ kiṭṭa vantu etirttu nīlakiri nāṭṭārkkuriya valimiyiṉiyum kuṭakunāṭṭārkkuriya valimiyiṉiyum paricōtittu aṟintu koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
atu kēṭṭa iḷiyakāvalāḷaṉāṉa viyākkiravīraṉ eṅkaḷ aracurimic celvamē, mutaṉmutaṟ piṉmutuku kāṭṭiyōṭiṉa paḻikārar iruvarun taṅkaḷāl oṉṟuñ cāyātu eṉṟu kaṇṭavuṭaṉē tuppākkiyi eṭuttuc cuṭṭaṉar; eṉ coṟpaṭiyellām vaṭṭamāyttirintum muṉṉōṭiyum piṉṉōṭiyum naṭakkiṉṟa eṉ kutiriyiṉ ilākavatti ṉālaṉṟō taṅkaḷi vicuvacikkum ūḻiyakkāra ṉāṉa aṭiyēṉ tappip piḻittut taṅkaḷōṭu ippōtu pēcik koṇṭirukkiṉṟēṉ! eṉṟaṉaṉ.
eṉṟatuṅ kēcari vīraṉ eṉ kātarukilum vicikkāṟṟu viṟuviṟuveṉṟu vīcutal pōlak kuṇṭukaḷ cuḻaṉṟu pōyiṉa. atukiṭakka nām kutamacākkiyari vaṇaṅkuvēmāka! veṟṟi nampakkamāyiṟṟu. viyākkira vīraṉ tōḷil vāḷ veṭṭup paṭṭatu oṉṟu tavira, nām vēṟōr iṭiyūṟu miṉṟi vantu cērntami viyappēyām. eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam pēcikkoṇṭē nīlalōcaṉaṉum avaṉ kāvalāḷarum vaḻi piṭittup pōyiṉar; araciḷiñaṉum ippōtu taṉ kāvalāḷaruṭaṉ cērntu pōyiṉāṉ; maṟṟoru muṟiyum vaḻimaṟittal nēriṭuvatāyiṉ ataṟkuc cittamāy upayōkittaṟporuṭṭu mūvarun tuppākkiyik kiyil ēntiya vaṇṇamāyc ceṉṟaṉar.
cellumpōtu ciṟitu nēraṅkaḻittuk kēcarivīraṉ eṅkaḷ aracē nammi vaḻimaṟittavarkaḷ, cōḻa nāṭ
ṭārkkum malināṭṭārkkum cilakālamāy periyatōr accattiṉi yuṇṭupaṇṇivaruṅ kaḷvar talivaṉic cērnta varkaḷāyirukkalām eṉpatu taṅkaḷukkut tōṉṟavilliyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ nallāṉik kuṟippikkiṉṟa ṉiyō? ām, avaṉippaṟṟip putupputuk katikaḷ innīlakiri maliccāral elliyit tāṇṭik kuṭakilirukkum nammuṭiya cevikaḷukkum eṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; uṉakku uṇmiyic collukiṉṟēṉ, nāṉ antak katikaḷi oruciṟitum nampuvatēyilli. nallāṉ eṉṉum ōr āḷē uṇṭeṉpatu peruṅkaṭṭu, avaṉ oru maṉappēy tāṉ eṉṟaṉaṉ.
eṅkaḷ taliva, nallāṉ eṉpatu kaṭṭāyirukkalām, meykāppāṉa cila turuppukkaḷuṭaṉ nīṅkaḷ pattiramāyp pōkalāmeṉṟu cilar kūṟiya uṟutimoḻikkum māṟākac ceṉṟa iraṇṭoru nāḻiki yaṉupavattiṉālēyē ippakkaṅkaḷil āyutapāṇikaḷāṉa āṟalikaḷvar kūṭṭam uṇṭeṉpatu namakkup pulappaṭavilliyā? eṉṟu kēcari vīraṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.
ataṟku nīlalōcaṉaṉ  ippirāyaṇañ ceyyumpaṭi eṉṉit tūṇṭutal ceytavōr tirumukaṅ koṇṭu vantavaṉāṉa anta nīlakiri nakarattiḷiñaṉṟāṉ aṅṅaṉam uṟutiyuri moḻintāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ meyyeṉṟu nampiṉatiyē kūṟiṉāṉ, atuvēyumaṉṟi nām pōvatip piṟar kavaṉiyāta vaṇṇamāy aṉṟō pirāyaṇañ ceyyumpaṭi kaṟpikkappaṭṭōm--ā! atō etirē oru kuṭici tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu! aṅkē nām kaṭukac ceṉṟu, aṅkuḷḷōrkku iṅkē nikaḻntaṉayāvum terivippōm. avarkaḷ nām vaḻiyilē pōṭṭu vanta piṇaṅkaḷi appuṟap paṭuttuvataṟku vēṇṭuvatu ētēṉuñceyvar.
appaṭiyē ammūvaruṅ kuṭiciyaṇṭipōyc cērntaṉar; aṅkirunta mutiyōṉ oruvaṉukku nikaḻnta vellāñ collap paṭṭaṉa. nallāṉ naṭattum āṟali toḻilkaḷ iṉitu naṭipeṟukiṉṟa mēṟkaṇavāy maliccāralukku neṭun tūrattēyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil iṅṅaṉam vaḻimaṟippu nērntatip paṟṟi avaṉ mikavum viyappaṭintavaṉ pōṟ kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. ataṉōṭu mūṉṟu puttarāl mika valiyarāṉa āṟukaḷvar muṟiyaṭikkap paṭṭataṉippaṟṟi avaṉ piṉṉum mikunta viyappaṭintu avarkaḷ coṟkaḷil avanampikkippaṭavē, avarkaḷ, kollappaṭṭa ak koṭiya kaḷvar mūvar uṭampum piṇamāyp pātiyiṟ kiṭattal kāṇalām eṉṟu uṟutiyurittaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam malināṭṭuk kāvalatikārikaḷukkut tāñ colla vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyic ceytu viṭṭuc, cilanēram ak kuṭiciyil viṭutikoṇṭa piṉ nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉ kāvalāḷar iruvaruṭaṉ payaṇam paṇṇap pukuntaṉaṉ. ik kāvalāḷar iruvarum veṟum ūḻiyakkārar allar. nīlalōcaṉaṉ ciṟanta maṉṉaṉ ākiyāl, avaṉuṭaṉ vanta ivarkaḷ pirapukkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiya vēṇṭum.
iṅku ivvaraciḷiñaṉ, cēkari vīraṉ, viyākkira vīraṉ eṉṉum iruvaruṭaṉum pōyk koṇṭirukkum pōtu, namakkunalla camayam vāyttamiyāl avaṉ uṭampiṉ aḻakiya tōṟṟattip paṟṟi varuṇittuc colla virumpukiṉṟōm. ivaṉ mikavum aḻakāyiruntāṉ eṉṟu muṉṉamē potuvākac coṉṉōm, ivaṉukkuk kariya aḷakamuṅ kariya periya viḻikaḷum iruntaṉa; ivaṉ mīciyāṉatu kival ōviyaṉ tukilikiyāl vaṉintavāṟu pōlap paḷapaḷaveṉṟu mēlutaṭṭiṉ mēṟ curuṇṭiruntatu.  ivaṉ mūkkuk kaḻuku pōṟ ciṟitē nuṉiyil vaḷintiruntatu, ituvum mukattil uḷḷa maṟṟi uṟuppukaḷum mika melliyataṉmi  yuṭiyavāyiruntaṉa. ivaṉ uṭampiṉ niṟam perumpālārkku uḷḷatu pōlak kariyatāyilli āyiṉum ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāṟ ciṟitu paḻuppu vaṇṇamāyt tōṉṟutaliṉ ivaṉuṭiya niṟam iḷamiyuruviṟ ciṟituparuvamutirccik kāṭṭiṟṟu. illā viṭṭāl, ivaṉ niṟam iṉṉun teḷivāy iḷamip paruvattiṟku icintatāy viḷaṅkum. ivvāṟu uṭampiṉ niṟam paḻuppāyt tōṉṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam āṇpālarkkuriya vēṭṭiyāṭutalil ivaṉ mikunta viruppa muṭiyōṉāyp pakaṟkālaṅkaḷil tirivatoṉṟē eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. vēṭṭiyāṭuvatiluṅ kutiriyēṟṟattilum ivaṉ nirampiya tiṟami vāyntiruntaṉaṉ. ciṟitu nērattiṟku muṉṉē vantu vaḻimaṟitta kaḷvarkaḷi añcātu ivaṉ puṟaṅkaṇṭu veṟṟi ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉatippōlavē, iṉṉuṅ kaṭumiyāṉa pōrmukaṅ kaḷilellām aṅṅaṉamē maṟañciṟantu tikaḻn taṉaṉ, ivaṉiṭattup poṟumiyum tayāḷakuṇamuṅ kuṭi koṇṭi runtaṉa; iṉṉum avaṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷiṉellām āṇṭaṉmiyōṭu kūṭiya oru peruntaṉmi ciṟantu kāṭṭuvatāyiṟṟu. accam eṉpataṉi ivaṉ aṟiyavē māṭṭāṉ. apāyam eṉkiṉṟa collik kēṭpiṉum nakiyāṭuvāṉ. ivviyal piṉaṉāyiṉum, oru pirāṇi varuntutalik kāṇiṉum piṟacīvar tuṉpuṟutalik kēṭpiṉum ivaṉ kaṇkaḷil nīr muttu muttāyt tuḷikkum
ivaṉukku ippōtu vayatu irupattoṉṟu, ivaṉuṭampu uyarnta pūṅkompupōl melliyatāyum, tiruntiya amippuṭiyatāyum mataṉavēḷ pōlak kikāl mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷ nīḷamāyum tuvaṇṭaṉa. vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa veḷḷic calavikkallil ivaṉ uṭampiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷi naṉ mātiriyāka vittu uḷiyāṟ cetukki iḻittu oru patumi amikkappaṭumāyiṉ atu vaḻuvaṟṟa āṇṭaṉmiyiṉik kuṟikkum vaṭivamāyp piracitti yaṭiyumeṉpatu tiṇṇa mēyām. ākavē avaṉ aṅkaṅkaḷiṉ amippiṉ aḻaki yām eṅṅaṉam varuṇikka māṭṭuvēm! avaṉ neṟṟi akaṉṟu amintu kiṭantatu; taṭippiṉṟi mika murintu vaḷinta ivaṉuṭiya puruvaṅkaḷ mikavum kariyavāy iruntaṉa; kaṇṇiṟippi cevviyākat tiṟantiruntaṉa; viḻikaḷ parumaṉāy imikaḷiṟ peṇkaḷuk kiruppatupōṟ karukiyaṭarnta mayirvariciyum iruntaṉa; taṉṉi uyarttip piṟarit tāḻttutal, cerukkaṭital, taṟukaṇmi mutalāṉa tīyakuṇaṅkaḷ ivaṉiṭattu oruciṟitum illi. āyiṉum āṇṭaṉmiyum pōrmukattu añcāmiyum eṉkiṉṟa uyarkuṇaṅkaḷ vāykkappeṟṟa iḷiñarkkut tamuyarvu tāmēyaṟiyum ōr uṇarcci iyaṟkiyāka uṇṭātalpōla ivaṉiṭattum ippeṟṟip paṭṭatōr uṇarcci tōṉṟutal uṇṭu. ivaṉ kaṉṉamīci vittirāviṭiṉum,  mēl itaḻil uḷḷa kariyamīciyum, elumiccampaḻattiviṭac ciṟitu paḻuppākat tōṉṟum mēṉiyiṉ niṟamum, etaṟkum añcāta kaṇkaḷ nōkkamum ivaṉ mukattil ōr āṇṭaṉmi viḷaṅkat tōṟṟuvittaṉa.
iḷiñaṉāṉa nīlalōcaṉaṉ iṅṅaṉam pēraḻaku ṭiyaṉāyt tikaḻntaṉaṉ. avaṉ uṭuttirunta uṭikaḷi nōkkumiṭattu akkālattu aracar vaḻakkappaṭiyē aṇin tiruntaṉaṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟiṟṟu. iruṇṭa pacci niṟamuṭiya caṭṭiyiṉ ōraṅkaḷiṉum, mārpuppuṟattiṉum pūttoḻil ceyyappaṭṭa poṟcariki piṉṉappaṭṭu miṉumiṉuveṉṟu miḷirntaṉa. ivaṉ ariyiṉ kīḻt tarittirunta kāṟcaṭṭiyil nīḷat tittirunta poṟpaṭṭiyiṉāl arikkīḻuḷḷa uṟuppu kaḷ kutiriyi naṭāttum pōtu mikavuntiruntiya ilakkaṇam vāyntu tōṉṟiṉa.  ivvāṟu ivaṉ aṇintirunta uṭi ivaṉ aṅkaṅkaḷil iṟukapporunti ivaṉ uruvavaḻakiṉi nirampavuñ ciṟap pittuk kāṭṭiṟṟu. ivaṉ toṅkaviṭṭirunta koṭuvāḷuṟi nām muṉmoḻintapaṭiyē poṉ miṉukkup pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṉ piṭiyil uyarnta muḻu maṇikaḷ aḻuttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kutiri mēṟpōrviyil poṟcariki yiṉāl aḻakiya urukkaḷ piṉṉappaṭṭu viḷaṅkiṉa. ellā vaṟṟāṉum āṇṭaṉmi iṉitutōṉṟak kutirimēl varum nīlalōcaṉaṉ aḻakiya uruvamāṉatu ammalināṭṭu makaḷir avaṉ celvatip pārkkumporuṭṭut taṅkuṭicikaḷi ṉiṉṟum puṟamvantu nōkkukiyil avarkku avaṉmāṭṭu mikunta viruppattiyum peruṅkātaliṉiyum viḷivittatu; ivaṉippārtta ammalināṭṭu āṭavarō ivviḷiñaṉ kutirimēl amarntuvarum ilēcāṉa niliyiṉiyum āṇṭa kimiyiṉiyum viyantu nōkki avaṉittāṅkiya kutiri yiṉ aṅka amippiyum uṟṟuppārttu makiḻntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, ivaṉ varukiyil ivaṉuṭaṉ piṉvaruvōr iruvarēyāyiṉum, ivaṉ uruvattōṟṟamāṉatu aṅkuḷḷār karuttiyellām ivaṉ vayappaṭuttu iḻuttatu.
avaṉum vaḻitoṭarntupōṉa vaṇṇamāy iruntaṉaṉ; ivvāṟu palanāḻiki kaṭantaṉa; tāṉun taṉkāvalarum iṭiyiṭiyē taṅki iḷippāṟik kutirikkun tīṉi koṭuttukkoṇṭu kaṭiciyāka mūṉṟuvaḻikaḷ piriyum ōriṭattilvantu cērntaṉaṉ. ippōtu avarkaḷ entavaḻiyiṟ celvateṉṟu teriyāmal tikittaṉar; immūṉṟil etu nērāka nīlakiri nakarattiṟkuc cellukiṉṟa teṉpatik kēṭṭu aṟiyum poruṭṭu arukāmiyil ētēṉuṅkuṭicivīṭu irukkiṉṟatāveṉat tēṭiṉar. accamayattil taṉakkuc ciṟitu tūrattil orukari mēl kiḻavaṉoruvaṉiruppati nīlalōcaṉaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. ikkiḻavaṉ ammalināṭṭil tāḻntakuṭiyiṟ piṟantavarkkuriya muṟippāṉa uṭi kaṭṭiyiruntāṉ; paṇiceyyappōmpoḻutu iḷippāṟutaṟ poruṭṭu aṅkēciṟitu nēram iruntavaṉākak kāṇap paṭṭāṉ. ivaṉip pārttu vaḻi etuveṉṟukēṭpa, avaṉun tāṉirunta vaḻiyē nērāka nīlakiri nakarattiṟkuc cellukiṉṟa teṉṟu urittāṉ. uṭaṉē nīlalōcaṉaṉ avaṉ mukamāy oru poṟkācu vīciṉaṉ. annāṭṭiluḷḷōr vaṟiyarā talāl ippoṟkāciṉik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ mikap paṇintu aṉpu tōṉṟa aḵtīntōṉi vāḻttuvar, maṟṟu ikkiḻavaṉō akkāciṉi mikavum alaṭciyamāy eṭuttuk koṇṭu ak kācu tanta iḷiñaṉi nōkki veṟuppuṭaṉ curukkamākac cilanaṉṟiyuri kūṟiyapiṉ, araciḷiñaṉukkut tāṉ kāṭṭiya vaḻikku etir mukamāyppōyiṉāṉ.
pōṉavaṉ appakkattuḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭṭiṉuḷḷē pukuntu marattokutikaḷiṉ naṭuvē maṟittu amikkappaṭṭa taṉkuṭiciyiṉuḷ nuḻittu,. tāṉ eṭuttuvanta poṟ kāciṉit tarimēl vīciyeṟintu mikavum veṟuppōṭu orukaṇamāttiramāvatu eṉ pakivaṉ kiyiṉiṉṟum ikkāciṉip peṟumpaṭi nērntatē! avaṉ eṉṉit tōṟka aṭittaṉaṉē! nāṉ avaṉāl tāḻvaṭintēṉē eṉa mika varuntik kūṟiṉāṉ.
