kumutavalli mukkāṭu iṭṭiruntum avaṉ avaḷit terintukoṇṭāṉ; - avaḷ vaṭivattiṉ mikavum nērtti yāṉa tiruntiya amippiṉālum, avaḷ mukkāṭṭiṉ maṭippu kaḷiṉ kīḻ eṭṭippārppatu pōṟ ṟōṉṟiya avaḷ karuṅ kūntaliṉ oru curuṇṭa kaṟṟiyiṉālum avaṉ avaḷik kaṇṭu koṇṭāṉ. avaḷ kaṇkaḷ aṅṅaṉam caṭutiyil orutīya oḷiyi vīciyatu eṉṉa? ataṉāl avaṉ ētō oru maṉak kalakkattāṟ caṭutiyiṟ paṟṟappaṭṭā ṉeṉpatum, ataṉi avaṉ taṉṉuḷ aṭakkamāṭṭāta vaṉāyiṉāṉeṉpatum pulappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa vallavō? palakiḷi yākkip piṉṉar avaḷic cuṟṟi valipōṟ piṉṉappaṭṭa iraṇṭakamāṉa upāyaṅ kaḷukkup piṟaku nākanāṭṭaraci tāṉ cēravēṇṭiya iṭattil vantu cērvaḷeṉa avaṉ etirpārkkavilliyō? allatu evariyō etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntu,. kutirik kuḷam paṭikaḷiṉ ōci teruvil niṉṟatik kēṭṭuk kumutavallikku māṟāka vēṟu oruvari etirppaṭum nampikkiyāl ōṭi vantaṉaṉō? iviyellām namatu katiyil pōkappōka viḷaṅkum kumutavalli vantataṉāl ivaṉiṭattiṟ caṭutiyil uṇṭāṉa maṉakkalakkatti avaḷ pārātapaṭi maṟittuviṭa avvaḷavu eḷitilē taṉṉiṭattil vēṟoruvakiyāṉa pārviyi varuvittuk koḷḷavāvatu aṉṟit taṉṉi virivil amitippaṭuttik koḷḷavāvatu iḷiñaṉāṉa cantiraṉ māṭṭātavaṉā yiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal iv viṭattiṟkuppōtum. cantiraṉuṭiya uṇmiyip paṟṟiyum nampakattip paṟṟiyum kumuta valliyiṉ maṉattil ēṟkaṉavē eḻunta aiyamāṉatu muṟṟum uṟutippaṭāviṭṭālum oru noṭiyil valuppaṭuvatāyiṟṟu; ākavē, mīṉāmpāḷ vāyili runtu tattaḷittuvantu taṉ ceviyiṟpaṭṭatu cantiraṉu ṭiya peyarē eṉṟum, ākiyāl tāṉ eccarikkiyōṭu irukka vēṇṭiyatu caktikaḷiṭattilēyē eṉṟum kumutavalli perumpālum uṟutikoṇṭāḷ.
kumutavalli tāṉ akappaṭṭumīṇṭa āpattukkaḷāl mikunta eccarikkiyōṭum muṉṉaṟivōṭum irukkavēṇṭum. avaciyattik kaṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ; taṉatu camutāyattiṟkuc cantiraṉ iṭamāyirukkiṟāṉ eṉpatik kāṭṭāmalirukka avaḷ tīrmāṉittuk koṇṭāḷ; ēṉeṉil, atu terintāl avaṉ mikavum eccarikkiyuṭiyaṉākit, taṉatu upāyam ētuvā yiruppiṉum ataṉik kaṇṭupiṭittaṟku nirampavum arumi yāṉa irakaciyattil mūṭivippaṉeṉa añciṉāḷ. atuvallā malum, kumutavalliyiṉ tayāḷa kuṇamāṉatu taṉṉiyotta orucīvakaṉiṭattu āṉṟakāraṇaṅkaḷ iṉṟit tappeṇṇaṅ koḷḷappukutalittaṭuttatu; atuvaṉṟiyum cantiraṉukku ecamāṉarāṉa maṉōkarar taṉakku uṟṟanaṇ parākavuṅkūṭumeṉa niṉikkalāṉāḷ. ākavē, tāṉ nīlakiri nakaril irukkum variyil antappaṇakkāra viyāpāriyiṉ vīṭṭiṉkaṇ acca miṉṟip pattiramāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutiṉāḷ; iṉṉum vēṇṭumāṉāl, cantiraṉ ūḻiyam puriyum ecamāṉariṭattil cantiraṉukkuḷḷa uṇmiyippaṟṟi avaṉ tāṉ aiyuṟavu koḷḷutaliyum eḷitilē collak kūṭumeṉṟu eṇṇiṉāḷ.
ittakiya eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉālē kumutavalli untap paṭṭu, uyarnta iṭattil toḻumpaṉāyirukkum ōr iḷiñaṉi ṭattil mikavum uyarnta niliyil uḷḷa oru perumāṭṭi evvaḷavukku mukaṅ koṭuttu iṉcoṟ pēcalāmō avvaḷavukkuc cantiraṉiṭattiṟ pēca icivu koṇṭāḷ; piṟaku avaṉ taṉ nilikkuvantu amiti peṟṟavuṭaṉē mikavum tāḻmiyōṭu taṉṉi vaṇaṅkavē, avaḷ makiḻnta kuralil, cantirā, kaṭiciyāka yāṉ pattiramāy iṅku vantucērntēṉ pārttaṉiyā? eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.
itaṟkuḷ taṉatumukatti muḻutum amitippaṭuttik koṇṭavaṉāṉa cantiraṉ kumutavalliyi nōkkip, perumāṭṭi, tāṅkaḷ ippaṭic colvataṟkuk kuṟippāṉa kāraṇam ētum illiyeṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē, periyavāyiliṉ kīḻt tām tiruppi nuḻi vitta kutiriyiṉiṉṟum avvammiyār iṟaṅkutaṟku utavi ceytāṉ.
cila ciṟiya iṭarkaḷi vaḻiyil aṉupavittēṉ eṉṟālum kaṭantupōṉa avikaḷi ippōtu niṉippatiṟ payaṉilli eṉṟu kumutavalli moḻintāḷ.
eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam viṭipakarukiyilēyē kumutavalli taṉatu mukkāṭṭi appuṟam eṭuttuviṭṭuc cantiraṉ mukattik kaḷḷamāy orupārvi pārttāḷ; āṉālum avaḷ taṉatu maṉattilirukkum aiyatti uṟutippaṭuttattakkatu ētum avaḷatu pārviyil tōṉṟavilli. apperiya vāyiliṉ uḷḷē irupuṟattum irunta vīṭṭuvācalkaḷiṉ uḷḷiruntu āṇum peṇṇumākiya ūḻiyakkārar palar veḷiyē vantaṉar; cantiraṉō avviḷiyakaṅkiyum avaḷtaṉ tōḻimārum uḷḷēpukumpaṭi tāṉ mariyāti yōṭum appāl vilaki niṉṟāṉ. avvīṭṭiṉ ūḻiyak kārarkkut taliviyeṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭa oruvayatumikka peṇpiḷḷi, kumutavalliyi varavēṟka muṟpaṭṭuvantāḷ. vantu, perumāṭṭi eṉ talivar illattiṟku uṅkaḷ varavu naṉṟākuka! eṉṟu moḻintāḷ.
nākanāṭṭaraci takutiyāṉa iṉcoṟkaḷāl maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. piṟaku akaṉṟa paṭikkaṭṭu kaḷiṉvaḻiyē ēṟi mikavum aḻakāka oḻuṅku paṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷa aṟikaḷiṉ varicikku avvammiyāl aḻittukkoṇṭu pōy viṭap paṭṭāḷ.
inta aṟikaḷ tāṅkaḷ iruppataṟkākavē cittañ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. celvattiṟ ciṟantavarum virun tōmpum iyalpiṉarumāṉa eṉ talivar maṉōkar avacara kāriyattiṉ poruṭṭu iraṇṭoru nāḷikku iṅkē irukkakkūṭātavarāyiṉār: āyiṉum, avar veḷiyē pōkum pōtu, tāṅkaḷ itaṉit taṅkaḷ contavīṭākappāvittu vēṇṭiyavellām ceytukoḷḷumpaṭi kaṟpittup pōṉār. eṉṟu ammutiyōḷ kūṟiṉāḷ.
kumutavalli virivil maṟinta ēmāṟṟap pārviyōṭu arumiyuḷḷa maṉōkar iṅkillāmi kēṭṭu varuntu kiṉṟēṉ; eṉṟālum, avar eṉ poruṭṭu ivvaḷavu aṉpukāṭṭiṉamikkum, avar colliyapaṭi naṭattum eṉatu aṉpāṉa taṉmikkum yāṉ naṉṟi pārāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
perumāṭṭi, taṅkaḷ viruppam ciṟitāyiruntālum ataṉi muṉṉarē terintukoḷḷuvatum, taṅkaḷ kaṭṭaḷi kaḷi niṟivēṟṟi vippatum eṉakku mikunta makiḻcciyit taruvaṉavākum. itu, tāṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉkaṇ mēṉmi yuṭaṉ vāḻpavarkaḷeṉṟu māṭcimiporuntiya entalivar coṉṉataṉāl māttiramaṉṟu, taṅkaḷ iḷamiyum aḻakum taṉṉi neruṅkappeṟṟavar ellārkkum taṅkaḷiṭattu iyaṟkiyākavē paṟṟuṇṭākumpaṭi ceyvataṉālēyām. taṅkaḷ pāṅkimārē taṅkaḷuṭaṉ irukka vēṇṭumō? aṉṟi ivvīṭṭiluḷḷavarkaḷum avarkaḷuṭaṉ cērntirukka vēṇṭumō? ataṉi aruḷicceyyuṅkaḷ. eṉṟu am mutiyavar kēṭṭāḷ.
eṉṉuṭiya tōḻippeṇkaḷē eppōtum eṉpakkat tilirukkavēṇṭum; uṉṉuṭiya talivar varumvariyil iyaṉṟa maṭṭum yāṅkaḷ taṉittirukkavēṇṭum; itutāṉ eṉmaṉattiṟku icintatu. eṉṟa kumutavalli ataṟku etirmoḻi kūṟiṉāḷ.
mj‰F m«KânahŸ “m¥gona ahf£L«, bgUkh£o, ïªj khËifÆ‹ Kj‹ikahd thÆY¡F¢ bršY« go¡f£il jh§fŸ K‹dnk bjÇªâU¡»Ö®fŸ.; ākavē, tāṅkaḷ nakarattiṉuḷḷa naṭantucella eṇṇap paṭṭāḷ, veḷiyē pōvataṟkuriya vaḻiyi nīṅkaḷ aṟivīr kaḷ. inta variciyiṉ kaṭiciyiluḷḷa atō anta aṟiyilē, kīḻuḷḷa tōṭṭattiṟkuc cellum maṟivāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukku nuḻivāyil irukkiṟatu; antat tōṭṭat tilē, perumāṭṭi, piṟarāl uṟṟuppārkkappaṭum accam iṉṟi enta nērattilum tāṅkaḷ ulāvalām. antat tōṭṭattiliruntuṅkūṭa nakarattiṟkuppōka oruvāyiliruk kiṟatu; anta maṟivāṉa vācaṟ kataviṉ tiṟavukōl yāṉ caṟṟu muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa aṟiyiṉuḷ irukkiṉṟatu. cila nāḷ tāṅkaḷ taṅka vēṇṭiyiruppatum, tāṅkaḷ irukkum variyil kūṭiyamaṭṭum atu taṅkaḷukku icivākavum iṉitākavum ceyyappaṭavēṇṭiyatumāṉa ivviṭattiluḷḷa ēṟpāṭu kaḷiyum nilimikaḷiyum tāṅkaḷ uṭaṉē terintu koḷḷum poruṭṭākavē ivikaḷiyellām nāṉ collalāṉēṉ eṉṟu iyampiṉāḷ.
avaḷkāṭṭiya aṉpiṉ palavakikaḷukkākavum kumutavalli ammutiyōḷukku naṉṟiyaṟital kūṟiṉāḷ; ataṉpiṉ ammutiyōḷ avarkaḷi viṭṭuppōyiṉāḷ.
kumutavalli mutaṉmutal taṉ aṟikaḷippōyt tērntu pārttāḷ. avvariciyil nāṉkaṟikaḷ iruntaṉa - mutaliluḷḷatu collāṭumiṭam, ataṉiyaṭutta iraṇṭu paṭukkiyaṟikaḷ, kaṭiciyiluḷḷa kuḷiyalukkum uṭuttik koḷvataṟkum takutiyākkap paṭṭatākum. aṅkirunta taṭṭu muṭṭukaḷellām viliyuyarntaṉavāyum nērttiyāyum iruntaṉa; potuvāṉa taḷavāṭaṅkaḷuṅkūṭac celavēṟap peṟṟaṉavāyum, vilimikap peṟṟaṉavāyum iruntaṉa. ippōtu taṉatu varukiyāl malināṭṭu viyāpāriyiṉ māḷikikkup perumiyiṉi uṇṭu paṇṇum pukaḻpeṟṟa aḻakiya naṅkiyiṉ vacatikku vēṇṭuvatoṉṟum kuṟivu paṭavilli. kumutavalli uṭaṉē nīrāṭu vāḷāṉāḷ; palarāl neṭuvaḻi tªjj‰F.p piṟaku mukkiyamāy iṉṟikkup puḻutimikka pāṭṭi neṭuka vanta kaḷippāṉa payaṇattiṉ piṉṉar ammuḻukku mikavum iḷippāṟṟu vatāyiruntatu. avaḷ taṉ ṟōḻimārōṭum irukkum aṟikkut tirumpi vantatum, nērttiyāṉa uṇavu koṇṭu vantu mēci mēṟparappi vikkap paṭṭiruntatu; iṉṉum ētēṉum vēṇṭi yiruntāl ataṉikkoṇṭu varuvōri aḻikkuṅ karuviyāka oruciṟiya veḷḷi maṇiyoṉṟu kikkarukē ammēcimēl iruntatu. cuvipala vāynta tiṟamāṉa melliya uṇavupporuḷkaḷ mika āḻnta aṟivōṭu aṭuttaṭuttu vikkappaṭṭirunta miyālum, vāyūṟacceyyavum paḻavaki kaḷōṭum, iḷip pāṟṟu mikkum pāṉakaṅkaḷōṭum uṇaviṟkuriya ovvoṉṟum mikunta karuttōṭum amikkap paṭṭiruntamiyālum cuviyi mikak karutippārppavarkkum kuṟi collattakkatu ētumēyilli. mēciyaṇṭi taṉṉōṭu kūṭa irukkumpaṭi kumutavalli taṉṟōḻimārkkum kaṟpittāḷ; avarkaḷ tamakketirilirunta melliya tiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷi eṭuttu aruntiṉārkaḷ. maṉōkarar ūril illāta viṣayam māttiram kalavāviṭṭāl, tāṉ cēra vēṇṭiya iṭattiṟkup pattiramāy vantu cērnta tippaṟṟiyum, taṉatumōtiram taṉṉiṭam tirumpavantu porunti yatip paṟṟiyum kumutavalli muḻutum maṉaamiti peṟṟi ruppaḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, taṉakkuriya nākanāṭṭiliruntu mali nāṭṭiṉ talinakarākiya nīlakirikku ayarcciyiṉiyum apāyattiṉiyum tarum nīṇṭa pirayāṇattit tāṉ ceyyum paṭi tūṇṭap paṭṭatu ētukkākaveṉṟu terintukoḷḷa avaḷ iyal pākavē āvaluṟṟuk kavaṉṟāḷ. mēlum, cila nāḻiki nēraṅkaḷukkumuṉṉē nērnta allalkaḷi niṉikkavē avaḷ tuyaram aṭiyātirukkakkūṭa villi; iṉṉum attaṉi iḷamiyum aḻakum aṟivum vāynta nīla lōcaṉaṉ avaḷtāṉ niṉittukkoṇṭapaṭi avvaḷavu peruṅkuṟṟam uṭiyavaṉāṉaritat tuyarattōṭum uṇṇi varuntiṉāḷ.
cāppāṭu muṭintatum, kumutavalli vacatikkākac ceyta mikkappaṭṭa palavaki ēṟpāṭukaḷum avaḷukku icivāka iruntaṉavāveṉṟu terintukoḷḷum poruṭṭu av vīṭṭukkāra mutiyōḷ avarkaḷ muṉṉēvantāḷ; avarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ iciviṉi maṉamāra makiḻnturippavē avvammi pōyviṭṭāḷ. avvaṟi varicikaḷiṉ veḷik katavukaḷ uṭaṉē tāḻiṭappaṭṭaṉa; kumutavalliyum avaḷ pāṅkimārum tuyilkoḷḷap pōyiṉar.
taṭiyiṉṟi avarkaḷ tiḷitta tuyilāṉatu iṉitāki ayarvu tīrppatāyiṟṟu; viṭiyaṟkālattil avarkaḷ uṟakkam nīṅki eḻunta pōtu, iṭarmikunta avvaḻippayaṇattiṉ piṟaku pātukāval aminta pukaliṭam tamakku vāyttatip paṟṟit tamakkuḷ makiḻntu pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ. veḷikkatavukaḷ tiṟakkappaṭṭavuṭaṉē vīṟṟirukkum nērttiyāṉa ciṟṟuṇavu kaḷipparappiṉar; cāppāṭu muṭintavuṭaṉē kumutavalli nallakāṟṟu vāṅkuvataṟkākat tōṭṭattiṉuḷ iṟaṅkak karutiṉāḷ. ammutiyōḷ colliyapaṭiyē, maṟivāṉa oru paṭikkaṭṭuk kuḷiyaṟiyiliruntu kīḻēyuḷḷa akaṉṟa pūntōṭṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟatu; appūntōṭṭattiṉ orupakka ōrattil carakkaṟikaḷum māḷikiyuñ cērnta nīṇṭa oru kaṭṭiṭavariciyum, maṟṟa mūṉṟu pakka ōraṅkaḷilum uyarnta cuvar kaḷum iruntaṉa. malarppāttikaḷum, pacuṅkoṭipaṭarnta niḻaluḷḷa vāyilkaḷum, koṭippantarkaḷum, nīruṟṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉavāka attōṭṭam aḻakākap paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭirun tatu; kaṭikkōṭi yiluḷḷa cuvarilē avvammiyāṟ kuṟippik kappaṭṭatum nakarattil oru teruvukku vaḻiyāyuḷḷatumāṉa maṟinta nuḻivāyiloṉṟu iruntatu. mutaliltām vantu cērntapōtu teruppakkattēyuḷḷa pakutiyimāttiram pārttu aviciṟṟaḷaviṉavāka uṟutippaṭāmal matittirunta kumutavalliyum avaḷtōḻikaḷum ippōtu maṉōkarariṉ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷuṭiya pēraḷaviṉikkaṇṭu peritum viyappuṟṟār kaḷ. ippōtu avarkaḷ akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmiyiyum cāḷaraṅkaḷiṉ aṭukkukaḷiyum kaṇṭu  apperiya māḷikiyiṉ perumpakuti celvattiṟciṟanta maṉōkarar tammuṭiya carakkukaḷi vittirukkum paṇṭaka aṟikaḷi uṭiyatāku meṉṟu collakkūṭiya tāyiruntatu.
cila nāḻikinēram kumutavalliyum avaḷ pāṅkimārum at tōṭṭattil taṅkiyiruntaṉar; aḻiyāmal varuvār evarum aṅkillāmiyāl, nākanāṭṭiṉkaṇ kumutavalliyiṉ māḷikiyi aṭuttuḷḷa iṉpa iḷaṅkākkaḷil iruppatu pōla avarkaḷ oruciṟitum accamum taṭiyumiṉṟi yirukkap peṟṟataṉi uṇarntārkaḷ. tirumpavum avarkaḷ vīṭṭiṉuṭ pukuntapōtu, maṟupaṭiyum mēcimēl uṇavu koṇṭuvantu vikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ammutiyōḷ taṉakketirē varak kaṇṭatum kumutavalli maṉōkarar tirumpi vantaṉarā eṉṟu viṉaviṉāḷ. avvammi avar varavilliyeṉṟu viṭi pakarntu, āyiṉum avar nāḷikku nīlakiri varuvatāka uṟutimoḻipukaṉṟu ceyti aṉuppiyirukkiṟār eṉṟurit tāḷ. tāṉ nākanāṭṭiliruntu aḻikkappaṭṭataṉ kāraṇat tippaṟṟiya aiyuṟavum āvalum virivil niṟivēṟum eṉṟeṇṇik kumutavalli icceytiyāl maṉa amiti peṟṟāḷ.
māliyil aintunāḻiki āyirukkum - kumutavalli maṟupaṭiyum taṉṟōḻimārōṭu tōṭṭattil ulaviyiruntu vīṭṭukkuḷ tirumpivantāḷ - accamayattil vīṟṟirukkum aṟiyil ammutiyōḷ vantu,
perumāṭṭi, tāṅkaḷ maṉam uvantāl orupeṇ piḷḷi taṅkaḷik kāṇavirumpukiṟāḷ. eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
entappeṇpiḷḷi? nīlakiriyil inta illattiṟkup puṟattē eṉakkup paḻakkamuḷḷavarkaḷ evarum illiyē. eṉṟu nākanāṭṭaraci kēṭṭaṉaḷ.
perumāṭṭi, tikaḻkali eṉṉum peyarimoḻintāl taṉṉip pārppataṟku nīṅkaḷ aiyuṟamāṭṭīrkaḷ eṉṟu avaḷ collukiṟāḷ. eṉṟu ammutiyōḷ viṭikūṟiṉāḷ.
ā, tikaḻkaliyā! eṉṟu viyantu kūṟik kumutavalli oru noṭiyil muṉṉāḷ naṭanta iṭariṟ campantamuṟṟa aṟivōḷi niṉivukūrntu, ām - nāṉ avaḷip pārkkiṟēṉ. avaḷi varaviṭu. eṉa moḻintāḷ.
ammutiyavaḷ aṅṅaṉamēpōyc ciṟitunērattil tikaḻkaliyik kumutavalliyiṉ etirē aḻittu vantāḷ. avvaṟivōḷ muntiyanāḷil uṭuttiyiruntapaṭiyē irun taṉaḷ; āṉāl iccamayattil avaḷkiyil maruntukaḷum uṟi maruntukaḷum aṭaṅkiya peṭṭi koṇṭuvaravilli. avaḷtāṉ aṇintirunta mukkāṭṭip piṉṉē vāṅkiyatum avaḷ mukattil oru tuyarkkuṟipput tōṉṟiyatu; avaḷ mīṉāmpāḷippaṟṟip pēcavē vantaṉaḷeṉṟu kumutavalli iyaṟkiyāka niṉintāḷ. tikaḻkalimikunta paṇivōṭum nam taliviyi vāḻtti, aḻakiya naṅkiyē, tāṅkaḷ ittaṉi karukkil eṉṉikkāṇa niṉittirukkamāṭṭīrkaḷ. eṉṟu pukaṉṟāḷ.
eṉṉa kāriyamāy iṅkē vantāy? eṉṉi enta iṭattiṟ kāṇpateṉpati nī eṅṅaṉam terintu koṇṭāy? eṉṟu nākanāṭṭaraci viṉaviṉāḷ.
iraṇṭāvatukēṭṭa kēḷviyi mella naḻuvaviṭṭup, perumāṭṭi, puṉitamāṉa puṇṇiya kāriyattiṉporuṭṭu yāṉ vantirukkiṟēṉ. yāṉ puttamatattic cērntaṉavāyiṉum, maliya nāṭṭārkkuriya koḷkikaḷil eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭu illiyāyiṉum, maliyanāṭṭārkkuriya avikaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa pētimiyiṉ pāṉmiyeṉa eṉakkut teṉpaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum ellāc camayakkoḷkikaḷiṉum eṉakkup pōtumāṉa naṉkumatippu irukkiṉṟatu; ataṉālēyē intattaṭavi taṅkaḷukkut tūtākavarumpaṭi tūṇṭap paṭṭēṉ. eṉṟu tikaḻkali viṭikūṟiṉāḷ.
kumutavalli maṟiporuḷāṉa iccoṟkaḷikkēṭṭu viyappeytiṉavaḷāy, nī colluvataṉ karuttuyātu tikaḻkali? evariṭattiruntu tūtāka varukiṉṟaṉi!? eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ.
iṟantavariṭamiruntu tūtāy varukiṉṟēṉ! eṉṟu verukkoḷḷattakka viṭipakarntāḷ.
kumutavalliyiṉ mukam veḷiṟiṉatu - ēṉeṉil, ammaṟumoḻiyil accamum tikilum koḷḷattakkatu ētō iruntatu; āṉālum, ataṟku uruvakapporuḷ vēṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉa viṉāviṟkarutiṉavaḷāyt, tikaḻkali, eṉṉiṭattil oṭṭivaruvatu uruvakattilāvatu pēcātirukkum paṭi uṉṉikkeñcik kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ; iyaṉṟa maṭṭum nī colla vēṇṭiyatit teḷivāka viḷakkiccol eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
yāṉ iṟantavarkaḷiṭamiruntu nērēvarāviṭṭālum eṉṟu tirumpavun toṭarntu iṟantavarkaḷ poruṭṭākayāka vantirukkiṟēṉ. ataṉālyāṉ iṟantavarkaḷiṭamiruntu uṇmiyāka vanta tūtiyē uyirōṭiruppavar tamatu pētimi yāl iṟantavarkaḷmēl ēṟṟiccollum āvikkuk kīḻp paṭintu yāṉ tūtāka vantirukkiṟēṉ! eṉṟu urittut, taṉ kaṇkaḷik kumutavalli mēl vittu ūṭuruvappārttuk kūṟuvāḷ: perumāṭṭi, kaḷvar talivaṉāṉa nallāṉukku nalviṉiyaṟṟa maṉiviyāy iṟantupōṉa mīṉāmpāḷ taṅkaḷukku pakivarāṉa cilarōṭu kaṭṭumāṉamāyc cērntu cūḻcci ceytāḷ; avaṉ iṟakkuntaṟuvāyil avaḷatu taliyit taṅkaḷ mārpil aṇittukkoḷḷumpaṭi puttar taṅkaḷi avaḷ cellumvaḻiyiṟ celuttiṉa atē camayattil avaḷ uṅkaḷukku oru pollāṅku iḻikkak karut tuṟṟiruntāḷ. avaḷuṭiya ēvaṟkārikaḷ vāyiliruntu yāṉ palavaṟṟik kēṭṭuṇarntēṉ.
nalviṉiyaṟṟa mīṉāmpāḷ eṉṉiṭattiṟ tīya eṇṇaṅ kaḷ vittiruntaṉaḷāyiṉ yāṉ avaḷi maṉṉittu viṭṭē ṉē! ō! eṉ neñcāra yāṉ avaḷi maṉṉittuviṭṭēṉē! eṉṟu kumutavalli iṭippukuntu kūṟiḷāḷ.
perumāṭṭi, nīṅkaḷ avaḷōṭu taṉimiyāka viṭap paṭṭa pōtu, yāṉum avaḷ tōḻimār iruvarum ciṟitu cēymiyiṟ pōyiruntapōtu, nīṅkaḷ avaḷmēṟ kuṉintirunta vakiyiṉālum uṅkaḷ pārviyiṉālum uṅkaḷ payilkaḷi ṉālum uṅkaḷ mukatti avaḷ ēṟiṭṭu nōkkiyataṉālum tāṅkaḷ avaḷi maṉṉittati uṟutippaṭuttik koṇṭirun tīrkaḷeṉṟu yāṉ tīrmāṉañ ceyyakkūṭiyatāyiruntatu. intakkāraṇam paṟṟiyē yāṉ ippōtu iṅkirukkiṉṟēṉ. intakkāraṇam paṟṟiyē taṅkaḷukkuriya iṉattavarkaḷum avarkaḷukku iṉamāṉavarkaḷum eṇṇukiṟapaṭi anta maṉṉippip piṉṉum uṟutippaṭuttumpaṭi taṅkaḷi āṇiyiṭṭuk kēṭṭukkoḷḷappōkiṟēṉ. perumāṭṭi, iṟantupōkum oruvarukkuc colliya maṉṉippu iṟantu pōṉa avaratu piṇattiṉ pakkattēyiruntu mēlum mikunta paricuttattōṭum payapaktiyōṭum tirumpavum vaṟpuṟuttap paṭṭālallāmal atu payaṉpaṭuvatilli yeṉṉum oru nampikki uṅkaḷ kāṭṭil eṅkaḷ conta nāka nāṭṭil irukkiṟataṉṟō? eṉṟu tikaḻkali maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāḷ.
tikaḻkali ippēcciṉ kaṭicippākattic colliya pōtu kumutavalliyiṉ mukam vaṇakkamum payapattikkuṟippum uṭiyatāyiṟṟu; iccoṟkaḷip payapaktiyōṭu cērnta āvaluṭaṉ uṟṟuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭirunta cuntarāmpāḷum, mīṉāmpāḷum tammuṭiya talivi ippōtu taṉ mukattil kāṭṭiya kuṟippip pōlavē tamakkuḷḷuṅ kāṭṭik koṇṭārkaḷ.
taṉatu camayakkalvikaḷiṉ iṭiyē ittakiya ōr āḻnta nampikkiyuṭiyavaḷāṉa kumutavalli ippōtu tāṉum ataṉ vayappaṭṭavaḷāki melliya kuralil, mēṟkaṇavāy malip pakkattiluḷḷavar attaṉipeyarkkum meyyāka an nampikki uṇṭu tāṉ - ākavē, tikaḻkali, ippōtu eṇṇik koṇṭiruppatiṉṉateṉa oru ciṟituṇarkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟu moḻintār.
ataṉittoṭarntu tikaḻkali kūṟuvāḷ. nalviṉi yaṟṟa mīṉāmpāḷ viṭṭuppōṉa iraṇṭu ēvaṟkārikaḷāṉa malināṭṭup paṇippeṇṇum, kāppirimātum tam aṉpuḷḷa taliviyiṉ piṇatti nīlakirikku eṭuttu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ; iṟantupōkun taṟuvāyil uyirōṭirunta peṇmakaḷiṉ arukilirunta nīṅkaḷ colliya maṉṉippu uṟutimoḻiyi maṟupaṭiyum piṇattiṉ pakkattil vantu collumpaṭi taṅkaḷiṭam pōyt taṉṉik keñcik kēṭṭukkoḷḷumāṟu eṉṉi vēṇṭiṉavaḷ anta malināṭṭup paṇippeṇṇē tāṉ. etaṉ poruṭṭut taṅkaḷ muṉṉiliyil yāṉ vantirukkiṟēṉō antap putumiyāṉa nampikki evvaḷavu tūram cellakkūṭumeṉpatip, perumāṭṭi eṉṉiviṭat tāṅkaḷ naṉṟāy aṟivīrkaḷ. iṟantupōṉa mīṉāmpāḷ taṅkaḷukku evvaḷavu tīṅkiḻittālum allatu iḻikka niṉittiruntālum avaṟṟiṟkākat tāṅkaḷ moḻinta maṉṉippu moḻikaḷ, uyiraṟṟa avaḷ piṇattarukēyiruntu payapaktiyōṭu tirumpavuñ collappaṭṭāl allāmal avaḷuyirkku amitiyit tarappōta māṭṭāvākum eṉṟu eṉakkut terivikkappaṭṭatu.
kumutavalli āḻnta cintippāḷāṉāḷ - avaḷ tōḻimār iruvarum tam iḷiya taliviyiṉ mukatti tikilōṭum aiyuṟavōṭum nōkkik koṇṭiruntaṉar.
kaṭiciyākak kumutavalli tikaḻkaliyiṉ mukatti ēṟiṭṭup pārttuk kaḷvar talivaṉāṉa nallāṉiṉ nalviṉiyaṟṟa maṉiyāḷatu piṇam eṅkē kiṭakkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
perumāṭṭi, iṅkiruntu neṭuntūramilli. eṉṟu maṟumoḻi tantu, atō appakkamāy mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku terukkaḷukku appāltāṉ eṉṟu avvaṟivōḷ avvīṭṭiṉ piṉpakutiyi nōkkic cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉāḷ.
maṟupaṭiyum kumutavalli cintippāḷāṉāḷ; maṟupaṭiyum avaḷ pāṅkimāriṉ kaṇkaḷ āvalkoṇṭa aiyuṟavōṭum avaḷmēṟ patintaṉa.
neṭunēram pēcāmalirunta piṟaku kumutavalli tirumpavum vāyittiṟantu, nī kēṭṭukkoṇṭapaṭi naṭappataṟku eṉṉuḷ ḷam eṉṉittūṇṭukiṉṟatu. nī pēcikkoṇṭirunta nampik kiyil uṇmiyirukkiṟateṉṟu yāṉ meyyākavē kaṟpikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟurittup piṉṉum anta tayāḷa kuṇam aminta iḷiya naṅki, ō! iṟantupōṉa avvuyirkku amitiyit tarattakkatetaṉiyum yāṉ muḻu tiruppattōṭu ceyvēṉ! āṉālum, iḵtu eṉṉip piṭippataṟku viritta valiyaṉṟeṉṟu yāṉmaṉaṅ koḷvateppaṭi? yām iṅṅaṉam viṭṭuc colvataṟkākat tikaḻkali, eṉmēl kuṟṟañ collutal kūṭātu. tīya eṇṇañ ciṟitum illā malirukkak kūṭiya oruvarmēl yāṉ iṅṅaṉam aiyuṟutal paṟṟi eṉmēṟ kuṟṟañ collutal kūṭātu. uṉṉuṭiya nōkkam nallatākavē yirukkalām - yāṉum aḵtu appaṭittāṉ eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ - eṉṟālum nī aṟiyāmalum uṉatu kaṇcāṭi illāmalum piṟar kikkaruviyākkap paṭṭirukkalām?
atukēṭṭa tikaḻkali oṭukkavaṇakkam mikka kuralil peritum eṇmitōṉṟak kūṟuvāḷ: perumāṭṭi yāṉ puṉitamāka niṉittavi yellām aṟiyap puttarum  avar cāraṇarum aṟiya āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. yāṉ uṅkaḷukkup poyyaṟuvēṉāṉāl iṟantupōṉa em peṟṟōr piḻittēṉāvēṉ ātalāl avarkaḷ aṟiya, illātal yāṉāvatu maṟṟiyōrāvatu uṅkaḷiṭattu oru ciṟitun tīya eṇṇam vittilēm eṉṟu āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ ceḻuṅkūntalil oru mayirēṉun tīṅkuṟa yāṉ kāṇpēṉ allēṉ! ēḻi aṟivāṭṭiyiṭam nīṅkaḷ aṉpōṭu pēciṉīrkaḷaṉṟō - alintu tiriyum tikaḻ kaliyi nīṅkaḷ puṉcirippukaḷāl nīṅkaḷ ūkkap paṭuttiṉīr kaḷaṉṟō - tāṉē perumi pārāṭṭippēciya avaḷatu tiṟami tavaṟip pōṉa accamayattil avaḷit tāṅkaḷ aruvaruttut taḷḷa vēṇṭuvatāyirukka, appōtum avaḷukkut taṅkaḷ poṟ paṇam koṭuttīrkaḷaṉṟō, atuvaṉṟiyum, peru māṭṭi, uṅkaḷukkut tītuceyyak kaṉavil eṇṇuvatāyiṉum atu vāṉulakattuḷḷa oru kantaruva mātiṉōṭu caṇṭi yiṭuta liyē okkum:!
tikaḻkaliyiṉ pārviyilum avaḷ naṭantukoṇṭa variyilum kaḷaṅkiṉmi kāṇappaṭṭatu; ataṉāl avaḷ pēciya coṟkaḷum antaṉmi yuṭiyavākavē yiruntaṉa; kumutavalli maṟupaṭiyum ciṟitu nēram eṇṇamiṭṭuk koṇṭiruntamiyiṉ, taṉ irukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu, yāṉ uṉṉuṭaṉē varukiṟēṉ. eṉṟu moḻintāḷ.
perumāṭṭi - aṉpiṟ ciṟanta perumāṭṭi! eṉṟu cuntarāmpāḷ collikkoṇṭē taṉ taliviyiṉ kālaṭi kaḷil vīḻntu, koṭumpulivāḻuṅ kukikkuḷ tāṅkaḷ cellā tirukkumpaṭi taṅkaḷik keñcik kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ! eṉṟu moḻintāḷ.
aṅṅaṉamē tuṉpuṟṟut tikilkoṇṭa ñāṉāmpāḷum maṇṭikkāliṭṭup paricuttamāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷum cāṉṟākap perumāṭṭi tāṅkaḷ iṅkēyēyirukkumpaṭi vēṇṭik koḷkiṉṟēṉ! alintu tiriyum oru puttap peṇpiḷḷi taṉakku uṭaṉpāṭillāta civacamayak koḷ kik kuriya kāriyaṅkaḷil attaṉi pirayāci yeṭuttuk koḷvaḷeṉṟu tāṅkaḷ niṉikkiṟīrkaḷā- eṉṟu kūṟukiyil,
tikaḻkali naṭuvē pukuntu, alintu tiriyum ēḻip puttap peṇ taṉ matatti iḻittuppēcuvōr koḷki kaḷiyum naṉku matikkiṉṟāḷ. eṉṟu ciṉavāmal amiti yōṭum tuyarattōṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
kārikiyīr eḻuntiruṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ eḻuntiṭumpaṭi nāṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ! eṉ poruṭṭu nīṅkaḷ kavali yuṟṟataṟkāka nāṉ naṉṟiyaṟitaluṭiyavaḷā yirukkiṉṟēṉ; eṉṟālum yāṉ uṟuticeytuviṭṭēṉ - eṉ viruppattiṟkuk kuṟukkuc colla lākātu, eṉṟu colluṅ kumutavalli ētō oṉṟic caṭutiyil niṉittavaḷāyt tikaḻkaliyiṉ pakkamāyttirumpi, ā! taṟceyalākavēṉum eṉ kaṇ etirppaṭum paṭi- eṉakkūṟappōkiyil, allaṟivōḷ aṭakka voṭukkattōṭum aḻuttamāy pakaruvāḷ: peru māṭṭi,yāṉ colvati uṟṟukkēḷuṅkaḷ! yāṉ taṅkaḷi aḻittup pōkak karutiyirukkum iṭattil eṉ vāyi liruntallāmal vēṟevar vāyiliruntum ōr eḻuttuk kūṭa uṅkaḷi nōkkip pēcappaṭamāṭṭātu! eṉ kiyit tavira vēṟevar kiyum uṅkaḷ mēṟpaṭamāṭṭātu! yāṉ kūṟum ik kaṭṭurikaḷukkuk kutamacākkiyariyum avar cāraṇariyum maṟupaṭiym cāṉṟāka vikkiṉṟēṉ!
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ yāṉ uṉṉuṭaṉ varukiṟēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟiya kumutavalli ataṉpiṉ pāṅkimāri nōkki mātarkaḷ nīṅkaḷ eṉṉuṭaṉ varavēṇṭām eṉṟu kēṭṭuk koḷḷu kiṟēṉ- eṉa moḻikiyil,
cuntarāmpāḷ, ām, perumāṭṭi, nāṅkaḷ taṅkaḷōṭu celvōm! eṅkaḷukkākavaṉṟu nāṅkaḷ accamuṟuvatu, eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
āṇṭavaṉ taṭiceyvāṉāka! ētēṉum iṭar nērumāyiṉ eṅkaḷ aṉpuḷḷa perumāṭṭiyōṭu nāṅkaḷum ataṉic cērntu aṉupavippōm! eṉṟu ñāṉāmpāḷ urittāḷ.
taṅkaḷukku viruppamāṉāl, perumāṭṭi, ivarkaḷ uṅkaḷōṭu varalām eṉṟu tikaḻkali moḻintāḷ.
avvaṟivōḷiṉ uṇmi yoḻukkatti meyp pittaṟku itu maṟṟumoru cāṉṟākat tōṉṟiyatu; āyiṉum taṉtōḻimāril oruttiyiyē taṉṉuṭaṉ aḻittuk koṇṭu cellak kumutavalli tīrmāṉittāḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, uṇmiyilē ētēṉum iraṇṭakamāṉa ēṟpāṭu ceyyap paṭṭirukkumāṉāl, taṉ taliviyum maṟṟittōḻiyum ēmāṟṟik koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭa vakikaḷi virittuk koḷvataṟku orutti taṉakkuppiṉ iṅkēyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu tīrmāṉañceytāḷ.
ataṟku iṇaṅkavē avaḷ, cuntarāmpāḷ, nī eṉṉōṭu vā; ñāṉāmpāḷ, nī nāṅkaḷ tirumpivarumaḷavum iṅkēyē taṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. ivviṭutiyiṉiṉṟum itaṉiccērnta vīṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum nāṅkaḷ yārum aṟiyāmaṟpōy varuvataṟku nallakālamāy eṉakku vaḻikiṭittirukkiṟatu. ñāṉāmpāḷ, nī inta aṟiyil iru. yāṉ illātapoḻutu ivvīṭṭu mutiyōḷ iṅku vantāl yāṉ ciṟitu nērattiṟku eṉ aṟiyil taṉiyē yirukkap pōṉēṉ eṉṟu col - cuntarāmpāḷ, eṉṉōṭu vā! tikaḻkali yāṉ āyatta māyirukkiṟēṉ! eṉap pakarntāḷ.
ñāṉāmpāḷ, taṉ iḷiya taliviyiṉ kiyip piṭittut taṉ viḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum nīr oḻuka ataṉmēl muttam vittāḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, tāṉ kumutavalliyi iṉi eṉṟuṅ kāṇātavāṟu pirintu pōvatākavē nampikkiyum aṉpum vāynta appeṇ eṇṇiṉāḷ.
eṉ mātē, maṭamiyāl varuntātē. eṉṟu nāka nāṭṭaraci kūṟiṉāḷ; taṉ coṟkaḷavaḷik kaṭintu koḷvatu pōṟ kāṇappaṭṭālum, aṉpōṭum viruppattōṭum taṉ kiyāl avaḷatu kiyippiṭittu aṇittuk koṇṭāḷ; tāṉ mikka maṉattiṇmi yōṭiruntum ivaḷ taṉ kaṇkaḷi ṉiṉṟum nīr tatumpāmal taṭi ceyyak kūṭavilli.
tikaḻkali piṉṉēvara ivaḷ avvaṟiyiviṭṭu virintu pōyiṉāḷ; cuntarāmpāḷō taṉṉuṭaṉ ṟōḻiyākiya ñāṉāmpāḷit taḻuvikkoḷḷum poruṭṭu oru kaṇap poḻutu piṉṉē nīṭittu niṉṟāḷ.
tikaḻkaliyic cila nimiṣanēram aṅkuḷḷa paṭukki yaṟikaḷil oṉṟil irukkumpaṭi kaṟpittuviṭṭuk, kumutavalli kuḷiyalaṟikkuṭ ceṉṟāḷ. aṅkē tāṉ aṇintirunta aṇikkalaṉkaḷiyellām kaḻaṟṟi ōr alamāriyuḷ pēṇivittāḷ. vilimatittaṟkariya ammantira mōtirat tiyum taṉatu paṇap piyiyum avaṟṟōṭu cērttu aṅkē vikka avaḷ maṟantu viṭavilli; ēṉeṉṟāl, koḷḷik kārariṉ āciyi eḻupputaṟkuriya aṇikala ṉāvatu nāṇaya māvatu ciṟitut taṉṉiṭattil irukkalākā teṉṟu avaḷ tīrmāṉittāḷ. avaḷ alamāriyi karuttāyp pūṭṭat tāṉ tirumpivarāviṭiṉum ciṟitu tēṭippārttāl tiṇṇamāyk kaṇṭupiṭittataṟku icinta ōr iṭattil ataṉ cāviyi vittāḷ. piṉṉar oru tuppaṭṭiyiṉāl taṉṉi mūṭik koṇṭu, tōṭṭattiṉ kaṭiciyiluḷḷa nuḻi vāyilit tiṟappataṟkuriyatum avvīṭṭu mutiyōḷ kūṟiyapaṭi am mañcaṇac cāliyiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatumāṉa cāviyik kiyiṟ paṟṟiṉavaḷāyt tāṉ puṟappaṭutaṟkuc cittamāyiruppatit tikaḻkalikkuñ cuntarāmpāḷukkuṅ kuṟippittāḷ. avvaṟi vōḷ taṉ kaṇkaḷi māttiram tiṟappākaviṭṭut, taṉ mukattiyellām caṇal nūṟṟuṇi yiṉāl mukkāṭiṭṭuk koṇṭāḷ; cuntarāmpāḷum taṉatu pōrviyiṉāl mukkāṭiṭṭuk koṇṭāḷ. ammūvarum maṟipaṭikkaṭṭiṉ vaḻiyāyt tōṭṭattiṉuḷ iṟaṅki ṉārkaḷ. virintucūḻum macaṅkal mālip poḻutiṉiṭiyē atiṟ kaṭuki naṭantārkaḷ; kaṭiciyil cuvariluḷḷa nuḻi vāyilaṇṭi vantu cērntārkaḷ. kumutavalliyiṉiṭamirunta cāviyāl ataṉit tiṟantu aṅku niṉṟum veḷip paṭṭārkaḷ; ippōtu tikaḻkali aṭuttuḷḷa terukkaḷi ṉūṭē vaḻi kāṭṭic cella lāṉāḷ.

atikāram - 13
camayac caṭaṅku

kumutavallikku uriya nāṭṭiṉ nilimiyi eṇṇip pārkkuṅkāl, avaḷ iyaṟkiyaṟivum ciṟanta kalvi yuṇarcciyum mikunta teḷivum uṭiyavaḷāyiruntāḷ, avaḷatu uḷḷam civacamaya uṇmikaḷāl āḻa niṟikkap paṭṭiruntatu. mēṟkaṇavāy malippakkaṅkaḷilē accamayat tiṉ mikac cuttamāṉa uṇmikaḷ avala nampikkikaḷ palavaṟṟōṭuṅ kalantiruntamiyāl, kumutavalliyiṉ uḷḷamum annampikki kaḷiṟ piṇippuṇṭiruntatu. ippōtu tāṉ mayakkuṟṟu naṭakkum nampikkiyippaṟṟi avaḷatu iyaṟki nallaṟivu āḻntu niṉittup pārkkumpaṭi tūṇṭappaṭu māṉāl, aḵtoru mūṭanampikkiyēyallāmaṟ piṟitilli yeṉṉum oru muṭipukku avaḷ varakkūṭum. āṉāl itaṟkumuṉ avaḷ ataṉi eṇṇippārkkumpaṭi tūṇṭappaṭavilli; ippōtō avaḷ ataṉi maṉa amitiyōṭiruntu āyntu pārkka oḻivilli. iṉṉun teḷinta aṟivuḷḷa nāṭukaḷil maṉavuram mikuntōr palar ittakiya avala nampikkikaḷukku iṇaṅkiyoḻukumāṟu pōlavē, ivaḷum itaṟku oruppaṭṭu naṭappāḷāyiṉaḷ. muntiṉa nāḷil mīṉāmpāḷippaṟṟi cērnta tuyaramāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ kumutavalliyiṉ uḷḷattil āḻntu patintaṉa. tāṉ uṇmiyil kumutavallikkuc ceyta allatu ceyya niṉitta tīṅkukaḷukkup parikāramākat tāṉ iṟakkun taṟuvāyiṟ collavēṇṭuvaṉa vellāñ colli yoḻikka, annalviṉiyillā maṅkiyiṉiṭattu avaḷ aḷavukaṭantu irakkappaṭṭāḷ. ākavē, ammaṅkiyiṉ uyir amitipeṟum poruṭṭu tāṉ ippōtu ceytaṟku mēṟkoṇṭataṉic ceyyum vakiyil itaṉippaṭippavar muṉṉarē aṟinta aḷavukkumēl, kumutavalli poḻutu nīṭṭittaliṉṟi iṇaṅkiṉāḷ. atāvatu: koḷḷikkāraṉ maṉivikku uyir irukkiyilēyē avaḷukkuc coṉṉa maṉṉippu moḻiyit tirumpavum avaḷatu piṇattaṇṭi yiliruntu colluvatē yām. inta avala nampikki oru ciṟanta karuttuppaṟṟiyē eḻuntateṉpatil aiyam iṉṟu valiya cila uṇarvukaḷiṉ vacappaṭṭa niliyil maṉṉippu moḻikaḷ collappaṭalām; taṉ pakivaṉāyiṉun taṉakkut tīṅkiḻit tōṉāyiṉum taṉ kaṇṇetirē aḻintupaṭukiyil oru noṭippoḻutu tayāḷakuṇattāl untappaṭṭum maṉṉippu moḻikaḷ collappaṭalām; eṉṟālum piṇattiṉ pakkattēyiruntu cīrtūkki vaṇakka oṭukkattōṭuñ collappaṭum maṉṉippu moḻiyil uṇmiyil mikkatum uṟutiyiṟ ciṟantatum āṉatu ētōyirukkiṉṟatu.
kumutavalli tāṉ ceytu muṭikka uṭaṉpaṭṭatum kurukkaḷ mārāl camayak koḷkiyiṟ cērttu vikkap paṭṭatumāṉa caṭaṅkiṉ nōkkamum tōṟṟamum aviyē eṉpatil aiyamiṉṟu. āyiṉum, nām muṉṉarē kūṟiyapaṭi ivviṣayattippaṟṟi eṇṇippārkkak kumutavallikku nēramilli. mutalil avaḷ taṉṉi akappaṭuttac ceyta cūḻcciyāyirukkalāmō eṉṟu māttiram niṉintāḷ. āṉāl tikaḻkaliyiṉ coṟkaḷiyum uṟutimoḻikaḷiyuṅ kēṭṭu anniṉivu tīrntavuṭaṉ, iṟantupōṉavuyir amiti peṟaṟporuṭṭut tāṉ ceyvataṟkuk kaṭamippaṭṭataṉic ceyvatu paricuttamāṉa kaṭaṉeṉṟeṇṇiṉāḷ.
ippōtu kiṭṭattaṭṭa āḷ vaḻakkam aṭaṅkiya terukkaḷiṉūṭu vaḻi kāṭṭikkoṇṭē tikaḻkali pōyi ṉāḷ. anta iḷavēṉiṟkālattiṭiyil vaḻakkamāy irup patik kāṭṭilum mikunta puḻukkam vāynta oru nāḷiṉ piṟaku ammālik kālamāṉatu iruṇṭu maṅkit tōṉṟiyatu. aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ pōykkoṇṭirukkiyil vāṉatti liruntu paḷīreṉa oru miṉṉal tōṉṟiyatu. avarkaḷ tammukatti maṟittirunta mukkāṭumāttiram illātirunta tāyiṉ aḵtu avarkaḷ kaṇkaḷik kuruṭupaṭuttiyirukkum.
anta miṉumiṉuppāṉatu ciṟitunēram avviṭatti muṟṟum oḷimayamākki, atteruviṉ irumaruṅkiṉum uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi noṭippoḻutēṉum naṉku pulappaṭak kāṭṭiyatu. atē nērattil etirppakkattē virintu pōykkoṇṭirunta oruvaṉuṭiya mukam kumutavalli pārvikkut teṉpaṭṭatu. clnd mtŸ â»šbfh©l X® Ia¤âdh‰ g‰w¥g£L¢ rLâÆš Ã‹whŸ; VbdÅš, mtŸ m§‡d§ f©lJ Úynyhrd‹ Kf¤ijna ah«!- accamūṭṭum nallāṉi yaṉṟip piṟaṉ allaṉ eṉa iṉṉum avaṉ piḻipaṭa nampiṉa avvaḻakiya puttiḷiñaṉatu mukattiyēyām! caṭutiyil maṟinta ammiṉṉoḷikkup piṉṉar aṅkut tōṉṟiya iruḷ muṉṉilum karumiyāyt tōṉṟiyatu, ākavē, kumutavalliyum cuntarāmpāḷum taṉṉip piṉ ṟoṭarntu vantila reṉpataṉit tikaḻkali uṭaṉē kāṇakkūṭavilli.
ammiṉṉoḷiyāṉatu taṉ taliviyik kuruṭu paṭuttiyatō aṉṟi ētēṉum paḻutu ceytatō veṉṉum naṭukkam vāyntavaḷāy aṉpuḷḷa perumāṭṭi, yātu nērntatu! eṉṟu cuntarāmpāḷ kēṭṭaṉaḷ,
oṉṟumilli-oṉṟumilli cuntarāmpāḷ! eṉṟu kalakkam pātiyum tikippup pātiyum uṭiyaḷāyk kumutavalli viṭi kūṟiṉāḷ.
ā! meyyākavē miṉṉalāṉatu tikiltarat takkatāyt tāṉ iruntatu! eṉṟu cuntarāmpāḷ naṭukkat tōṭu kūṟiṉāḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, kumutavalliyi aṅṅaṉaṅ kalaṅkappaṇṇiṉa atēporuḷi avaḷ kāṇumpaṭi nēravilli. ippōtu kumutavalli taṉpāṅkiyōṭu niṉṟu viṭṭa iṭattiṟkut tikaḻkali tirumpivantu, perumāṭṭi, nīṅkaḷ ēṉ eṉpiṉṉē varavilli? eṉṟu viṉavit taṅkaḷi veruḷac ceytataṟku mēl ammiṉṉalāl vēṟu ētun tīṅku nērāmal kutama cākkiyar kākkaṭṭum eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. avviḷiya perumāṭṭi ippōtu taṉnilikku vantavaḷāki, ammiṉ ṉoḷiyāṉatu ovvoru poruḷiyum teḷivāyp pulaṉākkiyatu; āṉāl, kaṭpārviyiṉ oḷiyō makkaḷ neñcil attakiya payaṉit taramāṭṭātu. tikaḻkali, eṉakkuḷ aiyuṟavu nikaḻkiṉṟatu- eṉṟu kūṟukiyil,
avvaṟivōḷ, perumāṭṭi, nāṅkaḷ ituvariyil tuṇintu vantataṟku mēl iṉivaravēṇṭāmeṉṟum, nīṅkaḷ tirumpippōkalāmeṉṟum yāṉ colluvēṉ, āṉāl appaṭic ceyvēṉāyiṉ taṅkaḷiṭam ippōtu pēcum ivaḷippaṟṟit tāṅkaḷ koṇṭa aiyam aṅṅaṉamē taṅkiviṭum - aḵtu avvāṟākappaṭātu! j§fis tŠá¤ bjhGf tšytŸ mšny‹ vd¥ò¤jrhuz® m¿a MizÆ£L¢ brhšY»nw‹!itō, perumāṭṭi, intak kuttuvāḷi eṭuttuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ; taṅkaḷit tīyōrkkuk kāṭṭik koṭukkiṟēṉ eṉṟu nīṅkaḷ kaṇṭa anta nimiṣamē, eṉ neñcil itaṉi aḻuntappāyccuṅkaḷ! eṉṟurit tāḷ.
ām - nāṉ ikkaruviyi vittukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ eṉṟu collik kumutavalli ataṉit taṉ ariyic cūḻnta paṭṭikiyil cevviyāy vittuk koṇṭāḷ. ippōtu yāṉ kēṭpataṟku viṭi col tikaḻkali! ippōtu eṉṉi aḻittuk koṇṭupōka irukkum iṭattil, nammiyum iṟantupōṉa avvammiyiṉ pāṅki māriyun tavira vēṟevarēṉum vantirupparō? veṉṟu nāṉ kēṭkiṟēṉ.
ām, perumāṭṭi - maṟṟoruvarum aṅkiruppar. eṉṟu tikaḻkali viṭikūṟiṉāḷ: āyiṉum, puttacāraṇar aṟiya avar taṅkaḷukkuc ciṟitum tīṅku iyaṟṟat takkavar allar eṉṟu āṇiyiṭṭuc collukiṉṟēṉ! uṅkaḷi nōkki oru māṟṟaṅkūṭa avar pēcamāṭṭār! taṉ viralkaḷil oṉṟē yēṉum avar uṅkaḷ mēl vippārallar! ippōtu taṅkaḷukku icivu tāṉā iṉṉum yāṉ yātu collak kūṭum?
nallatu vaḻikāṭṭic cel eṉṟu kumutavalli kūṟiṉāḷ.
taṉ iḷiya talivikkum avvaṟivōḷukkum iṭiyiṟ curukkamāy virintu nikaḻnta ippēccic cuntarāmpāḷ maṟupaṭiyum eḻunta kavaliyōṭum aiyuṟavōṭum uṟṟuk kēṭṭāḷ. intat tōḻippeṇ taṉ nākanāṭṭaraci tirumpiviṭamāṭṭāḷā eṉṟu taṉakkuḷ virumpiṉāḷ; āyiṉum, avaḷ muṉcellutaṟkut tīrmāṉaṅ koṇṭirup patik kaṇṭu, nāṉ ōreḻuttālāvatu taṭuttup pēcuvataṟkun tuṇintilaḷ. kumutavalli tiṭīreṉa niṉṟa kāraṇan teriyā miyāl, veruḷattakka miṉṉaloḷiyik kaṇṭu accuṟuttappaṭṭavaḷāy avaḷ melivuṟṟu niṉṟāḷeṉavē cuntarāmpāḷ taṉakkuḷ eṇṇik koṇṭāḷ.
ippōtu kumutavalli muṟṟilum accamum aiyuṟavum illāmal vaḻi naṭakka villi. avaḷukkut tikaḻkaliyip paṟṟic ciṟitu tāṉ teriyum. mikavuṅ koṭumiyāṉa oru kuṟṟattic ceyyat tuṇinta oruvar tamatu karutti oḷikkum poruṭṭu nirampiya aṭakka oṭukkattōṭum cattiyam paṇṇap piṉ vāṅka māṭṭār eṉṟu avaḷ eṇṇāmalirukka muṭiyavilli. eṉṟālum, tikaḻkaliyiṉ kuralilum mātiri yilum ētō uṇmiyiruntamiyāl, kumutavalli muṟṟilum aiyappāṭṭukkē iṭaṅkoṭuttu viṭat tuṇiyavilli. iṟantupōṉa mīṉāmpāḷiṉ uyirukku amitiyit taruvateṉṟu nāṉ eṇṇiya oru tuṇivāṉa ceykiyil uṟutiyiy niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ kaṭamiyiṉāl avaḷ tūṇṭappaṭṭāḷ; uḷḷapaṭiyē ētum cūḻcci niṉikkap paṭṭirāviṭṭāl- ētum vali virikkappaṭṭirāviṭṭāl-akkaṭamiyi niṟivēṟṟāmal tāṉ tirumpivantatip paṟṟit taṉṉiyē tāṉ koṭumiyāy ikaḻntu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyirukkum, mēlum, avaḷ appeṇpiḷḷiyiṉ kuttuvāḷit tāṉ vāṅkivittukkoṇṭāḷ; taṇṭa ṉik kuḷḷākāmal appeṇ taṉakku ētun tīṅku ceyya muyalak kūṭāteṉṟu avaḷ tīrmāṉittāḷ. ciṟitu nērattiṟku muṉṉē nāṉ nallāṉiyē kaṇṭatākat tuṇipu koṇṭavaḷāy iṟantu pōṉa taṉmaṉivi kiṭanta iṭattiṟku avaṉ varavēṇṭuvatu iyaṟkiyē yeṉṟu kumutavalli eṇṇi ṉāḷ; ivvāṟu eṇṇamiṭṭuk koṇṭē muṭivāka akkaḷvar talivaṉ aṅku vantiruppatu eṉakku ētun tīṅku ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṉum nōkkam illāmalum irukka lām! eṉṟu avaḷ taṉakkuṭ collikkoṇṭāḷ.
tikaḻkaliyōṭu curukkamākavum virivākavun tāṉ pēciyapoḻutu koḷḷit talivaṉāṉa nallāṉ peyari avaḷ moḻintilaḷ. ēṉeṉṟāl, mutalāvatu taṉṟōḻip peṇ cuntarāmpāḷi accuṟuttuvataṟku avaḷ virumpa villi; iraṇṭāvatu naṭutteruvula avaṉ peyar collappaṭumāyiṉ ataṉi oṭṭuk kēṭṭavar nīlakiri nakarattuḷḷāri yellām caṭutiyil eḻuppuvittu, avaṉip piṭippavarkkup paricil kiṭikkumātalāl ataṉit tēṭip piṭikkumpaṭi ceytuviṭuvāreṉ ṟeṇṇiyum, tāṉ mīṉām pāḷukkuk kūṟiya uṟutimoḻikaḷi kaḷintum ataṉic collātu viṭṭaṉaḷ.
ciṟitu nērattilellān tikaḻkali oru matiṟ katavaṇṭi vantuniṉṟu atiluḷḷa puḻi vāyiṟ katavit tiṟantu, piṉṉē kumutavalliyum, cuntarāmpāḷum vara uḷ nuḻintāḷ. uḷ vāyiṟ paṭimēl kāppirippeṇ kiyil viḷakkēnti niṉṟāḷ; intappeṇ tāḻmiyōṭuṅ kumutavalliyi vaṇaṅki, āṉāl oru coṟkūṭap pēcāmal mēṭippaṭimēl aḻittup pōṉāḷ. taḷattiṉmēṟ pōyc cērntārkaḷ. kumutavalli nāṉ vantirukkum avvīṭu paḻiyamātiriyāyum iṭamakaṉṟatāyum irukkakkaṇṭāḷ. ēṉeṉṟāl, intat taḷattiliruntu palakiḷikaḷāyp piriyum nīṇṭa vaḻiyuḷḷa vāyilkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. āyiṉum, cuṟṟilumuḷḷa avaṟṟip pārkkavum ārāyavum avaḷukku nīṇṭa nēram illi; itaṟkuḷ akkāppirimātu uyarnta oru cōṭu maṭakkuk katavukaḷil oṉṟit tiṟantāḷ.. avaḷ avvāyiliṉ uḷḷē pukum muṉṉamē, veḷiyē attaḷattiṉ mēl viḷakki vittuviṭṭāḷ. kariyacūriyakāntap paṭṭuttiri oṉṟu appāl iḻuttu viṭappaṭṭatu; kumutavalliyum tikaḻkaliyum cuntarāmpāḷum uḷ nuḻinta ariyil viḷakku veḷiccam maṅkalāy miṉukku miṉukkeṉṟu vīciyatu.
avvaṟi iṭam akaṉṟatāy tāṉ iruntatu: aṅkuḷḷa taḷavāṭaṅkaḷip pārttāl avi cevviyākavē amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āyiṉum, arivāci iruḷāy iruntatu; ēṉeṉil, naṭuvēyirunta mēcimēl iraṇṭu meḻukuttirikaḷ māttiramē erintu koṇṭiruntaṉa; cuvarkaḷiṉ mēl kaṟupputtuṇi toṅka viṭappaṭṭiruntatu. avvaṟiyil aṅṅaṉam niṟintu niṉṟa ciṟukiya caviruḷ accattiyum vaṇakka voṭukkattiyun tōṟṟuvippatā yiruntatu - āṉālaḵtu appōtu utavukiṟa nōkkattiṟkē icintatā yiruntatu. akkaṟiyil kaṭikkōṭiyil oru kaṭṭiliruntatu, atu nilamaṭṭattiṟku mēl iraṇṭupaṭi uyarttappaṭṭiruntu oru tiṇṇimēl iṭappaṭṭiruntatu. kariyacūriyakāntappaṭṭāṟ camitta oru perum paṭaṅku akkaṭṭiliṉmēṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu; atēpaṭṭāṟceyta toṅkalkaḷ akkaṭṭiliṉ talimāṭṭiccūḻa nāṟṟap paṭṭiruntaṉa. akkaṭṭiliṉmēl mīṉāmpāḷiṉ uṭampāṉatu oru veḷḷit tuppaṭṭiyiṉāṟ cuṟṟi nīṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu; avaḷatu mukam mukkāṭiṭṭu maṟikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
āṉāl, ōr araciyiyum taṉatu ceḻumiyāṉa vaḷarcciyiṉāṟ poṟāmi aṭiyac ceyyum avaḷatu kariya koḻuṅ kūntaliṉ nīṇṭa pala mayirc curuḷkaḷāṉavi ammukkāṭṭiṉ maṭippukaḷiṉ kīḻē citaṟik kiṭantaṉa.
avvaṟiyiṟ paraviyirunta arivāci maṅkalilum, akkaṭṭiṟ kālmāṭṭil niṉṟa oruvaṉikkumutavalliyum cuntarāmpāḷum uṭaṉē terintu koṇṭārkaḷ. avaṉatu kariya iyaṟkiyilē miṉumiṉuppāṉa mayiriṉ mēlum, nērttiyāy varintāṟ pōṉṟa mīciyiṉ mēlum. aḻakiya mukattiṉ mēlum, avaḷ aṇintirunta caṭṭiyiṉ pūntiyalkaḷ mēlum, avaṉatu kattik kippiṭiyil aḻuttiya maṇikaḷiṉ mēlum aṅkuḷḷa acciṟiya veḷiccam paṭṭu miḷirntatu. avaṉ kikaḷ mārpiṉ kuṟukkē maṟikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṉ tuyaramāṉa pārviyōṭu akkaṭṭiliṉ mēṟ kiṭanta uruvatti uṟṟup pārttukkoṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉ iḷiya nīlalōcaṉaṉē yaṉṟi vēṟallaṉ.
avaṉi avviṭattiṟ pārppatik kumutavalli etir pārāmal irukkavilli; avaṉic ciṟitu pārtta anta noṭiyē avaḷ cuntarāmpāḷ pakkamāyt tiruppip pēcātē! accamuṟṟuk kūvātē! tiriyamāy iru! iṟantu pōṉa taṉ maṉiviyiṉ paṭukkiyaṇṭiyiruntu koṇṭē avaṉ namakku oru tīṅku ceyyattuṇivāṉ allaṉ! eṉa kucukucuveṉṟu virintu kūṟiṉāḷ.
kumutavalli taṉ tōḻippeṇṇi nalla nērattil eccarittatu naṉṟāyiṟṟu; ēṉeṉil, payaṅkaraṉāṉa nallā ṉeṉṟē nīlalōcaṉaṉit tāṉum piḻipaṭa eṇṇiyirunta cuntarāmpāḷ avaṉippārttavuṭaṉē vīriṭum nilimiyaḷ āṉāḷ. attōḻippeṇ taṉakkuḷḷa maṉavalimiyi yellām taṉakkut tuṇiyāka oruṅkē varuvittuk koṇṭu taṉ aṉpuḷḷa taliviyiṉ kiṭṭa mika neruṅkiniṉṟāḷ. avvaṟiyiṉ katavukku mikavum eṭṭiyirunta pakkamāy akkaṭṭilaṇṭi mīṉāmpāḷiṉ tōḻikaḷil oruttiyāṉa maliyanāṭṭup paṇippeṇ maṇṭikkāliṭṭiruntāḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, mēṭi nīṇṭirunta antak kaṭiciyil cuvarukku mūṉṟaṭi vilaki akkaṭṭil niṉṟatu.
akkāppirippeṇ ciṟitu piṉṉē varak kumutavalli yiyum cuntarāmpāḷiyum tikaḻkali ippōtu akkaṭṭi laṇṭi mella aḻittuc ceṉṟāḷ. iṟantavarkkuk kāṭṭavēṇṭum vaṇakkatti eṇṇik kumutavalliyum cuntarāmpāḷum tamatu mukkāṭṭip piṉpuṟam taḷḷiṉārkaḷ. taṉakku muṉṉē nīṭṭappaṭṭirunta uruvattiṉiṉṟum nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉ viḻikaḷi uyara eṭuttu aṟikkuḷ nuḻintavar evareṉṟupārkka ippōtu tāṉ mutal mutal nōkkiṉāṉ. aḻakiya kumutavalliyiavaṉ pārttatum kalantu tōṉṟiya viyappiṉālum kaḷippiṉālum caṭutiyil tiṭuk kiṭṭāḷ; āṉāl, tikaḻkaliyō eccarikkuṅ kuṟip pōṭu uṭaṉē taṉkiyi uyarattūkki - metuvāyiruntālum kātukku naṉṟāyk kēṭkumpaṭi attaṉi teḷivāyuḷḷa kuralil, oru coṟkūṭa! ōr eḻuttuk kūṭappēca vēṇṭām! iṟantavarkaku etiril nīṅkaḷ irukkiṟīrkaḷeṉpati niṉiyuṅkaḷ! eṉṟu pakarntāḷ.
nīlalōcaṉaṉ iṅṅaṉam tiṭīreṉṟu niṉivuṟuttap paṭṭavaṉāy maṟupaṭiyum taṉ kaṇkaḷik kīḻnōkkik koṇṭāṉ; eṉṟālum kumutavalli taṉ kaṇkaḷ ivaṉ kaṇkaḷiṉ etirnōkkutalik karuttāy vilakkik koṇṭaṉa ḷeṉṟum, tikaḻkali tīrmāṉamākavum aḻuttamākavum curukkamākavum accoṟkaḷi moḻintapōtu avaḷatu mukattiṉmēl veḷiṟiṉa niṟam oṉṟu paraviṟṟeṉṟum karutippārkka avaṉ tavaṟavilli.
kumutavalliyum cuntarāmpāḷum akkaṭṭilaṇṭi ceṉṟa tamakkuk kiṭṭirunta pakkattil niṉṟārkaḷ - nīlalōcaṉaṉō kāl māṭṭaṇṭiyilēyē iṉṉum niṉṟu koṇṭiruntāṉ. malināṭṭup paṇippeṇ etirppakkattil muḻantāḷiṭ ṭiruntāḷ.
tikaḻkali vaṇakka oṭukkammikka melliya kuralil, iṟantu pōṉa ippeṇṇiṉ mukattip pāruṅkaḷ! eṉṟu collik koṇṭē mīṉāmpāḷ mukattiṉmēliruntu mukkāṭṭi melleṉat tūkkiṉāḷ. pāruṅkaḷ iḵtu evvaḷavu amitiyāyirukkiṉṟatu! perumāṭṭi, taṉ muḻu amitiyi uṟutippaṭuttumporuṭṭut tāṅkaḷ ippōtu iṅkēvantiruppataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa avvuyir nalmeyti yiruppataṉi aṟivikkum naṟkuṟiyāka ivvamitik kuṟippi yām niṉippāmāka!
iṅṅaṉam viḷampiya piṉṉart tāṉ mutaliṟtūkkiya paṭiyē metuvāka ammukkāṭṭiṉit tikaḻkali kīḻē iṟakkiviṭṭāḷ; mīṉāmpāḷiṉ mukamāṉatu maṟupaṭiyum ivarkaḷatu pārviyiṉiṉṟum maṟipaṭṭatu oru nimiṣanēram ellārum vaṇakka koṭukkattōṭu vāypēcātiruntaṉar; ataṉpiṟaku kumutavalliyiṉ kuralāṉatu veḷḷimaṇiyiṉ olipōṉṟu mellitāyt teḷivāyiruntālum naṭukkamuḷḷa tāyp pēcattuvaṅkiyatu.
mīṉāmpāḷ! nīṅkaḷ evvakiyāṉa tīṅku eṉakkuc ceytiruntālum aṉṟic ceyyak karutiyiruntālum--attīṅkiṉ aḷavum ataṉ taṉmiyum muṉṉum iṉṉum yāṉ aṟiyātiruntālum, avaṟṟiyellām yāṉ maṉṉittu viṭṭataṉ uṟutimoḻiyi nīṅkaḷ uyirōṭiruntapōtē yāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟiṉēṉ. āṉāl ippōtum,--ivviṭattum, uyir aṟṟa uṅkaḷ uṭampu kiṭakkum ikkaṭṭilaṇṭiyilum, iṉṉum puṉitamākavum vaṇakkavoṭukkattōṭum am maṉṉippu uṟuti moḻiyit tiruppic collukiṉṟēṉ. ō mīṉāmpāḷ! aḻaku paṭuttuvataṟkeṉṟē piṟantāleṉṉa nīṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya innilavukattiṉiṉṟum vīḻntu pōṉa vāṉmīṉ pōlat tōṉṟiya ciṟitukālam nīṅkaḷ maṟintu ceṉṟālum, iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷāl uṅkaḷ uyir iṉṉum uyarnta iṉiyatōr ulakattiṟku mēleṭukkappaṭṭu, aṅkē eṉṟum viḷaṅkum mutalvaṉatu aruḷirukkiyiṉiṉṟum pōtarum avvaruḷoḷiyiṉ nalla tūya viḷakkattiṉkaṇ nīṅkaḷ tikaḻntirukkumpaṭi avviyaṉ aruḷ vaḻaṅku vāṉāka! eṉṟu avviḷiya naṅki moḻintāḷ.
itaṉic colliyatuṅ kumutavalli niṉṟupōṉāḷ; avaḷ iṉṉuñciṟitu pēciyiruppāḷ, āṉāl ippōtu taṉṉi mēṟkoṇṭu eḻunta urukkattiṉāṟ pēcak kūṭāta vaḷāṉāḷ. ippōtu aḻutavaḷ avaḷ māttiram allaḷ; ēṉeṉil, arivāci aṭakkappaṭṭa tēmpal olikaḷ avaḷ ceviyiṟ kēṭṭaṉa. cuntarāmpāḷum, maliyanāṭṭup paṇippeṇṇum, antakkāppirip peṇṇum aḻutaṉar; avviṭattiṟṟōṉṟiya vaṇakka oṭukkamum irakkamum vāynta innikaḻcciyiṉāl nīlalōcaṉaṉ uḷḷaṅ kalaṅkiṉāṉ; tikaḻkaliyiṉ kaṇkaḷum nīraṟṟu irukkavilli. kaṭiciyāka, iṉṉum inniliyilirukkiṉṟa kumutavalli pakkamāyt tikaḻkali tirumpip pōtum, perumāṭṭi! puttacamayattiṟ paṟṟuṭiyavaḷāṉa yāṉuṅ kūṭa intac caṭaṅkāṉatu taṉ payaṉit tarāmaṟ pōkāteṉṟu ippōtu nampat talippaṭukiṉṟēṉ, mēlulakattuḷḷa oru teyvattiṉ vēṇṭukōḷ moḻiyi innilavulakattil veḷiyiṭṭuc collum ōruyiriṉ kuraloli oṉṟu uṇṭāyiṉ aḵtu uṅkaḷuṭiyatēyākum! eṉṟu melleṉac coṉṉāḷ.
ivvāṟu collik koṇṭē tikaḻkali kumuta valliyiṉ kiyip piṭittu avaḷi appāṟ koṇṭu pōkalāṉāḷ. accamayattil nīlalōcaṉaṉ muṉṉēṟi vantu nākanāṭṭaraciyi nōkkip pēcuṅ kuṟippuṭiyaṉātalik kaṇṭu tikaḻkali tuṭukkākac ciki ceytu avaṉ irunta iṭattilēyē vāypēcātirukkumpaṭi kuṟippittāḷ. avaṉ avaḷ collukkuc kīḻppaṭiyātirukkat tuṇiyavilli: ēṉeṉṟāl attuṇi vaṇakka voṭukkattiyum accat tiyum viḷivikkum iyalpu vāynta annikaḻcciyiṉ aṭiyē, ataṉiyellām naṭattum urimiyuṭiyavaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭa muṉ aṟimukam illāta appeṇ kāraṇa millāmalē accamūṭṭum ōratikārattic celuttu vāḷāṉāḷ.
koḷḷikkāra nallāṉeṉṟē tāṉ karutiviṭṭa nīlalōcaṉaṉ mēl kumutavalli oru muṟiyēṉum taṉ pārviyic celuttavēyilli. apperumāṭṭi iṅṅaṉam taṉṉiṭattu muṟṟuṅ kaṇicak kuṟiccalāy naṭantatip paṟṟi nīlalōcaṉaṉ mikunta varuttamum atip pōlavē mikunta viyappuṅ koḷḷap peṟṟāṉ--ittakiya naṭakkiyiṉ kāraṇam iṉṉateṉṟu pulappaṭāmiyōṭu, malināṭṭu viyāpāriyiṉ vīṭṭiṉ kaṇ tōḻami koṇṭirunta taṉṉi avaḷ caṭutiyiṟ pirintu pōṉa marumattiyum itu mikutip paṭuttiyatu. kumutavalliyiyum cuntarāmpāḷiyum tikaḻkali avvaṟik katavaṇṭi aḻittup pōṉāḷ; avarkaḷ orumuṟiyēṉum piṉṉē tirumpip pārkkavilli: cūriya kāntap paṭṭuttiri appāl iḻuttuviṭappaṭṭatu--katavu tiṟakkappaṭṭatu, avarkaḷtaḷattiṉ mēl vantu niṉṟārkaḷ.
ippōtu perumāṭṭi, nīṅkaḷ taṅkiyirukkum viṭutiyiṉ tōṭṭa vāyilukku maṟupaṭiyum uṅkaḷi aḻittuc celvēṉ. avviṭattiṟku nīṅkaḷ ceṉṟapiṉ allāmal, ciṟitu nēramēṉum tikaḻkalimēl nīṅkaḷ avanampikkiyuṟṟatip paṟṟi uṅkaḷukku uṇṭāṉa varuttatti nīṅkaḷ avaḷukkut terivikka vēṇṭām. eṉṟu tikaḻkali moḻintaṉaḷ.
eṉṟālum kumutavalli taṉatu varuttatti uṭaṉē avaḷukku eṭutturittiruppāḷ; āṉāl, avvaṟiviṉaḷō antak kāppirippeṇ taḷattiṉ mēl vittu viṭṭup pōṉa viḷakkik kiyileṭuttuk koṇṭu, avaḷi mettip paṭiyiṉ kīḻ mikavuñ curukkeṉa vaḻi naṭattik koṇṭu ceṉṟāḷ. tikaḻkali, kumutavalli, cuntarāmpāḷ eṉṉum mūvar mukaṅkaḷiṉ mēlun tirumpavum mukkāṭu iḻuttuviṭap paṭṭaṉa. talivāyiliṉ uṭpuṟattē viḷakku vittu viṭappaṭṭatu; piṉṉuñ ciṟitu nērattil avarkaḷ teruviṟ ceṉṟa pōtu avarkaḷukkup piṟakē vāyiṟkatavu mūṭik koṇṭatu. vāy pēcāmalum pātukāppōṭum avarkaḷ vaḻi toṭarntu ceṉṟārkaḷ. maṉōkaratu viṭutiyiṉ tōṭṭa vāyilaṇṭi vattatum kumutavalli tikaḻkaliyi nōkka, yāṉ ciṟitu nēramvariyil uṉṉip paṟṟi niṉikka vēṇṭiyatāyirunta tīya aiyuṟaviṉ poruṭṭu eṉ akat tuṇṭāṉa mikka tuyarattit terivippataṟku itutāṉ camayamāyiṟṟu. uṉatu kuttu vāḷit tirumpavum nīyē eṭuttuk koḷ. eṉṟu pukaṉṟāḷ.
tikaḻkali taṉatu kuttuvāḷik kumutavalliyiṉ kiyiliruntu vāṅkukiyil, perumāṭṭi, iṉiyorukāl tirumpavum nām etirppaṭukuvamāyiṉ ivvaṟivōḷiṭattut tāṅkaḷ nampikki uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruppīrkaḷ. ippōtu viṭi koṭuṅkaḷ! eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
oru moḻi, tikaḻkali--oru moḻi! eṉṟu kumutavalli virintukūvip piṉṉum, cila ceytikaḷip paṟṟi nī nirampavun terintavaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟāy: uṉakku avaṟṟi naṉṟāy aṟivikkak kūṭiyavarkaḷōṭu nī irukkiṉṟaṉi. eṉakkuc col, nāṉ uṉṉik keñcukiṉṟēṉ - iṟantupōṉa mīṉāmpāḷ muṉṉorukāl eṉakkucceyya niṉitta tītu yātu? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāḷ.
perumāṭṭi, eṉakkut teriyātu, eṉṟu tikaḻkali virintu viṭikūṟik, kaṭitiṟṟirumpic ceṉṟu orunoṭiyiṟ kaṭpulaṉukku eṭṭāmaṟ, cūḻntirunta iruḷil maṟintu pōṉāḷ.
aṉpiṟkiṉiya perumāṭṭi, nām pattiramāy vantu cērntataṟkāka nam perumāṉi vaḻuttuvamāka! eṉṟu cuntarāmpāḷ kaḷippumikka kuraliṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.
ām-nam iṟivaṉi nām vāḻttuvamāka! eṉṟu nākanāṭṭaraci icintu kūṟiṉāḷ.
piṉṉar avarkaḷ maṟivu vāyiliṉ vaḻiyē uḷ nuḻintu, tōṭṭattik kaṭantu, tattam aṟikaṭkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. aṅkē ñāṉāmpāḷ avarkaḷi varavēṟkum poruṭṭu mikunta kaḷippāṟ kutittōṭi vantāḷ; ēṉeṉil, avarkaḷatu varukiyāṉatu nampikkiyuḷḷa appeṇ koṇṭirunta aḷavaṟṟa kavaliyiyum tikiliyum aiyuṟaviyum tīrttatu.
maṟunāṭkāliyil uṇavaruntiyavuṭaṉē kumutavalli tōṭṭattilē ulāvuvataṟkākak kīḻ iṟaṅkiṉāḷ-- inta vēḷiyil avaḷ pāṅkimār avaḷ piṉṉē varavilli; ēṉeṉṟāl ciṟitu nēram avarkaḷ tam irukkiyiṟ ceyya vēṇṭiya aluvalmēl niṉṟārkaḷ. ākavē taṉiyirunta kumutavalliyiṉ uḷḷattiṟ piṉvarumāṟu eṇṇaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāy eḻuntaṉa. iṉṟikkuttāṉ maṉōkarar tirumpi vara vēṇṭiya nāḷ. nākanāṭṭiṉkaṇuḷḷa taṉatu māḷiki yiṉiṉṟum tāṉ varavaḻikkappaṭṭa mutaṉmiyāṉa kāraṇam iṉṉateṉṟu taṉakkut terivikkappaṭutaṟku avaḷ etir pārttirunta nāḷ iṉṟu tāṉ. iṉṉum muṉṉāḷ māliyil nērnta nikaḻccikaḷiyum avaḷ niṉittāḷ. attaṉi iṉimiyōṭum urukkattōṭum tāṉ evaḷuṭiya uyiriṉ poruṭṭuk kaṭavuḷit toḻutaṉaḷō avaḷi niṉittu oru perumūc ceṟintāḷ.
iraṇṭari nāḻiki nērattiṟku mēl kumutavalli attōṭṭattilē ulavik koṇṭiruntāḷ; appōtu taṟceyalāy avaḷ itaṟku muṉ tāṉ pukuntirāta ōr oṭuṅkiya cāliyiṉūṭē celvāḷāṉāḷ. ataṉ iru maruṅkum paḻantarumaraṅkaḷ aṭarntu, ammaraṅkaḷ mēṟ paṭar koṭikaḷ caṉṉal piṉṉalāy vaḷarntu kiṭantamiyāl, kumutavalli ippōtu iṟaṅkiyirukkum kaṭṭiṭattiṟku etir variciyāy aminta kaṭṭiṭatti nōkkic celluṅ koṭippantarp pātiyāy aḵtu amintiruntatu. kumutavalli ivveṇṇaṅ kaḷiṉ vayappaṭṭavaḷāyk kīḻ nōkkiya kaṭpārviyuṭaṉ iccāli vaḻiyē ceṉṟāḷ. caṭutiyilē oru cāḷarat taṭṭik katavu tiṟakkappaṭṭa ōciyum, atē camayattil makiḻcciyum viyappuṅ kalanta oru kuraloliyum avaḷ cevikku eṭṭiṉa. uṭaṉē avaḷ nimirntu nōkkiṉāḷ; aṅkumutalaṭukkiluḷḷa oru palakaṇiyaṇṭi yil iḷiya nīlalōcaṉaṉ niṉṟaṉaṉ.
koḷḷikkāra nallāṉeṉṟē tāṉ piḻipaṭakkarutiya avvāṭavaṉimaṉōkararatu māḷikiyiṉkaṇṇē--taṉakkum pukaliṭamāy aminta avvīṭṭiṉkaṇṇē aṅṅaṉam pārttalum kumutavalli taṉṉakattu naṭukkattāl eḻunta kūkkuraloliyi varuttattōṭu tāṉ aṭakkak kūṭiyatāyi runtatu. peruntikilum viyappumē avaḷ vāyit tiṟavātapaṭi tiṭīreṉap pūṭṭi viṭṭaṉa! uṭaṉē virintu mukkāṭṭi iḻuttiṭṭuk koṇṭu tirumpi avviṭatti viṭṭuk kaṭukic ceṉṟāḷ. taṉ kātiṟkuc cila coṟkaḷ vantu kiṭṭiṉa--avaḷatu pēraccattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta maṉitaṉiṉ vāyiliruntu mummuramāyk kattic collappaṭṭa coṟkaḷē aviyām. āyiṉum, accoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ allatu karuttu avaḷukkup piṭipaṭavilli, avaḷatu mūḷi at tuṇi kuḻampip pōyiṟṟu, avaḷuṇarvukaḷ attuṇi kalaṅkip pōyiṉa. avvīṭṭukkāra mutiyōḷi aḻittut taṉṉit tavira atē vīṭṭiṉkaṇ iṉṉum vēṟu evar taṅkiyirukkiṉṟā reṉpa taṉik kēṭṭaṟiyum maṉavuṟuti yōṭuavaḷ virintu taṉ aṟikkut tirumpiṉāḷ,--āṉāl, taṉ uṇarvu taṉ nilikku vantavuṭaṉē kumutavalli tāṉ mīṉāmpāḷukkuc colliya uṟutimoḻiyip pātukākkavum vilakka muṭiyā nikaḻcci kaḷāl veḷiviṭa nērntālallāmal tāṉ koḷḷikkāra nallāṉeṉṟē piḻipaṭa eṇṇiya avvāṇmakaṉik kāṭṭik koṭukkalākāteṉavun tīrmāṉittāḷ.
avaḷ mettip paṭikkaṭṭiṉ mēl ēṟic ceṉṟa māttirattē, kuḷiyalaṟi vāyiliṟ cuntarāmpāḷi etirpaṭac, cuntarāmpāḷum colluvāḷ perumāṭṭi, taṅkaḷik kāṇum poruṭṭu ivvīṭṭukkāra mutiyōḷ kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ talivarum periya viyāpāriyu māṉa maṉōkarar ippōtu tāṉ tirumpi vantirukkiṟār....
ā, avar tirumpi vantu viṭṭaṉarā? eṉṟu kūṟukiyiṟ kumutavalli collutaṟkariya āṟutal eytap peṟṟavaḷāyt tāṉ koṇṭa kaḷipput taṉ kaṇkaḷil oḷira īcaṉē pōṟṟi! eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ melleṉac collik koṇṭāḷ; ēṉeṉil, taṉakku mikat terintavarum māṭcimi niṟintavarumāṉa maṉōkarar taṉakku naṇparum taṉṉip pātukāppavarumāy iruppareṉa avaḷ ippōtu uṟutiyāy nampiṉāḷ.
taṉakku accattiyum, kalakkattiyum viḷivitta akkaṭiciyāṉa nikaḻcciyip paṟṟi avaḷ taṉ pāṅkimārkku ōr eḻuttuk kūṭac collavilli; ēṉeṉṟāl, avaḷ iyaṟkiyilē peruntaṉmiyāṉa uḷḷamuṭiyōḷāyirut taliṉ taṉṉiṭattē niṟinta paṟṟutalum naṉṟiyum vittirukkum avviḷam peṇkaḷ accuṟutaṟkuriya ceyti kaḷit taṉṉāl iyaṉṟa maṭṭum avaḷ eppōtum vilakkavē niṉintāḷ. celvattiṟ ciṟanta avviyāpāri muṉṉiliyil takuntapaṭi cellal vēṇṭi virivil avaḷuṭikaḷ ciṟitu māṟṟi uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaḷ mantira mōtiratti viralil aṇintu koṇṭavaḷāyt, taṉṉi avviyāpāri muṉṉiliyil aḻittuk koṇṭu pōtaṟkeṉṟu avvīṭṭukkāra mutiyōḷ vantu kāttuk koṇṭirukkum irukkiyaṟikkuc ceṉṟāḷ.
iṭam akaṉṟa akkaṭṭiṭattiṉ naṭuvil allatu mutaṉmiyāṉa iṭattil aminta aṟikkuc cellā niṉṟa pātiyiṉūṭē kumutavalli ammutiyōḷip piṉṟōṭarntē kiṉāḷ: avvaṟik katavi ammutiyōḷ parakkat tiṟantu viṭṭāḷ; kumutavalliyum uḷ nuḻintāḷ. aḻakiya poruḷkaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭirunta avvaṟiyiṉ kaṭikkōṭiyil cārmaṇik kaṭṭiliṉ mēl uṭkārntirunta māṭcimi mikka mutiyōṉ oruvaṉi mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭāḷ; koḷḷikkāra nallāṉeṉṟutāṉ piḻipaṭak karutiya avvāṭavaṉ muṉṉiliyilum aṅṅaṉamē tāṉ vantiruppatik kumutavalli ataṉpiṉ aṭuttuk kaṇṭāḷ.

atikāram - 14
maṉōkarar

maliyanāṭṭiṟ periya viyāpāriyāṉa maṉōkarar muṉṉiliyilē tammuḷiṅṅaṉam oruvariyoruvar etirp paṭṭapōtu nīlalōcaṉaṉukkuṅ kumutavallikkum uṇṭāṉa uḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷ vēṟu vēṟiyalpiṉavāy iruntaṉa. tāṉ mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭanērantoṭṭut taṉ neñci viṭṭut tavaṟāmal amarnta avvaḻakiya iḷa maṅkiyiṉ vaṭivik kāṇṭalum apputta iḷiñaṉ mikavuṅ kiḷarcciyāṉa kaḷippuk koḷḷap peṟṟāṉ. taṉṉiṭattu avaḷ mikavum putumiyāka naṭantu koṇṭa taṉip paṟṟi ivaṉ aṭinta viyappuṅ kalakkamum oru noṭip poḻutu ivaḷ ippōta ṭinta peru makiḻcciyiṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. iṉi maṟṟa vakiyiṟ kumutavalliyō taṉakketirē koḷḷikkāra nallāṉiyē tāṉ pārppa tākak koṇṭa maṉattuṇiviṉ vayappaṭṭavaḷāy iruntāḷ. avaṉ peyari veḷiviṭṭuc collāmaliruntatum , allatu taṉ uḷḷattiṟ paṭṭati veḷiyē tiṟappāka viṭāmal iyaṉṟamaṭṭum vilakka muyaṉṟatum avaḷ mīṉāmpāḷukkuk koṭutta moḻiyuṟuti yiṉpāl vitta naṉku matippiṉālē yām. iṅṅaṉamāka nīlalōcaṉaṉ oru pakkattil avaḷi viyappuṅ kaḷippuṅ kātaluṅ kalanta pārviyōṭu nōkkik koṇṭirukka, avaḷō maṟṟip pakkattil kīḻ nōkkiya pārviyiṉaḷāyum, nāṇamuṭiyaḷāyiṉum viḻumiya oḻukkam poruntiṉa vaḷāyum maṉōkarar taṉakkuc colvati etirpārttuk  koṇṭiruntāḷ.
nām muṉ colliyapaṭiyē immaliyanāṭṭu viyāpāri yāṉavar peritum naṉku matikkappaṭun tōṟṟamuṭiya varāyiruntār. avaruṭiya vayatu aṟupatteṭṭu allatu eḻupatukkuk kuṟintatāy irātu; taṉṉuṭiya mārpiṉ mēṟṟoṅkum nīṇṭa veḷḷiya tāṭimayir uṭiyavarāy iruntār. vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa kaṇṇāṭiyiṉāl māṭṭuk kompuc caṭṭattil vittuc ceyyappaṭṭa mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭi yāṉatu avaratu vaḷinta mūkkiṉ naṭuvē toṅkiṟṟu; veṇmiyāṉa melliya tuṇiyip pākiyākat taliyiṟ kaṭṭiyiruntār; muḻaṅkāl aḷavun toṅkum nīṇṭa caṭṭiyum pūṇṭiruntār. uṟuppukkaḷiṉ utavi koṇṭu makkaḷ maṉappāṅki mikavum mēṟ pārviyāyp pārttuṇar vārkkum, ivaratu mukattiṉ kaṇ aminta ētō oru kuṟippāṉatu aluvaliṟ paḻakiya nuṇṇaṟivōṭu ivaratu īra neñcattiṉ iyalpiyum pulappaṭak kāṭṭiyatu: mutaliṟ coṉṉa ivaratu kuṇattiṟkuriyatāṉa taṉṉaya viruppamāṉatu piṟar nalaṅkarutum ivaratu peruntaṉmikkumuṉ enta nērattilum kīḻ aṭaṅkip pōvatēyām eṉpati ivar pārvikaḷiliruntu terintu koḷvatu eḷitāyiruntatu.
anta aṟiyiṟ ṟaḷavāṭaṅkaḷ nērttiyāka oḻuṅku paṭuttap paṭṭiruntaṉa. taṭṭik katavukaḷuḷḷa ataṉ cāḷaraṅkaḷil tuṇimaṟippukaḷ toṅkaviṭṭu oppaṉi ceytiruntatu; vaṇṇam pūciya ataṉ maccukaḷic calavik kallāṟ ceyta tūṇkaḷ tāṅki niṉṟaṉa; cuvarkaḷilē ōviyaṅkaḷ toṅka viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa; cārmaṇik kaṭṭilkaḷ cūriyakāntip paṭṭiṉāl tittu ōraṅkaḷiṟ poṟcariki yiṉālum iṭiyiṟ poṉṉurukkaḷālum puṉiyappaṭṭirun taṉa. ciṟiya mēciyiṉ mēlum, nilattiṉ mēlum, palatiṟappaṭṭa uṇāp poruḷkaḷ niṟinta paḷuvāṉa veḷḷikkalaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa, perumpaṉik kaṭṭikaḷiyiṭṭuk kuḷirac ceyta iṉippāṉa paḻaṅkaḷi oruvakic cāntiṉāl vaṉiyappaṭṭa oru peruṅkalattilē nirappi ataṉi oru taviciṉ mēl vittiruntārkaḷ; am māḷikiyic cūḻa irunta veḷi naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻntatōṭu, ceyaṟki kuḻāy ūṟṟukaḷiliruntu kumiḻikkum paḷiṅkotta taṇṇīriṉāṟ kaṭuṅkuḷircciyum ōr aḷavāṉa iṉiya niliyuṭiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu.
nīlalōcaṉaṉ nām mutaṉ mutaṟ kāṭṭiyapaṭiyē uṭiyaṇintiruntaṉaṉ: āṉāṟ kumutavalliyō tāṉ kutiri mēṟ cavāri vantakkāl aṇintirunta ceḻumiyāṉa āṭiyi viṭa iṉṉum mikutiyāṉa aḻakōṭu uṭuttiruntaṉaḷ. nērttiyāṉa poṟpūviṭinta kacciṉi avaḷ muḻaṅki variyil aṇintiruntamiyāl, pālpōlum veṇmi yuṭiyatāy aḻakāka amikkappaṭṭa avaḷatu muṉ kiyāṉatu muḻaṅkivariyilum, allatu ataṟkuc ciṟitu mēluṅ kaṭpulaṉāyiṟṟu. avaḷatu āṭiyiṉ ōramellām paṭṭup piṉṉal uṭiyatāyp pala tiṟappaṭṭa niṟaṅkaḷ vāyntatāyk kaccōṭu kūṭa muḻutum pūttoḻil uḷḷatāy vayaṅkiṟṟu. poṟpaṭṭuk koṭṭiyuṭiya noyya āṭi avaḷatu talikkaṇiyāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu, avaḷ collutaṟkariya aḻakōṭun tōṉṟiṉāḷ; avaḷ aṅkē etirppaṭṭa iḷiya nīlalōcaṉaṉum āṇṭaṉmikkēṟṟapaṭi aṅṅaṉamē vaṉap puṭaṉ tikaḻntāṉ.
nīlalōcaṉaṉ mutalil avvaṟikkuṭ pukuntamiyāl, māṭcimi vāynta avviyāpāri avviḷam puttaṉuṭiya viḻivutarum mukattiyum aḻakiya vaṭivattiyum pōtumāṉa aḷavu ēṟkeṉavē pārttuk koṇṭār. ātalāl, ammutiyōṉ pārvi muḻutum ippōtu kumutavallimēṟ patintiruntaṉa; oruvaki aṉpāṉa uṉṉippōṭum viyap pōṭum iṟumpūtōṭum avar avaḷi muṟṟum nōkkiṉār, ivvāṟāka avaratu pārvi avaḷ mēl niṉṟamiyāl, nīlalōcaṉaṉ avvaḻakiya veṇmaṇiyi muṉ aṟinta aṟimukattōṭum, kaḷippum makiḻcciyum kalanta tōṟṟattōṭu aṅṅaṉamē avaḷi uṟṟup pārttuk koṇṭiruntataṉi avar kāṇārāyiṉar.
avaḷ mutalil avvaṟikkuḷ vantapōtu avaḷi varavēṟṟaṟ poruṭṭuc curukkamākac colliya cila coṟkaḷukkup piṉ ciṟitu nēram variyil maṉōkarar ētoru colluṅ kumutavalliyi nōkkik kūṟāmal iruntār. avar avaḷ mukatti nōkkik koṇṭiruntatu. avaḷuṭiya aḻaku attaṉi kavarcci mikkatāy iruntaṉ poruṭṭāka māttiramaṉṟu, avaḷ iyalpiṉiyum teritaṟ poruṭṭāka vēyām. ēṉeṉṟāl taṉ mēṟparappiṉmēṟ piratipalaṉamām etaṉiyum kaṇṭuṇara vallārkkut tāṉ uḷḷattiṉ kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟu eṉṟum iruppatākiya mukattiṉiyē oru karuviyākak koṇṭu uyiriṉ āḻattiyum uḷḷattiṉ āḻattiyun turuvik kāṇpatil tammi mikavun tiṟami yuṭiyarākac ceytaṟ kicinta aḷavu ammaliya viyāpāri nīṇṭakālam uyir vāḻntu vantaṉar, ulakiṉi yaṟkiyi vēṇṭiyavaḷavukkuk kaṇṭiruntaṉar.
kumutavalli mutaliṟ pukunta pōtu, maṉōkarar etirē nīlalōcaṉaṉ vaṇakkamāṉa niliyil niṉṟu koṇṭirukkak kaṇṭāḷ: avar tam mutumikkum avartan tōṟṟattiṟkum ammāḷikiyil viruntu puṟantaruntalivarāyirukkum avar tam nilimikkum taka atē vaṇakkamāṉa uṇarvōṭu avaḷum aṅṅaṉamē ippōtu niṉṟu koṇṭiruntāḷ. avviruvaratu naṭattiyum avarukku uvappiṉit tantatu; kaṭiciyāka avar vāyit tiṟantatu, eṉ iḷiya kēcarkaḷē--nām ippōtu tām mutaṉmuṟiyāka oṉṟu kūṭiṉō māyiṉum yāṉ uṅkaḷiru variyum aṅṅaṉamē karutukiṉṟēṉ-- nari mutiyōrkkuriya matippiṉi nīṅkaḷ aṟiyāmalirukka villi. eṉ muṉṉiliyil nīṅkaḷ niṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṉṟīrkaḷē; makkaḷukkuriya nilimikaḷiṉpaṭi nammuṭiya nilimikaḷip pārttāl ammuṟiyil yāṉ uṅkaḷiruvariyum viṭa mikat tāḻnta niliyil uḷḷēṉ. māṭcimi vāynta araci taṅkaḷiruk kiyil aruḷ kūrntu amaruṅkaḷ. eṉṟu kumutavallik kurittup piṉṉum nīlalōcaṉaṉ pakkamāyt tirumpi araca, tāṅkaḷum irukkiyil amarumpaṭi vēṇṭik koḷḷu kiṟēṉ. eṉṟu avar kūṟiṉār.
ōr aracikkuk kīḻppaṭāta nilimiyiṉik kuṟikkum moḻikaḷāl kumutavalli muṉṉilippapaṭuttik kūṟap paṭutalik kēṭṭatum avviḷam puttaṉ iṟumpūtuṟṟut tiṭukkiṭṭāṉ; iṉimaṟṟa vakiyiṟ kumutavalliyō nāṉum aṅṅaṉamē tiṭukkiṭāmal irukkakkūṭavilli- ēṉeṉṟāl, araciyalmuṟiyil ettakiyōrkkuriya kōlattil ak kaḷvar talivaṉ taṉṉi maṟittu ammaliya viyāpāri muṉ vantu cērntāṉeṉṟu avaḷ viyappuṟṟāḷ.
eṉ iḷiya naṇparkāḷ, eṉṟu maṉōkarar kūṟukiyil, nīlalōcaṉaṉ tamatu iṭatukippuṟattum kumuta valli tamatu valatu kippuṟattum uḷḷa irukkiyil amartalikkaṇṭu aṉpōṭum muṟuvalittu, uṅkaḷ ovvoruvarkkum ēṟṟa nilimikkuttakayāṉ uṅkaḷi muṉṉilippaṭuttip pēciyapōtu uṅkaḷ mukaṅkaḷiṉmēl viyappukkuṟi tōṉṟiyati yāṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ. āṉālum, itaṟkumuṉ eṉṟum nīṅkaḷ oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭu paḻakiyatillāmiyāl, ippōtu uṅkaḷi oruvarōṭu oruvar paḻakkap paṭutti vikkiṉṟēṉ. ataṉōṭukūṭak, kaṭṭāyamāy illāviṭṭāluṅ kāriya muṟikkāvatu oru viṉiyic ceytumuṭikkak kaṭavēṉ. eṉakku nampakamuḷḷa ēvalāḷāṉa cantiraṉ vāyilāka yāṉaṉuppiya tirumukamum ilacciṉiyum peṟṟuk koṇṭa nākanāṭṭaraci kumutavalli uṇmiyiṟṟāṅkaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟu piṟa allar eṉpataṉi yāṉ uṟutippaṭuttikkoḷḷum poruṭṭu avvaṭi yāḷatti eṉakkukkāṭṭumpaṭi perumāṭṭi, mutaliṟṟaṅ kaḷik kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṉṟēṉ.  eṉṟār.
muṉṉē palataramum nammāṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa ammantira mōtirattik kumutavalli taṉ viralkaḷiṉiṟuṅ kaḻaṟṟi yeṭuttu, ataṉi māṭcimikka maṉōkariṭaṅ koṭuttāḷ.
itō eṉṉuṭiyatu! eṉṟu nīlalōcaṉaṉ kūṟik koṇṭē katti māṭṭum taṉatu arippaṭṭikiyi aviḻt tāṉ; taṉṉi meyppikkum taṉatu aṭiyāḷatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭum avacarattil maṇikaḷ aḻuttiṉa taṉatu cuttiyiyum kīḻē metuvāṉa kampaḷimēl viḻumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ; piṟaku taṉaturuppāyattiṉ mārpaṇṭiyuḷḷa oru piyiliruntu ataṉi yeṭuttāṉ; pāṭṭiyilē tāṉ vantapōtu taṉatu paṇappiyiluḷḷavi taṟceyalāykkīḻē viḻuntatiliruntu, ataṉiṭattup perituṅ karuttuḷḷavaṉā yiruntāṉ.
ivvāṟu avaṉ maṉōkariṭam koṭutta nam oru mōtiramētāṉ - kumutavalli ippōtu koṭuttaṉiyō muḻutum ottiruntatu immōtiram- oru kaṉṉañ cetukkap paṭṭa orēyoru cemmaṇi kuyiṟṟip putumiyāṉa vēlip pāṭu vāyntatāyiruntatu immōtiram. itaṉiyum maṉōkarar eṭuttut toḻilāḷikkuriya uṉṉippōṭum ataṉic ciṟitunēram ārāyntu pārttār. taṉatu mōtirattiṟku muṟṟum iṇiyāṉa akkaṇiyāḻiyi kāṇṭalum kumuta vallik kuṇṭāṉa kuḻappattiyum viyappiyum itaṉip paṭippavarē eṇṇippārttuk koḷḷalām! itaṉ karuttu yātāy irukkak kūṭum? avviḷam puttaṉippaṟṟit tāṉ ētō muḻutum piḻipāṭāṉa eṇṇaṅ koṇṭu varuntu kiṉṟaṉavā? tāṉ koṇṭa aiyaṅkaḷāl avaṉukkup piḻi ceytaṉaḷā? cūḻa nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷ cāṉṟāy niṉṟu putumiyāykūṭi ivviṉiviṉit tōṟṟuvittaṉavā? etu niṉippateṉṟu avaḷukkup pulaṉākavilli - uṇmiyilē avaḷ niṉivukaḷellām kaṭitiṟ kalakkamaṭivaṉavāyiṉa.
nallatu uṅkaḷiruvariyum iṅkē varuvittaṟ poruṭṭut taṉittaṉiyē viṭutta muṭaṅkalōṭu aṉuppiya mōtiraṅkaḷē ivi. intap perumāṭṭi eṉṟu maṉōkarar kumutavalliyic cuṭṭiṉavāy avviḷam puttaṉi nōkki, nākanāṭṭaraci yāvar. eṉṟu kūṟip piṉṉar avviḷiñaṉic cuṭṭiṉavāyk kumutavalliyi nōkki, intap perumāṉ mēṟkarināṭṭu maṉṉaṟkuc cuvikārap putalvaṉāṉa nīlalōcaṉaṉ āvar. eṉa moḻintār.
aṉpum īcamum vāyntamaṉōkararē, yāṉ aracaṉāṉa nīlalōcaṉaṉi itutāṉ mutaṉmuṟiyāka etirppaṭṭēṉ allēṉ; yāṉ avarukkuc ceyta cila piḻikaḷukkāka eṉ neñcārnta maṉṉippu moḻikaḷi, eṉ mikavum uṇmiyāṉa piḻipoṟukku murikaḷik kūṟutaṟku oru noṭippoḻutun tāḻēṉ. eṉṟu urukkattāl naṭuṅkiya kuralōṭum kumutavalli kūṟiṉāḷ.
maṉōkarar avviḷiñar iruvariyum viyappuṭaṉ nōkki, nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum itaṟkumuṉ etirppaṭṭīrkaḷā? ivvaṟiyil nīṅkaḷ oruvariyoruvar terintirukka māṭṭīrkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. eṉṟu kūviccoṉṉār.
eṉṟālum nāṅkaḷ muṉṉamē etirppaṭṭirukkiṉ ṟōm, eṉṟu kūṟukiyil nākanāṭṭaraci nāṇattāl taṉmukañ civappēṟap peṟṟavaḷāy, ām, māṭcimitaṅkiya nīlalōcaṉaṉ maṉṉaṟku yāṉ tītu ceytavaḷāṉēṉ; atuvum mikappollāta aiyuṟaviṉālētāṉ. eṉṉuṭiya katiyi viriviṟ colliviṭukiṉṟēṉ. arumiyiṟ ciṟanta maṉōkararē, yāṉ iṭiyiṟṟaṅkiya cattirattiṉkaṇ eṉṉuṭiya mōtiratti - ippōtu taṅkaḷiṭam yāṉ koṭutta ivvili uyarnta mantiramōtirattik kaḷavu koṭuttu viṭṭēṉ; aṭutta nāḷil ivviḷam perumāṉit talippaṭṭu ivaroṭu tōḻami koṇṭēṉ. vaḻikkari yiluḷḷa aiyakkārar cilarkkuk kācutantu utavukiyil ivar taṟceyalāyt tamatu paṇappiyiliruntavaṟṟik kīḻē vīḻttiviṭṭār; vīḻntavaṟṟil oru mōtiram iruntatu; aḵtu eṉṉuṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu nampiviṭṭēṉ. āṉāl anta maṟi poruḷiṉ varalāṟu ippōtu vēṟuvakiyāyp pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu; anta nērattilō yāṉ orē eṇṇantāṉ koḷḷakkūṭiyamāy  iruntatu - itaṉāl, kūṭiyamāṭṭuṅ kālantāḻāmal māṭcimi taṅkiya ivvaraciḷiñari yāṉ pirintu cellavēṇṭuvatu iyalpāyiṟṟu. piṟaku oruvarkku nērnta kaḻivirakkattāl eṉṉuṭiya mōtirattit tirumpap peṟalāṉēṉ - eṉṟālum, annikaḻcciyiluṅkūṭa yāṉ māṭcimi taṅkiya nīlalōcaṉa maṉṉarippaṟṟip pollāṅ kākavē piṉṉum niṉikkalāṉēṉ. eṉṟu toṭarntu rittāḷ.
ō! puttaṉ vāḻka! eṉṟu avviḷam puttaṉ ōlamiṭṭavaṉāy, eṉ aṟivi kalakki eṉṉi mikat tuṉpuṟutti vanta anta maṟiporuḷāṉatu kaṭiciyil naṉku pulaṉāyiṟṟu! iṉi maṉṉippuk kēṭṭalip paṟṟiyō veṉṟāl, aḻakiya araci, taṅkaḷ āmpaṟ cevvāyiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum oru collē, māṭcimi mikka tāṅkaḷ eṉṉip piḻipaṭa niṉintataṉāl uṇṭāṉa eṉatu maṉattuyaratti eṉatuḷattiliruntu cuvaṭaṟattuṭittaṟkup pōtumāṉatākum. eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
kumutavalli taṉatu mukatti aḻakākac cāyntu iccoṟkaḷi ēṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ; appōtu nāṇattāl uṇṭāṉa civappu avaḷ kaṉṉaṅkaḷil maṟupaṭiyum paraviṟṟu; ivviḷam puttaṉum koḷḷikkāra nallāṉum oruvarē eṉṟu  ivaḷ piṟakuoru ciṟitum niṉiyāviṭṭālum nīlalōca ṉaṉip paṟṟiya maṟṟoruvarāy ivaḷit tikippittatu, aḵtu, avaṉ muṉṉāḷ maṉakkalakkattil kumutavalli taṉatu maṉṉippi mīṉāmpāḷukku uṟutippaṭuttik kūṟiṉa kaṭṭilaṇṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭatēyām. avviṭattilē nāṉetirppaṭṭatic cuṭṭi ippōtu avaṉ ciṟitun kuṟippiṭattakkatu paṟṟiyum avaḷ tikippuṟṟāḷ: ētō oru kāraṇam paṟṟi ataṉi maṉōkarar muṉṉiliyil avaṉ teriviyāmal maṟittuṭikkumpaṭi kaṟpikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉintavaḷāy avaḷ taṉakkuḷ iṇakkik koṇṭāḷ.
aṅṅaṉam nīṅkaḷ etirpaṭṭīrkaḷā? eṉṟu ammuti yōḷ avviruvar iṭiyilum iruntapaṭiyē avvaḻakiya iḷiñaṉiyum eḻilmikka annaṅkiyiyum iṭiviṭāta āvaluṭaṉ nōkkip piṉṉum, nīṅkaḷ avvāṟu etirp paṭṭatippaṟṟi nāṉ karutappaṭavilli. avar tāṅkaḷ kūṟiya apporuntā nikaḻcci nērāviṭiṉ, ivviḷam perumāṉip paṟṟit tavaṟāmal nāṅkaḷ nalleṇṇaṅ koḷḷutalit taṭiceyyattakkatu etuvun nērntirātu: nīllōcaṉa, tāṅkaḷum, araci kumutavalliyoṭu koṇṭa vaḻiyiṭi naṭpuc caṭutiyiṟ kulintupōṉapōtu nirampavum varuttappaṭṭīr kaḷeṉpataṉit taṅkaḷ pārviyiṉālēyē naṉku teḷiyap peṟṟēṉ.  eṉṟu urittār.
nīlalōcaṉaṉatu mukam veṭkattāl aṉaṉṟatu; eṉṟālum atil makiḻcciyum kalantu tōṉṟiyatu; āṉāl, kumutavalliyiṉiṭattuttōṉṟiya nāṇamō iṉṉum peritāyiṟṟu - aṅṅaṉamē avaḷ mukattiṟ kaṉaṉṟa nāṇac civappiṉiṭiyē aruvaruppiṟku aṭiyāḷamāṉa oru pārviyuṅ kalantutōṉṟiyatu. avaḷ niṉippāḷāṉāl - aṅṅaṉam niṉippatu iyalputāṉ - ammutiyōṉ pēciyatil, koccittaṉam eṉṟu koḷḷattakka, oru poruntāc ciṟu taṉmi iruntatu. peruntaṉmi mikka pārviyum attaṉi vayatum uṭiya avar, taṉatu kaṇṇiṭi nāṇattiṟku matippuk kuṟiccalāṉa coṟkaḷi eṇṇāmaṟ colliya kuṟṟattiṟku āḷāṉatik kuṟittu avaḷ viyappum varuttamum koḷḷalāṉāḷ.
āṉāl, apperiyōr puṉcirippuṟṟup piḻipaṭāta aṉpōṭu, avaḷ koṇṭa aruvaruppit taṭiyiṉṟi nīkkattakka vakiyāyk kūṟuvār: araci, taṅkaḷ kaṇṇimi nāṇattiṟku nāṉ piḻi ceyya māṭṭuvēṉ eṉṟu orupōtum niṉiyātīrkaḷ. nākanāṭṭaraci tamatu pēraḻakiṟkuppeṟṟa pukaḻiṉum tamatu nalloḻukkattiṟkup peṟṟa pukaḻiṟ ciṟituṅ kuṟintavar allareṉpatum - ōr aracikkuriya mēṉmiyāl tāṅkappaṭutaliṉum kaṉṉimip perumiyāl tāṅkappaṭutaliṟ ciṟituṅ kuṟintavar allareṉpatum nāṉ aṟivēṉ. curuṅkiya poḻutil vaḻi naṭantu cellutaṟkut tōḻamiyāyk koṇṭa ayalavaṉāṉa ōr iḷiñaṉiṭattil tāṅkaḷ miyal koḷḷattakkavar kaḷeṉṟu, iṉiya araci, attaṉi parumpaṭiyāṉa oru niṉivi yāṉ koḷḷattuṇivēṉeṉa nīṅkaḷ niṉikki ṟīrkaḷā? illi: eṉ coṟkaḷukku vēṟu poruḷuṇṭu. ippōtu uṇmiyic colliviṭukiṉṟēṉ - uṅkaḷiruvar kikaḷum nēcavurimiyiṟ piṇikkappaṭuvaṉavāka! ēṉeṉil, uṅkaḷ narampuk kuḻāykaḷil orē kuṭumpattiṟkuriya irattam puraṇṭōṭukiṉṟatu - nīṅkaḷ iruvarum mittuṉak kiḻami uṭiyīrkaḷ!
nīlalōcaṉaṉ kumutavalli eṉṉum avviruvar vāykaḷi ṉiṉṟum uṭaṉē viyappolikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; ciṟitu nēram mikapperiya tikippiṉ vayappaṭṭavarkaḷāy iruntamiyāl, avarkaḷ tamatu irukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu niṉṟaṉarāyiṉum, tām kitaḻuvavēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṟpikkap paṭṭapaṭiceyya niṉivaṟṟavarkaḷāy iruntaṉar.
eṉ iḷiyōrkaḷē, nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum nākanāṭṭi orukāl aracāṇṭa kōcceṅkaṇmaṉṉaṟkup pērap piḷḷikaḷ āvīrkaḷ eṉpatu uṇmiyēyām. eṉṟu maṉōkarar vaṇakkavoṭukkat tōṭuṅ kūṟiṉār.
putta camayattavaṉāṉa nāṉ, nākanāṭṭil aracupurinta civacamaya aracaṉ kālvaḻiyil vantavaṉātal evvāṟu kūṭumeṉṟeṇṇi nīlalōcaṉaṉ perituntikippuṟṟaṉaṉā yiṉum, viyappuṅkaḷippum oruṅku kiḷarappeṟṟavaṉāy, mittuṉi, eṉ kiyi ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal vēṇṭum! eṉṟu urattuc coṉṉāṉ.
kumutavalliyum tāṉ kēṭṭavikaḷip paṟṟit tikip puṟṟaṉaḷāyiṉum, pōṟṟattakka tam naṇpaṉāṉa ammutiyōḷ vāyiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum ovvoṉṟum uṇmiyākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarntavaḷāyt taṉatu veṇmi yāṉa aḻakiya kiyi nīlalōcaṉaṉ kiyil vittāḷ.
uṭaṉē, maṉōkarar tamatu irukkiyiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu, avviḷiñar iruvariṉ ceṉṉimēṟ ṟamkikaḷi nīṭṭik, kaṭavuḷ uṅkaḷiruvarkkum aruḷ vaḻaṅkuvārāka! maṟṟo ruvar poruṭṭāka yāṉ uṅkaḷukku virivil koṭuppatāy irukkum putumiyāṉa poruṭkaḷukku nīṅkaḷ takutiyuṭiyavar kaḷākumpaṭi nām vaḻipaṭum mutaṟperun kaṭavuḷ aruḷ purivārāka! ō, nīlalōcaṉa, civa camayattiṉ aruḷoḷi yāṉatu numatuḷḷattiṉ ūṭuruvic ceṉṟu viḷaṅkuvatāka? eṉṟu vāḻtturi kūṟiṉār.
iccoṟkaḷic colliya vakiyilum ōciyilum veḷittōṟṟamum vaṇakkavoṭukkamum vāyntatu. ētō iruntamiyāl, apputta iḷiñaṉum acciva naṅkiyumavar kālaṭikaḷil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ; avaruṭiya kikaḷi eṭuttu muttam vittārkaḷ. āṉāl, avviruvarum oṉṟumēpēcavilli; avarkaḷ tammiṭat tuṇṭāṉa mikunta maṉavurukkattāl pēcakkūṭātavarāy iruntaṉar. ākavē camayam māṟutalippaṟṟi ammutiyōṉ colliya coṟkaḷ evvakiyāṉa eṇṇatti nīlalōcaṉa ṉiṭattil eḻuppiṉaveṉpati aṅṅaṉaṅkōriya ammuti yōṉē aḵtu aṅṅaṉamirukkalāmeṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt tuṇintaṟiyak kūṭavilli.
eṉ aṉpiṟkiṉiya iḷiya nēcarkaḷē, eḻuntiruṅkaḷ! eḻuntiruṅkaḷ! uṅkaḷ irukkikaḷil iruṅkaḷ - yāṉ collappōkiṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷi uṟṟukkēḷuṅkaḷ. eṉṟu maṉōkarar kūṟiṉār.
aṅṅaṉamē kumutavalliyum nīlalōcaṉaṉum eḻuntu, vaṇakkattakka avviyāpāriyiṉ valatu pakkattilum iṭatu pakkattilum muṟiyē uṭkārntaṉar. ivviruvarum avartam mukatti mikunta aiyuṟavōṭum uṟṟuppārttaṉar; ēṉeṉ ṟāl, mutaṉmiyāṉa ceytikaḷit tām ippōtu aṟiyap pōkuntaṟuvāyil iruppatāka avarkaḷ uṇarntārkaḷ.
maṉōkarar avvaraci pakkamāyttirumpi, māṭcimi niṟinta taṅkaḷi nōkki māttiram yāṉ kūṟuvatāyiruntāl, taṅkaḷ pāṭṭaṉār kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarip paṟṟit tāṅkaḷ muṉṉamē aṟintu vittirukkum palaciṟuvaralāṟu kaḷi yāṉ kūṟātu viṭṭuc cellalām. āṉāl, perumāṉ nīlalōcaṉa, tāṅkaḷ avvaralāṟukaḷic ciṟitum uṇartaṟku iṭamillā tiruttaliṉāl, yāṉ avaṟṟiṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷi eṭuttuc collavēṇṭuvatu akattiyamāy irukkiṉṟatu. kōc ceṅkaṇ maṉṉar nākanāṭṭit taṉiyāṉa āṭci purintava reṉṟu tāṅkaḷ aṟital vēṇṭum; avar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivu muṟiyālum, tām piṟaḻātu naṭāttiya ceṅkōṉmiyālum, tāmākavē āṅkāṅku nāṭṭiya aṟac cālikaḷālum tam kuṭimakkaḷāṟ peritum aṉpu pārāṭṭippōṟṟappaṭṭu vantār. nākappūriluḷḷa tamatu araṇmaṉiyil avar vaḻakka māyt taṅkiyiruntaṉar - aḻakiṟ ciṟanta kumutavalli, tāṅkaḷ avvaraṇmaṉiyiliruntu tāṉ ciṟitu kālattiṟku muṉ payaṇam puṟappaṭṭu nīlakiri nakarattiṟku vantu cērntīrkaḷ. nīṅkaḷ iruvarum piṟappataṟkumuṉ - atāvatu irupattintu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ irukkalām- vaṇaṅkattakka muṉivarar oruvar orunāḷ avvaraṇmaṉi vāyilil vantu niṉṟu, tām kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉaṉōṭu pēca virumpuvatākat terivittaṉar. taṉṉik kāṇa varupavarkaḷellārkkum eppōtum eḷiyarāyirukkum ammaṉṉar tuyaramāṉa attuṟaviyi evvaḷavu viruppattōṭu varavēṟpāreṉpati nām collalvēṇṭā; mēlum uṅkaḷ pāṭṭaṉār civa camaya uṇmiyil mikavum maṉam aḻunti niṉṟār; tamatu celvākkup paraviya iṭaṅkaḷilellām avar ataṉit tammāṟ kūṭiya maṭṭum vaḷarttu vantār. ammuṉivarar avar muṉṉiliyiṟ ceṉṟu, tāmiruvarum taṉittirukkumpaṭi kēṭṭukkoṇṭār. ammaṉṉarum ataṟkicintu tampari vāraṅkaḷi appuṟappaṭuttiviṭṭār; avaṉ piṉ attūya muṉivarar avvaracarceviyiṟ paṭumpaṭi mikavum putumiyāṉa ceytikaḷik kūṟattuvaṅkiṉār. piṉvarukiṟapaṭi avvaracari nōkkic cila pala kūṟuvārāṉār: eṉ aracē, putumimikka oru maṟivāṉa ceytikku vippiṭamāy uḷḷavar in nērattil ivvulakil iraṇṭu peyarē irukkiṉṟaṉar. cila kiḻami kaḷukku muṉ ataṉi yaṟintavar mūṉṟu peyar iruntaṉar; āṉāl, oruvar iṟantu pōyiṉar--ippōtu immaṟi viṉit tam neñcattē pūṭṭi vittiruppavar iruvarē yirukkiṉṟaṉar. toṉṟu toṭṭu varum oru vaḻakkat tiṉ tūya kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉpaṭi, ippōtu am maṟiporuḷ mūṉṟām oruvarkku aṟivikkappaṭa vēṇṭuvatāy irukkiṉṟatu; ēṉeṉil, am maṟiporuḷ muṟṟumē teriyāmal aḻintu pōtaṟku iṭam illātapaṭi iyaṉṟa maṭṭum ataṉip pātukāttuk koḷḷum poruṭṭāka ataṉit terinta varkaḷ eppōtumē mūṉṟu peyar irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu akattiyamākak karutappaṭṭatu. yāṉ ippōtu kuṟippiṭṭuc coṉṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkum, am maṟiporuḷiṉ iyaṟkiyum pōṟṟattakka ammaṟi viṉip putiya oruvarkku aṟivikka vēṇṭuvatu akattiyamām poḻutu ataṟku mikavuntakkavar oruvariyē terinteṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉan takkavarāyuḷḷavar ivvaḷavu vayacuṭiyavarāyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṉum vari yaṟiyilli: āṉāṟ camayak koḷkiyip paṟṟiyō veṉṟāl appaṭi yilli--ēṉeṉṟāl, ciya camayattit taḻuviṉō rallāmaṟ piṟar ipperiya putiporuḷi aṟiyum makiḻcciyip peṟalākātu. māṭcimi taṅkiya taṅkaṭku ippōtu tāṉ yāṉ terivitta vaṇṇam, immaṟiviṉi aṟintavaril oruvar itaṉiṉuñ ciṟanta maṟumi yulakattiṟkuc cila nāḷukku muṉ ēkiviṭṭamiyāl, avarirunta iṭattiṟku nirampat takutiyāṉa maṟṟoruvarit terinteṭukkuṅ kaṭami eṉṉip poṟuppa tāyiṟṟu. aṟivālum nītiyālum aracar ellāriṉuñ ciṟantavarum, aṉpoḻukkat tāṟ civa camayattār ellāriṉum mēṟpaṭṭavarumāṉa kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarit tavira vēṟu ciṟantavari yāṉ eṅṅaṉan terin teṭukkavallēṉ? eṉṟu ivvāṟu mēṟkaṇavāy mali naṭuviliruntu vanta attūya muṉivarar uṅkaḷ pāṭṭaṉārkkuc colliṉār.
pōṟṟattakka maṉōkarar tuvaṅkiya katiyi mikavum maṉaṅkavintu kēṭṭuk koṇṭu vanta araci kumutavalliyum araciḷiñaṉ nīlalōcaṉaṉum piṉṉum ataṉ ṟoṭarcci yāy avar collap pōvataṉik kēṭka evvaḷavu āval koṇṭār eṉpati avarkaḷiṉ pārvikaḷē pulap paṭuttiṉa.
vaṇaṅkattakka avviyāpāri piṉṉum ataṉit toṭarnturippārāṉār: mikavum pōṟṟattakkateṉṟuñ ciṟantateṉṟum collappaṭṭa maṟiporuḷi vittirup pavarkaḷil oruvarākat tammi terinteṭuttataṉāl tamakku nayampurinta attūya muṉivararukku nuṅkaḷ pāṭṭaṉārāṉa akkōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉar tamatu naṉṟiyaṟitalit terivittār; piṉvarumāṟu ammuṉivarar ammāṭciyi niṟinta maṉṉarkku urippārāṉār:-- emperumāṉē, itu kālakālamāka uṇmiyōṭuṅ koṭukkappaṭṭu varukiṟa oru putiporuḷākum; mikavun tāḻnta nilimiyiluḷḷa tuṟavi kaḷiṉiṭattum, mikavum valiya nilimiyiluḷḷa aracar kaḷiṭattum immaṟiporuḷ vikkappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉit terintu koḷva tāṉatu ulakattil nirampavum viyakkappaṭukiṉṟa payaṉkaḷit tām aṉupavikkat takkavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟavarkaḷukku oru tiṟavukōlā yirukkum. taṉataṟivukkuḷ akappaṭumpaṭi iṅṅaṉaṅkoṇṭu varappaṭṭa viyappāṉa poruḷkaḷi miku kaḷippōṭum pēriṉpa viyappōṭum uṟṟuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭu vanta kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarkku ammuṉivar ammaṟi poruḷi ataṉ piṉ vaḷakki veḷiyiṭṭār. anta maṟiporuḷ yātāyiruntatu--allatu yātāyirukkiṉṟatu--eṉpati, eṉ aṉpuḷḷa iḷiya naṇparkaḷē, ippōtu yāṉ colluṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, uṅkaḷukku ivvamayattil collak kūṭātavaṉāyiruk kiṉṟēṉ. āṉālum, aḻakiṟ ciṟanta kumutavalli taṅkaḷukku māttiram atu curukkil terivikkap paṭalām; nīlalōcaṉarē, taṅkaḷukku yāṉ atē vakiyāṉa nampikkiyi eppōtu kāṭṭap peṟuvēṉō veṉṟāl atu taṅkaḷittāṉ poṟuttirukkiṉṟatu.
maṟupaṭiyum maṉōkarar ciṟitunēram pēcāmaliruntār; apperiya maṟiporuḷit tāṉum aṅṅaṉamē terintu koḷḷat takutiyuṭiyavaṉāyk karutappaṭuvatellām, tāṉ puttacamayak koḷkiyi aṟavē viṭṭoḻippataṉiyē poṟuttirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu tāṉ itu variyiṟ kēṭṭu vantavaṟṟāl iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟateṉa nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉakkuḷ niṉippāṉāṉāṉ. itip paṟṟi avaṉ maṉa nili evvāṟintateṉpatu piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāṟ pulaṉākum.
maṉōkarar piṉṉun toṭarnturippārāṉār: yāṉ ippōtu kuṟippiṭṭa maṟiporuḷic ciṟitum vaḻuvāmal muṟṟun terintu koḷḷum poruṭṭuk kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉar attuṟaviyōṭuṅ kūṭap payaṇam puṟappaṭa vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiṟṟu. appayaṇañ ceṉṟu tirumpu vataṟkuc cila nāṭkaḷ mēṟ cellātu; ākavē, tām nāka nāṭṭiṉ tali nakari viṭṭu veḷiyē ceṉṟiruppatu tammic cūḻntirukkum naṉṟiyaṟivuḷḷa ēvaṟkārar cilark kaṉṟip piṟarkkun teriyā tirukkum vaṇṇam avvaracar peruntaki ataṉi maṟi vākavē muṭikkak kūṭiyavarāṉār. eṉṟālum, avar attūya tuṟaviyit tavirap piṟare variyum uṭaṉ kūṭṭic cella villi; ammuṉivarar eṭuttuc coṉṉa uṟuti moḻiyiṉ tiṟattil avar ēmāṟṟam aṭiyavilli. avviyattaku maṟiporuṭkaḷañciyamāyum, ataṉiyaṟinta aṟivāl tām aṉupavikkak kiṭitta niliyāṉa payaṉkaḷil cilavaṟṟip peṟṟavarāyum avar nākappūrukkut tirumpi vantār. iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻinta piṟaku appuṉitamuṉivarar nampikki vāynta oru tūtuvaṉik kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉar pāl aṉuppit tām iṟakkun taṟuvāyil iruppatākat terivittār. avvaracar peruntakiyum annallōr tamatu yiri iṟivaṉṟiruvaṭikkuc celuttuntaṟuvāyil irunta iṭattiṟku oru noṭip poḻutuntāḻāmaṟ ceṉṟār; avvaracarum pirintu pōkum ammuṉivarariṉ vāḻtturikaḷip peṟṟuk koṇṭu piṉṉar avari aṭakkañ ceyya vēṇṭum caṭaṅkukaḷi muṭittār. ataṉ piṟaku, ammaṟi poruḷi vittirukkattakka maṟṟoruvarit tēṭippiṭikka vēṇṭuvatu ammaṉṉar peruntakiyiṉ mēlatāyiṟṟu; tām cevviyāy aṟinta civacamaya aṉparkaḷi yellām tamatuḷḷattil aḷaviṭṭup pārtta piṉṉar, avar muṭivāka eṉṉiyē terinteṭukka lāṉār. M« v‹Åisa ner®fns., taṅkaḷ ciṟanta pāṭṭa ṉāriṉ neruṅkiya naṭpip peṟṟu iṉpuṟum celvatti ṉiyum perumiyiṉiyum eytiṉēṉ. eṉatuṇmic civapaktikku aṭiyāḷamāka yāṉ appōtu kāṭṭiya oppaṉikaḷ ippōteṭuttuc colvatu eṉakkuttakātu: ākiyāl, iṟantu pōṉa ammuṉivararuṭiya nilimiyi yāṉ aṭiyalāṉēṉ eṉṟu colvatu pōtum. iṅṅaṉamāka avvuyarnta maṟi poruḷi teritaṟku aruḷ peṟṟa mūvaril yāṉum oruvaṉāṉēṉ.
piṉṉuñ ciṟitu nēram pēcāmalirunta piṟaku maṉōkarar maṟupaṭiyum piṉvarumāṟu pēcappukuntār-
kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarkku iraṇṭu putalvarkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiruvarum maṇañceyyappaṭṭavarkaḷē, avarkku viruppamiruntāl tamputalvarkaḷil oruvarkkāyiṉum tammarumakkaḷil oruvarkkāyiṉum ammaṟiporuḷi avarterivittirukkalām; ēṉeṉil, nāṉ muṉṉamē terivittapaṭi vayatippaṟṟiya variyaṟiyilli - āṇ peṇ eṉṉum pāl variyaṟiyum illi yeṉpataṉi nīṅkaḷ muṉṉamē terintirukkiṟīrkaḷ. avartam putalvarkaḷ kaṭami yil vaḻukāmal kīḻppaṭitaluḷḷavarkaḷāy iruntālum, apputalvarkaḷiṉ iḷiya maṉivimār naṟkuṇamuṭiyarāyum maṉattukkiṉiyarāyum iruntālum, avarkaḷellārum ulakappaṟṟu mikkavarāyum tamatu uyarnta niliyiṉ āravārattilum veḷippakaṭṭilum araciyalilum niṉivu aḻuntiṉavarāyum iruntamiyāl, avar tamatu maṉamāra avarkaḷuḷ evariyumterinteṭuttu appēriṉpa aṉupavat tiṟku uriyarāmpaṭi ceyya iyalātavarāṉār. atuvallā malum, attakiyatoru maṟiporuḷit taṉ maṉi yāḷukkut teriyāmal kaṇavaṉ tāṉē vittiruppatu ovvāta toṉṟām-- iṉikkaṇavaṉi vilakki avaṉ maṉivikku orumaṟi poruḷip pulappaṭuttuvatum naṉmuṟikkum nallaṟivukkum piṉṉum iciyātatoṉṟām. āka ellā vaṟṟiyum eṇṇippārkkiyil kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉar eṉṉiyē terinteṭukkalāṉār. āṉāl, ippōtu yāṉ ikkatiyiṉ maṟṟoru pakutiyippaṟṟip pēcap pōkiṉṟēṉ. kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarkku maṇañceyyappaṭṭa putalvar iruvar iruntaṉareṉṟu yāṉ muṉṉamē colli yiruk kiṉṟēṉ. nīlalōcaṉa, ammaṉṉariṉ iḷiya putalvarkkum avartam aḻakiya maṉiviyārkkum tāṅkaḷ irupat tōrāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ piḷḷiyāyp piṟantīrkaḷ!
eṉ peṟṟōrkaḷ? eṉṟu nīlalōcaṉaṉ viyantu viṉavit tirumpavum varuttam mikka aiyuṟaviṉāl maṉaṅkalaṅki, naṭuṅkiya melliyakuralōṭu avarkaḷ iṉṉum uyirōṭiruk kiṟārkaḷā? ō! illi! illi! inta nampikki vikka yāṉ tuṇiyalākātu - appaṭiyiruntāl ittaṉi kālam yāṉ avarkaḷāl, ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭāmal kiviṭappaṭṭirunti rēṉ! eṉṟu colliṉāṉ.
eṉ ariya iḷiya nēcarē, tāṅkaḷ mikavum tiṟamāyk karutukiṟapaṭiyē tāṉ. eṉṟu maṉōkarar urukkattōṭu kūṟiṉar; kumutavalliyiṉ irakkamāṉa pārviyum taṉ mittuṉaṉiṉ peṟṟōrkaḷ uyirōṭilli yeṉṟu avaḷum avaṉukkut terivippaṉapōṟ kuṟi kāṭṭiṉaḷ.
accō, eṉ ariya tāy tantiyarē! eṉṟu nīlalōcaṉaṉ muṇumuṇutturikkiyil kaṇṇīr avaṉ kaṉṉaṅkaḷil vaṭintatu. vaṇaṅkattakka maṉōkararē, avarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam iṟantārkaḷeṉpati eṉakkuc colluṅkaḷ, colluṅkaḷ.
taṅkaḷ irakkattakka aṉṉiyar tāṅkaḷ piṟanta ciṟitunērattiṟkellām orukāyccalāṟ koṇṭu pōkap paṭṭār. eṉṟu ammaliya viyāpāri maṟumoḻintār.  taṅkaḷ tantiyār muṭivippaṟṟi yāṉ ippōtu pēcappōkiṟēṉ. eṉ iḷiyanaṇparē, uṅkaḷ āttiratti aṭakkik koṇṭu, inta muṭivu tuyaramāṉa toṉ ṟeṉpatik kēṭka āyattamāy iruṅkaḷ. maṉṉariṉ mūtta makaḷukkut tirumaṇamākic cila āṇṭukaḷ variyil piḷḷi piṟantilatu.
iraṅkattakka eṉ tantiyār! eṉṟu kumutavalli muṇumuṇuttāḷ.
ippōtu eṉatu katiyil tuyaramāṉa pakutiyippaṟṟic collap pōkiṟēṉ, eṉṟurittu maṉōkarar piṉṉuṅ kūṟuvār: nīlalōcaṉarē, nīṅkaḷ muṟṟunterintukoḷḷum poruṭṭu, mutalil yāṉ colliyapaṭi, ikkuṭumpa nikaḻcci kaḷiyum varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷiyum neṭukac colla vēṇṭuvatu eṉakku akattiyamāy irukkiṉṟatu. nīṅkaḷ piṟantu cila tiṅkaḷ tām ceṉṟaṉa - uṅkaḷic cūḻanaṭantavaṟṟi uṇarāta ciṟumakavāy .iruntīrkaḷ - appōtu ariṭamaṉṉaṉ nākanāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttu vantāṉ. āṇṭil mutirnta kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarum tammuṭiya paṭikaḷiyellām oruṅku tiraṭṭiṉār; avarē tam paṭikaḷukkut talivarāy niṉṟu, tammarumakaṉum iḷiya putalvarun tammōṭu kūṭa varap pakiyaracaṉuṭaṉ pōr puriyac ceṉṟār. aiyō! añcāta pērāṇmiyiruntālum, atu perun tokiyāṉa paṭi maṟavarkkumuṉ yātu ceyyum! kōcceṅkaṇṇār paṭi yāṉatutōlviyaṭintu nāka nāṭṭiṉ talinakarāṉa nākap pūriṉuḷ alaṅkōlamāyp piṉṉiṭintu ōṭiṟṟu. annakaratti lirunta kuṭimakkaḷō tammaracaṉiṭattil mikunta aṉpuṭiyarāy iruntamiyāl, muṟṟuki ceyyum pakivari etirttu niṟṟaṟku vēṇṭum ēṟpāṭukaḷi mikka ūkkattōṭum ceytārkaḷ. ariṭa maṉṉaṉ paṭittalivaṉō kōṭṭi matiṟpuṟattē vantu cērntu annakarattāri aḻittuk kōṭṭiyit tamakku oppaṭittuviṭumpaṭi coṉṉāṉ. nakarattārō ataṉik kaṭumiyāka maṟuttukkūṟiṉārkaḷ. muṟṟuki ceytavarkaḷi mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷvariyil annakarattār etirttu niṉṟārkaḷ; āṉāl, atu muṟṟiluñ cūḻappaṭṭuc cuṟṟiyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ pōkkuvaravukku iṭamillātapaṭi vaḻiyaṭi paṭṭami yiṉālē, atu pañcattiṉ koṭumikaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭu meṉṉum accam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pakivarpaṭiyiṉ talivaṉ ōr aṟivippu viṭṭu, avvaṟippiṟkaṇṭa mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷuḷ nākappūrkkōṭṭiyi oppaṭiyāviṭṭāl iṅṅaṉam piṭivātamāy etirttu niṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam aracaṉum avaṉ putalvar iruvarumē eṉṟu karutappaṭuvār kaḷeṉavum, ataṉāl ataṉip piṭittapiṟaku ammūvariyum kōṭṭik kottaḷaṅkaḷiṉ mēl irakkamiṉṟik kaḻuvēṟṟuvēṉ eṉavum terivittāṉ. caṇṭiyippaṟṟip pēcitteḷiyum poruṭṭu ōr avi kūṭṭappaṭṭatu; tamatu talinakar etiriyiṉ kippaṭuvatu tiṇṇameṉṟuṇarnta ammutiya aracar tām kōṭṭiyip pakiyaracaṉukku oppaṭittuviṭuṅ karuttuṭiya rātali avvakiyil terivittār. āṉāl, avar marumakaṉum putalvarum atikārikaḷum etirttē niṟkumpaṭi avari vēṇṭik koṇṭārkaḷ; ēṉeṉṟāl, cāḷuvamaṉṉaṉ paṭi nākanāṭṭaracarkku utaviyāy varuvatāka ōr uṟutimoḻi iruntatu. kuṭimakkaḷō araṇmaṉiyic cūḻntukoṇṭu, taṅkaḷ mutiya aracarkku aṅṅaṉam collappaṭṭa yōca ṉiyik kiḷarcci mikka coṟkaḷāl tāṅkip pēciṉārkaḷ. avarum kuṭimakkaḷ karuttukku iṇaṅka vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu; ēṉeṉṟāl, pakippaṭittalivaṉ miraṭṭutalukkun tāṉ uṭaṉpaṭṭāl taṉṉuyirkkum taṉ makkaḷuyirkkumē tām kavalippaṭuvatāka niṉikkakkūṭum eṉṟu avar añciṉār. ākavē, nākappūr piṉṉum nīṇṭa nāḷ pātukākkap paṭuvatāyiṟṟu; kōṭṭikkuḷḷiruntapaṭi caṭutiyiṟ pāyntu tākkiṉamiyāl, ariṭamaṉṉaṉ paṭiyil orupakuti muṟṟilum muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭatu; appaṭit talivaṉō ataṉāṟ peruñ cīṟṟam aṭintāṉ; ātalāl, pīraṅkiveṭivittu aṭittuk koṇṭē pōyt taṉpaṭi vīrarellārum tākkal vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. kōṭṭiyikkātta paṭimakkaḷ uyirukkut tuṇintu caṇṭi ceytārkaḷ; eṉṟālum, aḷavukku miñciṉa tokiyāyuḷḷa pakivar paṭikaḷiṉ muṉṉē ettakiya āṇmiyum pōtumāṉatu aṉṟu. curuṅkaccolluṅkāl, nākappūr piṭipaṭṭuviṭṭatu; aracakuṭumpattāric ciṟip paṭuttum poruṭṭup pakivar araṇmaṉiyiṟ ceṉṟu moyttuk koṇṭārkaḷ. aracakuṭumpattār tappip pōtaṟkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ cūḻntu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa! āyiṉum, uṅkaḷ tantiyārum, nīlalōca ṉarē, nīṅkaḷum -- appōtu avarkiyil nīṅkaḷ oru ciṟu makavāy iruntīrkaḷ -- nākappūr kōṭṭiyit tāṇṭit tappippōkalāṉīrkaḷ. mutiya aracarum avartam mūttamakaḷum, ammakaḷiṉ kaṇavarum palamukamāyt tappiyōṭukiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuc ciṟi ceyyappaṭṭārkaḷ. nalviṉiyaṟṟa kuṭimakkaḷō terukkaḷ neṭuka niṉṟu koṇṭu, veṟṟic cerukkōṭuṅ kutirimēṟ ceṉṟa pakivar paṭittalivaṉit tāḻmiyōṭum vaṇaṅki, avvaraca kuṭumpattāri viṭṭu viṭumpaṭi irantu kēṭṭārkaḷ. mutalil appaṭittalivaṉ maṉam iraṅka villi: āṉāl, kaṭiciyākac ciṟitaḷavu maṉam iraṅkiṉāṉ. kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉar uṭaṉē nākappūriviṭṭup pōyviṭutal vēṇṭu meṉṟum, avar inta ulakavāḻvi muṟṟuntuṟantu celvatākap puṉitamum vaṇakkavoṭukkamum mikunta cūḷ urikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟupaṭiyum avar nākanāṭṭu makkaḷiṭi varutalum, nākanāṭṭu aluvalkaḷil taliyiṭutalum ākāveṉṟum, -- curuṅkaccollumiṭattu, iṉi avar uyirtuṟantavar pōlāka vēṇṭumē yallāmal, nērākavēṉum piṟarvāyilākavēṉum vēṟevvaḻiyālum eppōtum avar tamkuṭumpattavarōṭu maṟupaṭiyum ciṟitun toṭarpuvittal kūṭāteṉṟum kūṟi apporuttaṉikku icivatāyiruntāl avari uyirōṭu viṭṭuviṭuvatākap pukaṉṟāṉ. āṇmimikka ammutiya aracar māṉakkēṭāṉa apporuttaṉikaḷi ericcaluṭaṉ ikaḻntu, ataṉāl nām kaḻuvēṟavun tuṇintiruppār; āṉāl, tam mūttamakaḷ marumakaṉ uyirum kūṭa ataṉāl iṭiñcalukku uḷḷāvati eṇṇiṉār. ākavē, apporuttaṉikku icintu veḷippuṟappaṭṭār. antap paṭittalivaṉ avartam iḷiya makaṉi -- nīlalōcaṉarē uṅkaḷ tantiyip, piṭittut tarupavarkaḷukkup paricaḷippatākat terivittāṉ; āṉāl, nāṭkaḻintatēyallāmal, uṅkaḷ tantiyārippaṟṟi ētum ceytivaravilli. nākappūrip piṭittuk koṇṭu, nāka nāṭṭārkkuḷḷa paṟṟutalit tolittuviṭalāmeṉṟu appakivar eṇṇiṉārkaḷ; āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṇṇam īṭēṟavilli. eṅkēpārttālum kalakam talikāṭṭuva tāyiṟṟu -- kaṭiciyāka antak kāṭṭiluḷḷavarkaḷit taṇivuceyyum poruṭṭu appakivar tam aracāṭciyil ōr oḻuṅku ceyvārāyiṉar. nākanāṭṭil uḷḷa mēlōri yellām aḻittu orucapi kūṭṭap paṭṭatu; iṉimēl antanāṭu taṉakkuriya ōr aracaṉālēyē āḷappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, mutaṉmiyāṉa iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nakaraṅkaḷ māttiram avarkaḷ uṟutipmoḻippaṭi ariṭamaṉṉaṟ kuriyavarkaḷ ceyyum vāṇikattikkākkum poruṭṭu ammaṉṉavaṉ paṭimaṟavarāṟ kāvalceyyappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum atil tīrmāṉañceytārkaḷ. itaṟkēṟpa ōr uṭaṉpaṭikkiyum ceyyappaṭṭatu; kōc ceṅkaṇ maṉṉariṉ marumakaṉār -- eḻil mikka kumutavalli taṅkaḷ tantiyār -- tam maṉiviyarōṭum ituvariyil nākappūri lēyē oruvāṟu mēṉmiyākavē ciṟipiṭittu vikkap paṭṭiruntavar aracāṭcikku uriyavarākkappaṭṭār. an nikaḻccikku iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ piṟpaṭṭu -- atāvatu patiṉēḻu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉṉē, kumutavalli, nīṅkaḷ piṟantīrkaḷ; iṉṉum, nīlalōcaṉar terintu koḷḷum poruṭṭut, taṅkaḷ peṟṟōr iruvarum ēṟakkuṟiya orē vakiyāṉa nōyāl iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ iṟantu pōyiṉa reṉṟum, appōtu nākanāṭṭukkuṭimakkaḷum ariṭa maṉṉaṉ araciyaṟṟalivarum taṅkaḷiyē annākanāṭṭukkut taṉi araciyāka ēṟṟu amikkalāṉārkaḷeṉṟum kūṭṭic collukiṉṟēṉ.
iṉi, eṉ tantiyariṉ muṭivu eppaṭiyāyiṟṟu? eṉṟu nīlalōcaṉaṉ taṉṉit tōṟṟuvitta tantiyiṉ muṭivi yaṟiya, niṟintapiṉ yiḷami āttirattōṭum viṉāyiṉāṉ.
avar nākappūriṉiṉṟum maṟintu ceṉṟa nīṇṭa kālattiṟkuppiṉ, avarum avartam makavum mēṟkaṇavāy malikaḷiṉiṭiyē cettukkiṭakkak kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu eppaṭiyō oru pēccu ūreṅkum paravalāyiṟṟu; ākavē, avarkaḷ appaṭittāṉ aḻintupōṉārkaḷeṉṟa nampikki eṅkum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. āṉāl, uṇmi aṅṅaṉamaṉṟu eṉpati, ippōtu nīṅkaḷ viriviṟṟerintukoḷvīrkaḷ. āyiṉum, mutaṉmutal yāṉ mutiyōrāṉa kōcceṅkaṇ maṉṉarippaṟṟip pēcalvēṇṭum. appakittalivaṉiṭam tām ceytu koṇṭa poruttaṉiyiṉ muṟippaṭi avar nākappūriviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭatum, mēṟ kaṇavāy malit toṭariṉ kaṇ uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil uḷḷuruvic ceṉṟār; ap puṉitamātavaṉ tamakkut terivittirunta ammaṟiporuḷi ippōtavar tamakkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷvārāṉār; ammaṟipporuḷit tām teriyalāṉatu paṟṟi avar tiruvaruḷukku vāḻtturi moḻintār. ēṉeṉṟāl; ipperiya maṇṇulakiṉ kuḻappaṅkaḷ nērātatum, pōriṭum iriccal nuḻiyātatum, cūṟāvaḷiyāṉatu vāṉulakattil tōṉṟātatupōlat taṉṉaya viruppaṅkaḷ muṭṭātatum āṉa ōr iṭattil pattiramākavum iṉitākavum otuṅkiyiruttaṟku ēṟṟa vaḻiyi avarukku atu tantatu. eṉṟa maṉōkarar maṟu moḻi pukaṉṟār.
iṅkē maṉōkarar ciṟitunēram cummā iruntār: kumutavalli taṉ uḷḷattiṟ palvēṟuvakippaṭṭut tōṉṟiya putumiyāṉa uṇarvukaḷōṭu eṇṇamiṭṭuk koṇṭirun tāḷ; nīlalōcaṉaṉatu mukamō taṉ tantiyiṉ muṭivit terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṉum āttirattōṭum āvaluṟṟa kuṟippippulappaṭakkāṭṭiyatu.

attiyāyam - 15
ceytiviḷakkattiṉ muṭivu
